# P42 — the shell's sound (crates/openmx/src/audio.rs plays it; nothing here is ported to game-sim).
# The engine note: a state machine of whole samples chained one after the other, read off the bike
# archive's vehicle_*.aud. Journal: research/journal/*-p42-sound-and-music.
engine_sound_load boot 0x0030fb00 reads the vehicle .aud: the [Idle]..[Panic] lists, LowTorqueSpeed,
#                                 the distances
engine_sound_list boot 0x0030fd10 one list: NumSounds, SoundN, SoundNLooping, the section's Looping
engine_sound_decide boot 0x0030ffc0 the frame's next note from the hands (gas, clutch, grounded, speed)
engine_sound_chain boot 0x00311160 queues the note's next sample after the one playing; picks through
#                                  sound_pick (draws rand_raw) and draws rand again for a pitch variance
# The note states' starters, by the state each sets (+0x2d8) and the list it plays (P49 corrected
# P42's reading of four of them). Lists: +0x204 Idle, +0x21c LowAccel, +0x234 HighAccel, +0x24c Decel,
# +0x264 Power, +0x27c Sputter, +0x294 Panic, +0x2ac Clutch (0x18 each; +0x8 the array).
engine_note_idle boot 0x00310370 idle (1): Idle[0], queued under the one sounding, next 1
engine_note_accel boot 0x003104f0 accel (2/3): from 8/9/10 HighAccel cut in; else LowAccel at speed <=
#                                LowTorqueSpeed, HighAccel above, cut in from 1/6 only; next 4
engine_note_power boot 0x00310ad0 power (4): Power picked, queued, next 5
engine_note_sputter boot 0x00310c50 sputter (5): Sputter picked, queued, next 4
engine_note_decel boot 0x00310980 decel (6): Decel[0] cut in, next 1
engine_note_rev boot 0x00310dd0 (state) 7 Clutch picked cut in, next 8; 9 with gas the limiter,
#                               next 10; 9 without and 10 the limiter off, state 10, next 1; else
#                               the free rev Panic[0] cut in, state 8, next 9
# P49 — the rest of the engine note.
engine_sound_frame boot 0x0030fcb0 (a0 the note, f12 dt) unless muted (+0x2fc): decide, and when it
#                                  started nothing, chain; then the emitter's own update
engine_sound_limiter boot 0x003110d0 (note, on) +0x2f8: pauses Panic[0] (sound vt +0x24) and plays or
#                                    stops Panic[1] over it
engine_sound_vtable boot 0x00458b50 the note's class: +0x7c queue (cut stops it first), +0x9c stop
engine_sound_queue boot 0x001b6b10 (emitter, sound, cut) the emitter's vt +0x7c: cut stops the queue
#                                  (vt +0x9c), then the queue is the one sound
sound_3d boot 0x00104480 (f12 min, f13 max, a0 listener, a1 sound, a2 at, a3 velocity) the positional
#                        sound's volume, angle and Doppler off the listener; 0 (and no play) at
#                        MaxAttenuationDistance and beyond
sound_3d_mix boot 0x00103020 100% within Min, 100 - (int)((d-min)/(max-min)*100) to Max; Doppler is
#                            the closing speed along the line, level components only
sound_doppler boot 0x00166bc0 the sound's vt +0x68: pitch x (1 + DopplerFactor * (int)(v*0.68182)/1125),
#                             a shift under 0.01 none
sound_class_vtable boot 0x0044ed00 one sound: +0x1c start, +0x20 playing, +0x24 pause, +0x28 stop,
#                                  +0x40 set pitch, +0x48 its pitch (+0x54)
voice_position boot 0x0019c390 (spu, voice) seconds into a voice; -112 when it is not sounding
sound_start boot 0x00166380 the sound's vt +0x1c: start it
sound_playing boot 0x001666e0 the sound's vt +0x20: still sounding (a paused one is)
sound_pause boot 0x00166580 the sound's vt +0x24: (sound, on) pause or resume its voice
sound_stop boot 0x00166640 the sound's vt +0x28: stop it
sound_set_pitch boot 0x00166970 the sound's vt +0x40: (sound, pitch) in pitch units, 4096 = 48 kHz
sound_pitch boot 0x00166e60 the sound's vt +0x48: its own pitch
emitter_play boot 0x001b6e70 (emitter, sound, 1, 0) the limiter's Panic[1] started on the emitter
emitter_stop boot 0x001b6150 (emitter, sound) the limiter's Panic[1] stopped
emitter_update boot 0x001b6f30 (emitter, f12 dt) the emitter's own frame: positions its sounds
engine_sound_hands boot 0x00280fb0 points the note's +0x200 table at the rider O's snapshot: [0]
#                                  grounded O+0x93c, [1] gas O+0x888 (the rate-limited throttle,
#                                  +0x658), [2] speed O+0x860, [3] clutch O+0x890 (the lever, +0x644,
#                                  not +0x65c the clutch the engine is driven through)
sound_class_ctor boot 0x00165d60 (sound, bank) +0x30 the sample handle (slot << 16) and +0x34 the
#                                voice, both -1; +0x24 the length 0 until it is loaded
sample_length boot 0x0019d860 (spu, handle) seconds of the slot's sample: spu+0x3e8 + 8*slot, -106
#                             for none; the loaders write it to the sound's +0x24 (0x0016633c)
voice_start boot 0x0019cde0 (spu, handle, ...) starts the slot's sample (spu+0x3e4 + 8*slot)
# P49: Aud\audio.res's directory is [count][word][len][name][offset][size]...; the original's slot
# lengths (first_recording: Engine\125A\decel01 2.219 s, clutch01 0.252 s) are the files after each
# name, not before it as P42 read.
rider_wreck_sound_load boot 0x0033e5b0 reads the rider archive's rider.aud [Fall] and [IMPACT] into the
#                                      emitter wreck_sound plays from
crowd_sound_load boot 0x001fe220 reads event.aud's Crowd* lists when the track's CrowdPresent is set
music_list_load boot 0x001fd9f0 reads appstart.res aud\music\music.pb: [Artists], [Song N] Filename,
#                               Title, Artist
# P66 -- no landing sound on the bike. VehicleSoundEmitters.cpp's machine emitter (vtable 0x0045ea90)
# is made and loaded only by the car (0x0038f5b0, at +0x54c), MXBuggy (0x003990f0) and MXBiplane
# (0x00353a60); the motocross bike's sound setup (engine_sound_hands) makes the engine note +0x1494,
# the wreck clank +0x1498, the event emitter +0x149c and the drive-on loop +0x14a0, and no landing one.
machine_suspension_load boot 0x003dcd10 (emitter, ini, prefix, at, vel) [<p>TireParams] gates the rest;
#                                       [<p>TireTouchdown] Min/MaxLevelVelocity x1.46666 to +0x21c/+0x220,
#                                       attenuations +0x224 and +0x228 = max - min, list +0x200;
#                                       [<p>Suspension] Min/MaxLevelVelocity as read (no x1.46666) to
#                                       +0x248/+0x24c, +0x250 min attenuation, +0x254 max - min, list
#                                       +0x22c, SoundNVelocityTierStart floats at +0x244
machine_touchdown_play boot 0x003dd0a0 (emitter, 0, v*) touchdown: a no-repeat rand pick of the list,
#                                      level min + clamp((|vel+0xd4| - lo)/(hi - lo)) * range (int, vt
#                                      +0x30); suspension when v given: s = |v.y|, nothing below tier
#                                      1's start, else the last tier whose start <= s, level min +
#                                      clamp01((s - MinLevelVelocity)/(Max - Min)) * range, then sound_3d
#                                      and start. The car calls it on the frame its record's +0x40 goes
#                                      0 -> touched (0x0038e894), v the body's +0xb0 rotated by +0x18c
# P146 -- the plane's touchdown (crates/openmx/src/audio.rs TireTouchdown, proofs in openmx
#   tests/audio.rs and game-sim tests/flight_collision.rs). biplane_audio loads +0x67c with at = body
#   +0x60, vel = body +0x70, so |vel+0xd4| is the body velocity's length. biplane_measure calls it with
#   a1 0 on the grounded flag's rise (+0x594 0, +0x590 1). The disc has [biplaneTireParams] 30/150 and
#   [biplaneTireTouchdown] 60/40/150/100, one sound (Impact\plane_tire, 22050 Hz mono, 0.324 s), and
#   no [biplaneSuspension]: the suspension half never runs for the plane. The speeds are chopped on the
#   FPU: 87.99959564 and 219.99899292 (IEEE would give one bit more). The pick calls rand() (0x0040fd40)
#   once even for a list of one, before the crash test; +0x218 is never written (-1 throughout). The
#   sound's level word is its +0x3c (a1 of vt +0x30). biplane.p2m2 (frames 10971..12688): touchdowns
#   11665 (the nose-in's wreck tick) 42, 11739 (a bounce, wrecked) 40, 11748 (the put-back) 40, 12465
#   (a landing) 62, 12650 (the put-back) 40; the sound's +0x40 changes on each, 11748's too, though the
#   bounce's clip still sounds (9 ticks < 0.324 s): it starts over.
machine_wreck_load boot 0x003dc570 [<p>Wreck]: Min/MaxDistance, MinImpulse +0x228, MaxImpulse +0x22c,
#                                  list +0x208; +0x200/+0x204 the two gate words, +0x224 the impulse*
bike_wreck_clank boot 0x003dc8c0 the wreck emitter's frame (vt +0x38): when *+0x200 (bike +0x948 =
#                                wrecked, from the +0x868 reason copy) and *+0x204 (record +0x40
#                                touched) and the emitter idle and |bike +0x550| >= MinImpulse: a
#                                no-repeat rand pick at min(100, |imp|/MaxImpulse*100)%. Ported
#                                (P144, audio.rs WreckClank on BikeCrash): see P144 below
bike_wreck_entry_clank boot 0x003dcb90 (emitter, 0) the player's wreck entry's call: when the
#                                      emitter is idle, 0x003dc730 -- the same gate on the impulse
#                                      (|*+0x224| >= MinImpulse, else nothing), a no-repeat rand() pick
#                                      and the same level. So the entry clank is not unconditional
#                                      either: S109's reason-11 fall had none (P180's matched filter)
# P144 -- the bike's [Wreck] emitter (bike +0x1498, made in engine_sound_hands 0x00280fb0 with
# machine_wreck_load(emitter, ini, "" , bike+0x820 position, +0x840 velocity, bike+0x948,
# *(bike+0x7d0)+0x40), then 0x003dcb80(emitter, bike+0x550)). Live (journal p144-wreck-clank,
# capture/clank.toml + play.sh; context/capture/p144/clank2.csv, animandpump.p2m2 in copy 2, rows
# 3700..3939): the emitter's +0x224 is bike+0x550 and +0x200 bike+0x948 (start.ee.bin too). +0x748 = 11
# at 3725, +0x948 at 3726 (a tick late), both 0 at 3919. While down, bike+0x550 equals the wreck body's
# shock peak (*(*(bike+0x7a8)+0x110)+0xb0) on every row: (27.78, 54.63, -21.47) at 3792, then 15.23,
# 15.47 and 8.39 (|v|) at 3796, 3847, 3876, 0 elsewhere; the record's +0x40 the emitter reads is set
# 3792-3798, 3833-3835, 3842-3845, 3847-3850, 3870-3874, 3876-3883, 3892-3912 (the wreck bike's
# touches: a downed rider's riding record carries the wreck body, as noted above). The 125's emitter reads
# MinImpulse 18.8, MaxImpulse 40 (= Bike\125sx.res vehicle_*.aud [Wreck]). Its last pick (+0x220)
# goes -1 -> 3 at 3792 and nowhere else: one clank, at the bike's touchdown, level 100; the entry
# (+0x550 = 0 on a fall off the back) and the three later hits (< 18.8) are silent.
# P180 -- the rider's voice past the wreck entry. rider.aud [Params] MinImpulse (12.8) is at +0x238.
rider_voice_frame boot 0x0033e790 the rider emitter's frame: when +0x23c == 0 and the body record at
#                                 +0x240 has reason +0x104 not 0 and not 13, +0x1bc set (touching, as

#                                 idle (vt +0x90 == 0): 0x0033e850(emitter, 1), an [IMPACT] pick
rider_voice_pick boot 0x0033e850 (emitter, which) 0: [Fall] (+0x200 count, +0x208 sounds, +0x218
#                                last), 1: [IMPACT] (+0x21c, +0x224, +0x234); the no-repeat pick

rider_voice_play boot 0x0033e900 (emitter, sound) starts it on the emitter
wreck_clank_play boot 0x003dcaa0 (emitter, sound) the clank's start, placed on the body (P164); S173
#                                hooks it out in the oracle tests of bike_wreck_clank and the entry's
# S109 run2 (reason 11 at tick 3716, video t = (fc - 1938)/60): the matched filter finds pre_fall3 at
# 29.66 s (the entry), Vehicle WRECK05 at 30.82 s (tick ~3787, a_touching rises then) and Rider FALL02
# ([IMPACT]) at 30.93 s; nothing else from 26 s to 34.5 s.
# S173 -- both emitters past the entry, in game-sim (crates/game-sim/src/wreck_sound.rs Clocks/Kit, called
# from Rider::frame_bumped; journal 2026-10-28-s173-wreck-sound-draws). rider_voice_frame and
# bike_wreck_clank take the emitter in a0 and dt in f12 (Ghidra's param_1 is the float). Both lengths are
# mula x*x, madda y*y, madd z*z, sqrt.s (not the z-first sum the decompiler prints): the voice cries
# when MinImpulse < |v| (c.ole.s), the clank when |v| >= MinImpulse (c.olt.s); the clank's pick draws
# rand() for any count and moves on from +0x220 only for two or more. Proven over 10 000 generated
# cases each (tests/wreck_sound.rs) on first_recording's state, where the rider emitter's +0x23c is 1
# (frame off) and three bikes' emitters (human's bike 0x00dad810, emitter 0x00dcba90) read the 125's
# four clanks, MinImpulse 18.8, MaxImpulse 40. Live (capture/voices.toml + play.sh, animandpump.p2m2
# in copy 1, frames 3700..3959, context/capture/s173/voices.csv): +0x23c is 0 throughout the wreck;
# the body record's reason +0x104 goes 11 at 3726 (the bike's +0x748 at 3725), back to 0 at 3919;
# +0x1bc goes 1 at 3792 and stays 1 past the put-back (a latch, not a per-tick touch); +0x130 is 0
# until 3792, then (32.9, 71.5, -37.4) |v| 87, 180, 145, 104, 35, 23, 16, 8.7, then 5..9. The three
# picks of the whole wreck: [Fall] 2 at 3726 (the body's entry), the clank 3 and [IMPACT] 0 at 3792;
# gated voice touches over 12.8 at 3793..3795 and 3818 (21.5, 26 ticks into IMPACT0's 0.9295 s clip)
# stay quiet, busy. Clip lengths (+0x24, s): [Fall] 0.289 0.519 0.439 0.331 0.266, [IMPACT] 0.9295
# 0.8444 0.7403 0.8660 0.9537, [Wreck] 1.332 1.368 1.316 1.562. The decoder's (audio.rs Clip::seconds)
# are up to 1.3 ms shorter. Within 3792's seven draws the clank's index fits draw 2 or 3 and
# [IMPACT]'s draw 0, 1 or 6: the order of the two inside the tick is not pinned.
drive_on_frame boot 0x001b9690 the drive-on emitter's frame (vtable 0x004505e0): per wheel, the touched
#                              material's id (contact +0x89) plays its DriveOnSoundFile (vt +0xa8)
#                              or stops it (vt +0xbc)
drive_on_play boot 0x001b8da0 (emitter, wheel, material, level) starts/keeps the material's loop
material_table_load boot 0x003ea350 Col\template.cmt: per [material] ID, DriveOn*, Collision* (sound
#                                   file, distances, Min/MaxImpulse). Only props' materials (IDs 1..22
#                                   on free09); no dirt entry
# P145 -- the wheels' drive-on loop (journal drive-on-loop). The bike's emitter has one wheel, the rear,
# and no terrain slot: the ground gives material 0 and stops the loop. crates/openmx/src/audio.rs
# DriveOn; the material is game_sim's Ride::rear_material.
drive_on_lookup   boot 0x001b8d30 (emitter, material) the material's sound in the +0x214/+0x21c list, or 0
drive_on_update   boot 0x001b9060 (emitter, wheel, level) cur +0x22c == 0: nothing; else works out pitch
#                                 and level, sets position, pitch (vt +0x38), volume (vt +0x30), and
#                                 sound_3d; 0 from it stops the sound (vt +0x28) and keeps cur. Never
#                                 starts one
drive_on_speed    boot 0x001b91f0 mph = (int)(0.68182 * sqrt(x*x+y*y+z*z)) of the copied velocity
#                                 (+0xc0, mula/madda/madd); k (+0x208 per wheel, -1 unset) =
#                                 (int)(0.5 + FreqSlope * (own / (float)SampleSpeed)), own = sound vt +0x48
#                                 (+0x54); pitch = (int)(own + (float)((mph-SS)*k - (int)((k+1) *
#                                 (2^-31 * rand())))); level: mph <= RFS: (100-MinVol)/(RFS-MinSpeed) *
#                                 (mph-MinSpeed) + MinVol, else -(100-MinVol)/(MaxSpeed-RFS) * (mph-MaxSpeed)
#                                 + MinVol, integer quotients, clamped 0..100
drive_on_stop_old boot 0x001b94a0 (emitter, wheel) cur = 0, k = -1, stops cur's sound
drive_on_stop     boot 0x001b9570 (emitter, wheel) stops every material's sound for the wheel, k = -1, cur = 0
drive_on_ctor     boot 0x001b8790 the emitter; init 0x001b8a10 callocs the per-wheel arrays (+0x200 position,
#                                 +0x204 contact object, +0x208 terrain char, +0x20c touched word, +0x210
#                                 terrain gate), count at +0x230, level share +0x234
drive_on_table    boot 0x001b8be0 builds the material sound list from the table, +0x238 TerrainMask (200)
drive_on_wheel    boot 0x001b9a20 (emitter, wheel, position, contact object, touched): the bike's builder
#                                 0x00280fb0 calls it once, (bike+0x14a0, 0, rear wheel +0x140, bike +0x7d4,
#                                 rear wheel +0xd4); +0x208/+0x210 stay NULL. bike +0x7d4 is cleared each
#                                 substep by bike_wheel_contacts and set to the last rear (from.z <= 0)
#                                 contact's struck object; its +0x89 is the .col surface word's low byte
drive_on_share    boot 0x001b98b0 the level share: 100 / wheels when |copied velocity| != 0, else 0
sound_store_pitch boot 0x00166a20 sound vt +0x38: +0x44 = pitch only when (unsigned) pitch < 0x4000
sound_set_volume  boot 0x001667e0 sound vt +0x30: volume clamped 0..100

# in the journal wreck-camera. The camera's frame is the game's 30 Hz one (its lags get 1/30 s; the

wreck_cam_base_ctor   boot 0x0031be20 SimpleWreckCam, vtable 0x004592b0: +0x9c base fov 80, +0xa0 0.75
#                                     the look-at's height lag ratio (0.25 at 16:9), +0x114 45 the
#                                     second target's radius (25 at 16:9)

#                                     the eye's floor 5, +0x38c the ceiling FLT_MAX

#                                     doff = distance - 13 clamped, foff 0

wreck_cam_target      boot 0x0031cf60 point = (T0 + sum_i>=1 [T0 + (Ti-T0)(1-i/(n+1))max(0,1-d/R)])
#                                     / active; velocity averaged, &8 targets use +0x50, others their
#                                     point differenced; vertical x0.7 under 20 ft/s
wreck_cam_frame       boot 0x0031c670 the frame: pitch and yaw ease 0.05 a frame (yaw only with the
#                                     spread over 3); the direction +0x2d0 is not eased but set to the

wreck_cam_spread      boot 0x0031d480 spread, foff and doff walks: a tenth of the spread's growth
wreck_cam_fov         boot 0x0031dcd0 fov = base + foff clamped to [20, 82]; past 82 foff is set to 62
#                                     (the span), which is the live 62/-3 alternation
wreck_cam_look_at     boot 0x0031de70 point + vel x 0.2 while moving, held inside a 1 ft deadband
wreck_cam_distance    boot 0x0031e2d0 13 + doff clamped to [13, 100]

#                                     nearest [Part4]'s node (0.05-0.15 ft still, ~0.7 ft flying); the
#                                     writer of request+0x1e0 was not found
# The second target is the rider's bike camera block, rider+0xdd0, flag &8: its velocity is the one it
# had as the wreck began. screen_wreck_notice seeds the eye from the viewport's eye (lag 0.28, 0.2 for

# S56 — the ragdoll through whole wrecks (crates/game-sim/tests/ragdoll_wrecks.rs). The rider body
#   actor is the human's type-4 vehicle record less 0x400 (@game_manager -> 0x34 -> 0x34 -> 0x8 x8 ->
#   0x0, then -0x400; its own +0x14 is another rider's body, an AI's). watch/s56-ragdoll.toml
rider_body_update boot 0x0024e640 (dt in f12, the actor in a0) the body actor's vt update: the
#                                  request's +0x238/+0x23c effects, the two look clocks
#                                  (+0x474/+0x478), rider_body_mode while the request's +0x8 is set
#                                  (else placed +0x2ac = 0), rider_physics_update, then 0x0024e250
#   What the update reads off the rest of the game each tick: the request block [actor+0x488] and the
#   0x200 before it (the hand dummies, which the request's +0xf0/+0xf4 point at), the 0x100 at the
#   request's +0x1ac (the bike: centre +0x40, radius +0x60), and, once a bone nears the bike or
#   another collidable, those actors' scene data and the terrain cell cache at 0x0049f540 (ten 0x70
#   cells keyed ix<<16|iz). Laid in from a capture, the Ee replays a wreck bit-exactly until the first
#   contact with something moving that the capture does not carry (front 2608, side 2613, back 2491,
#   tip 2482 of the s56 captures); the reads it was then missing: 0x00601900(4), 0x00dfd580(27),
#   0x00dfd690(15), 0x00dfd6d0(11), 0x00dfe350(36), 0x00e379bc(3), 0x00e37a4c(7), 0x00e37c04(64),
#   0x011b1680(4), 0x011b16fc(20), 0x011b1734(7), 0x01672fec(3), 0x01673b14(2) (heap, this session).
#   rand_state draws within the tick: the rest of the game draws 0..~20 before the body updates;
#   the wreck entry draws 6 inside the update (side 2605), the damping/settle switch 1 each time.
#   A save state requested by PINE can land between the frame counter's step and the tick: the
#   recorder then logs the previous tick again under the next counter (rng and bones identical).
#   VU0 vsqrt (and so vrsqrt) is chopped under PCSX2 (Roundmode 3), not rounded: with nearest the
#   Ee's tick missed the captured bones by 1 ulp in 207..261 lanes; chopped, every lane matches.
# S62 — the ragdoll against moving collidables (ragdoll_contact::push_out_against / moving / NodeBox,
#   proved in tests/ragdoll_contact.rs on generated cases; the replay is tests/ragdoll_bike.rs).
#   bone_collision_push_out's moving paths (the "not ported" above) are contact.rs's relative velocity:
#   other +0x70 null -> (0,0,0,1); a mover (+0x7c, class +0x1b0 == 2) -> vt +0xbc spin and +0xb8
#   velocity at the node +0x254 about the origin +0x190; a collision object with +0x80 and a body
#   +0xf4 -> body q +0x170, centre +0x60, rates +0x1b0, vel +0x70; on a vehicle -> chassis +0xf0, node
#   +0xf8. It writes the object's +0xa0. The bone's velocity relative to that is what gets pushed; the
#   sweep uses the solve's last scene contact's other. bike_box_solve's +0x14 path: clamp in the
#   node's frame, then body pos = place(node, p) and body vel = rotate(node, p): the position, not the
#   velocity, is turned into the velocity (disassembly; the decompile reads as rotate(v)). Kept.
#   The wreck bike, by chain: the request's (actor +0x488) +0x1ac -> B (0x00dfdca0 in recording2's
#   heap); rigid body at B-0x780 (+0x60 pos, +0x170 quat, +0x1b0 rates: S56's missed 0x00dfd580/690/
#   6d0), collision objects at B-0x580 and B+0x670; watch/s56-ragdoll.toml's bike_a/bike_b carry
#   B-0x980..B+0xb80. S56's 0x00e37xxx / 0x011b16xx / 0x01672xxx-0x01673xxx reads are broad-phase list
#   nodes relinked every tick (one at 0x011b170c holds B, B-0x580 and B+0x670): no chain reaches them,
#   so the spec carries their 4 KB pools absolutely (w0e37000, w11b1000, w1672800, w1673800, and
#   w0601800 for 0x00601900); the heap is laid out the same from the same save state.
#   Replays (context/capture/s62, research/s62_wreck.py): side 2607, back 2477 and tip 2472 hold every
#   bone through the wreck, the put-back (~79 ticks in) and on (side to 3146, where the riding body's
#   rows start to alternate; back and tip to the capture's end); front 2602 holds to 2627 and parts
#   at 2628 in every lane with no push-out in the tick, nothing new read that the tick does not write
#   or the capture lay in, and the request/bike/pools from the row before do not mend it (not found);
#   front3 (another front wreck, states from 2630) holds through its end, 2687. In these wrecks the
#   bones' push-outs are almost all against the ground path; the scene push-out ran once a wreck,
#   against other = 0. The bike body's rates are read in front 2611, 2620..2627, side 2615, front3
#   2631. A slow tip-over was not reached: the game holds a slow or standing bike upright (no ground
#   roll limit; ground pitch limits not met up to ~60 deg), every wreck tried entered at 30+ ft/s.
#   Loading a wreck's state and holding the same pad does not ride the same wreck again (front3).
# S56 (reopened, 2026-10-23): why no slow tip-over can be ridden. bike_ground_step 0x00257a00 calls
#   chassis_set_flags(chassis, 1, 1, 0) every tick a wheel is down (rider +0x574 on_ground) and
#   (1, 1, 1) in the air: the ridden bike's roll freedom (body z) is off on the ground. Live on copy 2
#   (recording2's state, standing): +0x2ac..+0x2b4 read 1, 1, 0, and a roll rate written into chassis
#   +0x280/+0x290/+0x310/+0x31c is gone the next tick. bike_wreck_test 0x002522c0 has no ground roll
#   limit (the roll test on +0x98 runs only with +0x57c airborne, against +0x778 = 1.4835 rad). In the
#   air the roll is levelled too (2.5 rad/s written: 0.26 rad reached; 12 rad/s: 1.26). 18 rad/s after a
#   20 ft/s hop crossed it: reason 8 at 2.3 ft/s, the s62 "roll" capture (frames 2481..3180, states
#   roll-2481..2483 and roll-start = frame 2473, the hop and roll written in; research/s56/roll.pad).
#   The original's rider_body_update holds that capture on all 699 ticks with nothing laid in but

# P57: the bike's put-back ghost (S57's "timer not found" above, and its 0.7-1 s is wrong: it is 3 s).
# Live on copy 3 (freeride state 8, the journal's capture/ghost_watch.py). Every put-back of the bike,
# the wreck's own (kind 1) and L1+R1's alike, runs player_respawn -> bike_unwreck 0x00252ee0(kind) ->

ghost_start   boot 0x00250580 (rider, 0, kind) kind != 0: clock +0x7dc = length +0x7e0, state +0xb7c = 2,
#                             the bike model (*(+0x7a0)+0x30) and every part (+0x954 list of +0x950)
#                             see-through: model +0x136 bit 0x10 on (0x001a12a0, which also sets +0x148
#                             = 1.6, meaning unknown), alpha +0x16c = 0.5 (0x001a1b20); and both bodies
#                             +0x7c4/+0x7cc ignore the vehicle class mask (word 0x00468f68) via 0x0011b080.
#                             kind 0 makes the model solid (0x001a12d0)

#                             the clock > 0: body +0xe8 = 0, an overlap query 0x0011a150, +0xe8 = 1; the
#                             clock counts dt down only while body+0x116 bit 0x20 is clear (nothing
#                             overlapping); still > 0: alpha = 0.1 + gone * 0.9 (adda/madd), gone =
#                             (clock - len) / (0 - len), clamped to [0.1, 1], len 0 -> 0.1; else solid
#                             again, the mask removed (0x0011b160), +0xb7c = 1
# The ghost's length, rider +0x7e0, is 3.0: written by the rider's constructor, not read from a file.
# Live: the put-back's tick draws 0.5 with the clock at 3.0; the next 90 ticks 0.109999 .. 0.999909
# (dt 0.03333 a tick), the 91st the clock is -0.033028 and the flag is off. The bike's parts carry the
# model's alpha on every tick. L1+R1 in a running ghost starts it over at 3.0.

#                                    and velocity noise, and its Zone1..16 groups (none = 0xff) from
#                                    mxparticlezones.dat's ZoneDescriptor section
vehicle_fx_dust_zone boot 0x003d63f0 a zone change on the entry's tire: 0xff -> vt +0x1c (zone off: flag 2,
#                                    emitter off), else the zone's group and vt +0x20 (flag 2 cleared)
vehicle_fx_dust_update boot 0x003d64e0 latch +0x90: ground contact on, object contact off; off unless latched
#                                    and compression > MinCompression; rate = file x (base + scale c);
#                                    past the burst threshold + burst_scale c, growth min x GrowthMult,
#                                    noise min.x, max.x x VelocityMult and max.y = file max.z x it

#                                    none -> off; h < Begin full rate, < End rate x (1 - (h-B)/(E-B)), else off
# the manager (0x003d5aa0) also builds each tire's flags (+0x28) off its record: +8 ground contact (the
# plane tire's +0x80), +0xc object contact (+0x84), +0x10 compression (+0x8c), +0x24 zone (+0xb0's byte).
# MatchTerrainContour (emitter +0x238): emit (0x00150540) puts each particle on ground_query's height

# V40 — the bike's tire tracks, the manager's TireTrack entries (journal 2026-10-25-v40-bike-tracks,
# ported as crates/openmx/src/tire_tracks.rs). vehicle_fx_read takes Type TireTrack too, but skips the
# entries when the event has more than one human player (event +0x114): no tracks in a split.
vehicle_fx_track_ctor boot 0x003d7c40 defaults: MinimumSpeedThreshold 10, MinimumCompression 250,
#                                     MaximumCompression 1, BasePercentAlpha 0.7, FadeIn 1, FadeOut 5;
#                                     fade-out left (+0x44) 5, fade-in left (+0x4c) 0. NumberOfStrips
#                                     and HeightAboveGround are not set (every file gives them)
vehicle_fx_track_init boot 0x003d7d00 AttachedToTire, Material, NumberOfStrips, HeightAboveGround,
#                                     Width, BreakAngle (degrees), the thresholds, the fades, and the
#                                     ZoneDescriptor's Zone1..16 (None = 0xff, entry +0x18 + zone)
vehicle_fx_track_zone boot 0x003d7f60 a zone change: 0xff -> vt +0x1c (0x003d7fc0: flag 2, then off),
#                                     else vt +0x20 (0x003d7fd0: flag 2 cleared)
vehicle_fx_track_update boot 0x003d7ff0 last = tire while the strip is ended; d = |tire - last|; off
#                                     when (!spinning && comp <= MinC) || speed <= MinSpeed: f = out/FadeOut,
#                                     out -= d (x speed/MinSpeed below MinSpeed when MinSpeed > 1),
#                                     in = f FadeIn; else f = (FadeIn - in)/FadeIn, in -= d, out = f FadeOut.
#                                     a = f (Base + clamp01((comp-MinC)/(MaxC-MinC)) (1-Base)): above 0.005
#                                     strip vt +0x78 (add), else vt +0x7c (end)
vehicle_fx_track_off  boot 0x003d82c0 strip end, out = 0, in = FadeIn (vt +0x18 0x003d8310 the same and
#                                     the enable bit cleared; vt +0x24 0x003d8270 resets flags and the ring)
track_strip_init      boot 0x003d8cb0 the strip and its ring: NumberOfStrips batches of 16 vertex slots
track_strip_emit      boot 0x003d8d80 a row into the ring: 2 slots, 4 at a batch begun mid-strip (the
#                                     last row again, ADC set); the first row of a batch ADC; the two
#                                     slots after the write ADC. The whole ring draws; nothing ages
track_strip_add       boot 0x003d8f10 (Ghidra folds it into 0x003d8d80; read 0x3d8ffc..0x3d9600) u +=
#                                     d/2 a call; a row at d >= 2 or u+1 >= 8, edges pos + n h -+ (dir x n) W,
#                                     h = Height + rand() 2^-31 0.005 (0x0040fd40, the C rand); a new
#                                     segment past BreakAngle or u+1 > 8: count += 2, u = -8, alpha byte
#                                     (strip +0x1d4) = a 128 only then and at a strip's start
track_strip_end       boot 0x003d9600 ended = 1; count and u to 0 if it had rows
# bike_tire_record      0x0027adf0 builds each wheel's record the manager reads: position, ground and

# S58 — the spin step whole (wheel_spin_integrate, wheel_surface_drag, wheel_set_tire's 1/I), ported as
# tire::spin_integrate / surface_drag / spin_inertia and proved in tests/spin.rs (10 000 cases each,
# every word of the wheel and its chassis).
wheel_gyro_torque boot 0x0025d030 (scale in f12, wheel, torque) the spin step's gyroscopic helper: w = 1,
#                                 the torque added whole to the chassis' +0x140, and scale times its
#                                 part along the fork's axis (+0x8c0) to the hinge through
#                                 float_mul_add_into (jumps to 0x0025d180)
wheel_hinge_moment boot 0x0025d090 (scale in f12, wheel, force, point) (point - mount +0x150) x force,
#                                  handed to wheel_hinge_torque
wheel_hinge_torque boot 0x0025d0f0 (scale in f12, wheel, torque) the torque unrotated into the chassis'
#                                  frame (0x001b30e0), +0x6c += scale * (it . axis +0x8c0)
# The spin step's constants: 0x00420bf0 = 0.25, the scale its tire force goes through the hinge with
# for a balanced, steered wheel; 0x00426d70 (0,0,0,1) the both-in-the-air local velocity and force;
# 0x00426d80 (1,1,1,1) the couple's lane mask in wheel_hinge_force.
# S58 findings: the wheel's +0xb0 (BodyTireForcePosition) is the arm the tire force turns the chassis
# on, not the hub (+0xa0, the model's TireFront/TireRear nodes). +0x64 is not 1/TireInertia: it is
# 1/(r * (r * ((m/2) * 6/12))) with m = (6/12) * 2*pi*r*r, 0.614 for race125p; TireInertia (+0x68)
# only feeds the gyroscopic torque. WheelFriction is a rolling torque -fade*WheelFriction, never grip;
# grip is MaxDriveForce*load, past which the tire spins (or locks) and pushes SkidDriveForce*load.
# S63: the ghost's collision half, from the decompilation (no two-rider session of the original exists).
#   0x0011b080 / 0x0011b160 add / clear one class mask in a body's ignore list (+0xec words, +0xf0
#   count); 0x0011b070 writes the list's mode +0xe8 (1 exclude, 0 include). The pair filter 0x00119d70
#   (a, b, 1) rejects a pair when either side's list, in exclude mode, holds the other's class +0x84 —
#   and in include mode passes it only when the list holds it. The overlap/sweep query 0x0011a150 runs
#   every candidate of type 2 through it, and the bike's own collide 0x002708a0 runs that same query on
#   its body before it adds its ray contacts (own + mirrored), so a ghost neither strikes nor is struck.
#   ghost_step's include window makes its query see vehicles only; another ghost's own list (exclude)
#   still rejects it, so two ghosts do not hold each other's clocks. Bit 0x20 of +0x116 is set by the
#   query on a passing overlap; where it is cleared between ticks was not found (only on an inactive
#   body, at the query's end). The type-3 candidates (not filtered) also set it unless +0x116 bit 0 —
#   live (P57) the clock ran on the ground, so they do not reach a riding bike.
# P61 particles that meet the ground and take the wind (journal 2026-10-21-p61-biplane-particles).
# particle_update's collision: on a tick with (app tick counter +0x374) & 1, a particle with flag 0x200
# asks ground_query for the terrain under a copy of its point; d = y - ground; if d < 0 and vel.y < 0:
# vel *= Restitution (emitter +0x208); then d > -0.1 puts it on the ground and clears flags 4, 0x200,
# 0x20000, 0x80000 (no more move/collide/gravity/drag), else vel.y = -vel.y where it is.
# emt_read: GroundLevel (+0x1dc, default FLT_MAX) sets 0x200 when present; Restitution default 0.55;
# [Properties] Wind (+0x234), MatchTerrainContour (+0x238). emit adds the system's wind (+0x140) to
# the start velocity before the noise when Wind; the system is built with 0,0,0,1 there and nothing
# writes it (free06 and first_recording save states: 0,0,0,1 in the track system, vtable 0x004593c0).
vehicle_fx_collision_init boot 0x003d73e0 a CollisionModel entry: loads its .emt, then HasCollision
#                                         ORs 0x200 into the emitter's flags (the chunks say TRUE)
ini_switch           boot 0x0014b3f0 an int/switch key: T/F by first letter, ON/OFF, Y/N by first
#                                    letter (all ignoring case), else strtol base 0

# S61 — rider_body_update's whole tick, ported as crates/game-sim/src/ragdoll_body.rs (Actor::tick)
#   and held against the Ee tick from the same RAM in crates/game-sim/tests/ragdoll_wrecks.rs
#   (the_ports_tick_is_the_originals, ..._to_rest: 35 + 874 ticks of the s56 captures, every bone
#   lane, pin, joint, contact, flag, hold, box and frame bit-exact; 12 ticks with a moving hit skipped).
#   The order inside rider_body_mode's wreck arm, as ported: throw clock (+0x6bc/+0x6c0 count down,
#   the hands let go when +0x6c0 runs out), touching = any bone collision's +0x14, roll +0x490 runs
#   while touching, off_bike +0x6b0 once the main bone is more than 1 + the bike's radius (+0x60 of
#   [request+0x1ac]) from its centre (+0x40), ground_query, the settle test above, then the fade:
#   every stiffening restarts the torques' fade (+0x648 = its length), which counts down by dt after.
#   Outside the wreck the main bone's orientation is request quat (x) the main bone's rest, and that
#   rest is the one the skeleton held *before* its own update this tick; the step reads the rests
#   *after* it (the skeleton updates between the mode machine and rider_physics_update). Hooking
#   the body's vt+0xac (the bone quaternions' normalisation, run right after the world step) is
#   where the test reads the step-time rests.
#   The tail (0x0024e250): the impact is the sum of the nine bone collisions' dv in the order
#   2,3,4,6,5,8,7,1,0, added with vadd.xyz -- its w stays 1 (./kit dis 0x0024eb8c).
#   Correction to S56's "the damping/settle switch 1 each time": the settle switch draws nothing;
#   the draw seen on the settle tick is the animation selection (vt+0xa8) that runs after it.
#   The ride machine's gate (+0xd3c on the rider) is still *not* the body's +0x6b4: S26 saw it come
#   on with the body at 42 ft/s, so Down::settles keeps S53's hands-off-and-touching inference.

# S65 — the ride machine's settle gate (+0xd3c on the vehicle) and the off-bike sphere, found.
#   The rider body actor's request pointer (+0x488) is the human's vehicle + 0xb80 (vehicle vtable
#   0x454ef0 at +0x48 in the s56 states), so request+0x1bc *is* the vehicle's +0xd3c. The actor's
#   tail (0x0024e250) writes [actor+0x488]+0x1bc = actor+0x48c, the touching flag: that is the gate's
#   writer S26/S53 never found. Touching is written only by the actor's constructor (0x002472c0, to 0)
#   and rider_body_mode's wreck arm (0x0024c7e0, 0/1), so it keeps its last wreck's value while riding
#   (s26 shows the gate 1 after a respawn); the machine also wants the clock past 0.5 s (2.5 s for
#   reason 13), and the arm refreshes it long before that.
#   Order: the player update (0x0027c890) runs the base update (0x002520d0: physics, then the ride
#   machine 0x0027bc90 at its end), then 0x0027c2d0 fills the request; the rider (master list index
#   8, after the bike's 2) ticks later. So the machine reads the gate the previous tick's tail left.
#   tests/ragdoll_gate.rs: req_area's gate == a_touching on all ~1600 rows of the four s56 captures,
#   on with a_hand_hold > 0 on some (S53's "hands off first" was wrong).
#   Off-bike sphere: [request+0x1ac] is the wreck bike's collision object ("Wreckbike.col" in its
#   name, from wreckbikef.col). Its +0x60 radius 0x4089b63b = |root node half extent| through the
#   FPU (mula/madda/madd, sqrt; chopped -- a host float gives 0x4089b63c), its +0x40 centre = the root
#   node's centre (file offset 284) through the request's frame (+0x10..+0x4c, a copy of the wreck
#   bike's), translation first then x, y, z (transform_point's x,y,z,w order is 1-2 ulp off).

# S59 — off the map: the bike's cannonball (reason 13). Port: crates/game-sim/src/off_map.rs, wired in

# P73 — the binding table, whole (crates/game-sim/src/bindings.rs; tests/bindings.rs holds it against
#   three save states and runs binding_table_build in the interpreter for both pads x four presets).
#   The map is *(*(app_pointer)+0x104)+0x648 (first_recording: app 0x479990, manager 0x678550, map
#   0x679ca0), 0x750 bytes: +0x000 kind 0x10 (keyboard-like), +0x138 pad0 first, +0x270 pad1 first,
#   +0x3a8 pad0 second, +0x4e0 pad1 second, +0x618 kind 1; 0x4e commands x 4 bytes, a control index
#   in input::Control order, -1 unbound. All three riding states (first_recording, recording2,
#   biplane): pads 0 and 1 identical, preset 0, no second binding, kind 0x10 and kind 1 rows all -1.
binding_table_alloc  boot 0x0011d670 allocates the 0x750-byte map, every slot -1 (from 0x00169ac0)
binding_set_pad      boot 0x0011cfc0 (map, command, control, pad) one pad's first binding
binding_set_both     boot 0x0011d010 (map, command, control) both pads' first binding
binding_table_build  boot 0x00200f00 (app, pad, preset) the only writer of the table: the preset's
#                                    eight commands via binding_set_pad, then 52 via binding_set_both
#   Preset commands, in call order:  0x20   0x22   0x0e    0x0f    0x10  0x11    0x12      0x13
#     preset 0 (shipped):            L2     R2     Cross   Square  L1    Circle  Triangle  R1
#     preset 1:                      L2     R2     Square  Cross   L1    Circle  Triangle  R1
#     preset 2:                      L2     R1     Cross   Square  R2    Circle  Triangle  L1
#     preset 3:                      L1     R1     R2      Square  L2    Circle  Triangle  Cross
#   Fixed, both pads, in call order: 00 Left, 01 Right, 02 Up, 03 Down, 04 Start, 05 Select, 06 L2,
#     07 R3, 08 R2, 09 Up, 0a Down, 0b Left, 0c Right, 0d L3, 14 L1, 15 R1, 16 Circle, 17 L1, 18 R1,
#     19 Cross, 1a Cross, 1b Square, 1c L1, 1d R1, 21 Select, 2e Select, 25 Circle, 26 Triangle,
#     27 L1, 28 R1, 29 L2, 2a R2, 2b Cross, 2d L3, 30 Square, 1e L3, 1f R1, 23 R3, 32 Left,
#     33 Right, 34 Up, 35 Down, 36 Cross, 3a Square, 38 Triangle, 37 Circle, 39 Start, 3e R1,
#     3f L1, 40 L2, 4c L1, 4d R1. Never bound: 24, 2c, 2f, 31, 3b-3d, 41-4b.
#   Callers and the preset they pass: 0x00205c40, 0x0023f0b0 and 0x002b0300 build every pad on the
#     player's profile preset, *(*(app+0x3d4)+8) + 0x28 + pad*4; vehicle_switch 0x00210b90 builds
#     the profile's preset when the new machine is the bike (its case 0) and preset 0 for the five
#     others (biplane and the rest); the Controller Presets screen (0x003c8c30) rebuilds on a change.
#     So the table changes by player preset and by vehicle, never by mode; the bike's is the profile's.
#     The Map Stunts screen (OptionsControllerMapStunts, ctor 0x003cd330) never writes it: the two
#     setters have no other caller.
# What each command is, from its readers (value = pad_control_index's strength of the first binding;
#   event = binding_is_event, either binding):
#   00-03 Left Right Up Down: the bike's d-pad (pad_to_gesture: +0x628 = -1/+1 x, +0x62c = -1/+1 y,
#     flags +0x620/+0x624, and the trick gesture's direction), the ring (pad_ring_listener 0x0025d240),
#     the biplane's bank/dive/climb (biplane_input), the rider body (0x0024e3b0), two other vehicle
#     classes (0x0038d8e0, 0x00396490). Value, held = value != 0.
#   04 Start: pause, an event (0x00206e90).
#   0e Cross gas: bike +0x63c = 1.0 when the value is not 0, else 0 -- a SWITCH, the pressure is
#     dropped (P9's "the gas is analogue" holds for the biplane's +0x338, not the bike). 0x00396490
#     reads it as a pressure under a flag, else as a switch.
#   0f Square brake: bike +0x640 = 1.0 (or StoppieBrake +0x72c while the stoppie latch +0x728 is set),
#     0 released; a switch. The biplane's throttle down (+0x33c), a pressure.
#   gas and brake both > 0 on the bike: speed (+0x438) < 5 -> reverse +0x648 = 1, gas and brake 0;
#     else +0x648 = 0 and gas = (+0x57c != 0). The original's only reverse.
#   10 L1 clutch: bike +0x644 = 1.0/0, a switch; 0x00396490 +0x3e4.
#   11 12 13 Circle Triangle R1: the three trick buttons (pad_to_gesture's group, +0x9fc).
#   14 15 L1 R1: the biplane's rudder. 16 Circle: 0x00396490 +0x3fc. 17 18 19 L1 R1 Cross: the rider
#     body's input slot (0x0024e3b0, +0x44c..+0x460). 1a 1b 1c 1d Cross Square L1 R1: 0x0038d8e0.
#   1e L3, 1f R1: camera_nudge's two modifiers -- held, the right stick zooms / widens instead of
#     turning (1f also read at 0x0021f770).
#   20 L2, 21 Select, 22 R2: the camera manager's event handler (0x00200000, vtable 0x00452314):
#     20 press -> look-back preset (0x002006e0; release 0x00200820 restores it and sets +0x144),
#     22 press -> the camera at +0x64 while moving faster than 3 (0x00200740; release 0x002008b0,
#     +0x148), 21 press with +0x144 and +0x148 set (neither held) -> next preset (0x00200330(-1) ->
#     camera_next_preset). The original's camera button is Select, not L1.
#   27 28 29 2a 2b 2d 2e 30: the engine's free camera (0x00118920, from 0x00118f90).
#   32-3a, 3e-40: front-end screens' menu commands (Controller Presets 0x003c9130: 32 left, 33
#     right, 34 up, 35 down, 36 OK ...).
#   4c L1 + 4d R1: the reset chord (player_hand_reset, mxbiplane_reset_chord, 0x00391830, 0x00398c8c).
#   05-0d, 23, 25, 26: no constant-command reader found (the pad vtable's query slots take the
#     command in a register).
#   The bike's own vt+0xec, bike_input_limiter, then walks +0x650/+0x654 brake (front = rear),
#     +0x658 gas, +0x65c clutch toward those switches; not ported by P73.
# P78 — the camera manager's two holds, look back (0x20) and the other camera (0x22). Port: the look-back
#   as chase::LookBack (crates/openmx/src/chase.rs), proof crates/openmx/tests/right_stick_camera.rs;
#   the new layout's buttons in pad::Scheme::ride (Square and Cross reach the ride as L2 and R2).
#   The manager (MXCameraController.cpp, vtable 0x00452314; ctor 0x001feea0): +0x40 the active camera,
#   +0x54 the race camera (SimpleFollowCam, built at 0x001ff270), +0x58 (0x001ff450), +0x5c
#   (0x001ff550), +0x60 (0x001ff640), +0x64 the thrill camera (MXCameraThrill.cpp, ctor 0x00320b50,
#   set up at 0x001ff7f0 through 0x00320d00 with 75, 18, 200, 20, 5 and 200 or FLT_MAX by the
#   track's +0x3b8 +0x1c over 1500), +0x144 / +0x148 look back / other camera let go (0 while held),
#   +0x150 the camera to go back to, +0x154 the player's race-camera preset (the next-camera walk
#   0x00200330 writes it after every change; the ctor's is 0, or +0x13c - 1 in mode 5 with more than 7
#   presets), +0x138 preset count, +0x13c the look-back preset less one.
race_camera_setup    boot 0x0021e270 (..., cam, ...) the race camera's +0x1a8 = the first preset whose
#                                   +0x7c IsLookBackCam is set (the ctor 0x0021e1d0 leaves -1)
# the press handler, boot 0x00200000 (manager, event), not named: a test uses 0x200000; it does: 0x20 clears +0x144, 0x22
#                                   +0x148; with the race camera or +0x5c active, 0x20 with +0x1a8 >= 0
#                                   -> camera_look_back, else 0x22 -> camera_thrill, else 0x21 with
#                                   both let go -> the next-camera walk
camera_look_back     boot 0x002006e0 (manager) +0x5c active -> the race camera made active; the race
#                                   camera's set preset (vt +0xfc) to +0x1a8
camera_thrill        boot 0x00200740 (manager) unless the thrill camera is up already: the race camera
#                                   active and its target's +0x50 velocity longer than 3, or +0x5c
#                                   active and its +0xbc target's +0x40 longer than 3 -> the thrill
#                                   camera made active (vt +0x80)
camera_release       boot 0x002005e0 (manager, event) 0x20 -> camera_look_back_off, 0x22 ->
#                                   camera_thrill_off
camera_look_back_off boot 0x00200820 (manager) +0x144 = 1; the race camera active and on preset +0x1a8
#                                   -> preset +0x154, and +0x5c made active if +0x150 is it
camera_thrill_off    boot 0x002008b0 (manager) +0x148 = 1; the thrill camera active and +0x14c 0 ->
#                                   camera_restore (0x001ff140) and, look back let go, the race camera
#                                   active on preset +0x1a8 (the look-back, as if 0x20 were still
#                                   pressed; ported by P157, below)
#     +0x658 gas, +0x65c clutch toward those switches; ported by P77 (below).
# P77 — the levers are switches, and the limiter's walk (crates/game-sim/src/ride.rs: switch,
#   Controls::pad_read_tail, Held::step; crates/game-sim/tests/levers.rs runs pad_to_gesture then
#   bike_input_limiter in the interpreter on 20000 generated cases, bit for bit on +0x63c..+0x65c).
#   pad_to_gesture's lever half, not wrecked: +0x63c = (value 0x0e != 0), +0x640 = value 0x0f == 0 ? 0 :
#     (+0x728 stoppie latch ? +0x72c StoppieBrake : 1.0), then the reverse tail (P72), then +0x644 =
#     (value 0x10 != 0). Wrecked (+0x748): stick, gas, brake, clutch, +0x9fc, +0xa18 zeroed, +0x648 kept.
#   bike_input_limiter's lever half: +0x61c latch set when +0x610 is clear and +0x618 (last tick's
#     +0x610) set; cleared when +0x610 is set again, or +0x614 set with gas > 0 and clutch lever 0;
#     while set the brake lever +0x640 = 1.0 and +0x648 = 0. +0x618 = +0x610. Brake +0x650 rises at
#     +0x68c (BrakeRateLimit), drops at once; +0x654 = +0x650. Gas +0x658 = 0 when +0x614 is clear or
#     +0xa44 (the no-hander) is set, or when reversing (+0x648 != 0) on the ground (+0x57c == 0); else
#     rises at +0x688, drops at once. Clutch +0x65c toward +0x644 at +0x690 rising, +0x694 falling.
#   +0x610 / +0x614: the bike's steer and throttle enables, 1 at the bike's set-up (0x0024f5d4), 1 on
#     a put-back (0x0027f61c), set by a message handler (0x0024a120/0x0024a138), and copied each bike
#     tick (0x0027ca2c) from the words at +0x1068 / +0x106c, which in first_recording point at the
#     bike's own +0x610 / +0x614 (a no-op). Every riding save state: +0x610 = +0x614 = +0x618 = 1,
#     +0x61c = 0. The same tick, before the copy, gas +0x63c = 0 while *(+0xf9c)+0x1c0 > 0 (0x0027ca18).
#     The port has no lock that clears them, so it leaves the latch, the enables and the +0x1c0 cut out.
#     (P158 ported all three; below.)
# P158 — what clears the enables, and the +0x1c0 cut. Port: ride::Lock in Held (inverted: a lock where
#   the original keeps an enable), Held::step's latch / steer / throttle half, Controls::gate_cut and
#   Score::gate_penalty (crates/game-sim/src/ride.rs, score.rs); proof crates/game-sim/tests/levers.rs
#   (the enables, the latch and the cut generated over the 20000 cases, +0x640/+0x648/+0x618/+0x61c/
#   +0x670 compared after the limiter; the card list's run-down in the interpreter).
#   Nothing clears +0x610 / +0x614 during a ride. Every store to bike +0x610..+0x61c in the ELF (an
#   objdump scan of every sw at those offsets, and of every address formed as base+0x610/0x614): the
#   base vehicle ctor 0x002472c0 (all four 0), the vehicle builder 0x00249cb0 (0x0024a0f4..0x0024a138:
#   the type-8 parts whose +0x7c->+8 kind is 0x0e/0x11/0x10/0x0d go to +0x61c/+0x618/+0x610/+0x614 as
#   pointers -- the "message handler" P77 named is this, the set-up), the set-up 0x0024f5d4 (1, 1, 1,
#   0), the put-back 0x0027f61c (+0x610 = 1), the limiter's +0x618/+0x61c, and the bike tick's copy
#   0x0027ca2c / 0x0027ca38 through +0x1068 / +0x106c, which are written only by the ctor 0x00279ed0 (0)
#   and the same tick (0x0027cab4: bike+0x610 / +0x614 when +0x1068 is 0). The other sw hits at those
#   offsets (0x001e009c, 0x0011d6cc, 0x00393b78) are other objects. So the latch never sets in the
#   shipped game; neither the freestyle event's countdown nor a race's start or finish touches them.
#   The +0x1c0 cut is the score card's start-gate penalty: card +0x1c0 is 0 at the card's join
#   (0x0025f4d0), run down by dt while above 0 for every card on mgr+0x34 (score_gate_penalty_tick,
#   first thing in score_manager_tick unless the manager's flag 8), and set by the race manager's tick
#   (0x002fcff0, vtable 0x004587a4, its manager at +0x50): while its start gate +0x3dc has flag 1 (closed),
#   every card on +0x84 whose entity crossed the gate between last tick's +0x60 and now
#   (start_gate_crossed == 1) is pushed back along its +0x20 vector times (-5 - 5*rand/2^31) and gets
#   +0x1c0 = rand*(1/2^31) + 1.0 -- one to two seconds of no gas. The bike tick's cut (0x0027ca18) runs
#   before the base update's limiter (vt+0xec, 0x002520d0); pad_to_gesture is reached from a vtable
#   slot (0x0027ff90, vtable word 0x00454f10) whose caller was not traced, so "after the pad read" is
#   assumed from the cut being live at all, not proved.
score_gate_penalty_tick boot 0x0025ffc0 (dt f12, mgr) every card on mgr+0x34 (+0x1c8 next): +0x1c0 -= dt while above 0
start_gate_crossed boot 0x00246720 (gate, from, to) 1 crossed, 2 beside, 0 not, 4 gate not closed (flag 1 clear)
# Strings: the preset screen's labels are STR_BUTTON_1..6_TEXT / STR_BUTTON_DES_* in AppStart's
#   loc\ui.loc, whose values are encoded (not plain or UTF-16); not decoded.
# S66 — the bike's put-back record and where L1+R1 / the wreck clock put the bike, ported as
#   ride::Ride::keep_back / place_back (crates/game-sim/src/ride.rs) and held bit for bit in
#   crates/game-sim/tests/ride.rs (the_put_back_record_is_kept_bit_for_bit: 1975 ticks of five
#   captures on free06 and free09, riding and down; every_captured_put_back_lands_where_the_original_put_the_bike: 9).
put_back_keep               boot 0x0027e9d0 (bike, point, heading) -> kept: the race manager's call
#                                           after the physics; heading = race +0x120 (the node's
#                                           row 2). Probe, freeride: x/z/w of the collision record
#                                           +0x7d0 -> +0xe0 (the sweep's point, node(T-1) applied to
#                                           (0,3,0,1)) with y = body +0x7a4 -> +0x64 riding; the
#                                           ragdoll part *(+0x7a8 -> +0x124) +0x40 while +0x748 != 0.
#                                           ground_query on +0x7ac; an overhead probe (radius 1000,
#                                           from y+10 down, (y-ground)+10 long) above the ground
#                                           refuses. +0xc84 != 13: put_back_clear, and normal.y > 0.8
#                                           keeps +0x3b0 = spot + up_old * +0x13a0 (vmulx/vadd xyz),
#                                           +0x3c0 = heading flattened (vrsqrt), +0x3d0 = the normal,
#                                           +0x1380 = race +0xa0; the checkpoint +0x1350 copy only in
#                                           race modes. +0xc84 == 13 and *(*(+0x7a8 -> +0x120)+0x40):
#                                           the body node's translation, mode 1, normal straight in.
put_back_clear              boot 0x0027e530 (bike, spot in/out, heading, normal out, mode) -> clear:
#                                           14 probes in one bundle, radius 40: down from y+10 (20);
#                                           12 level at y+3 (20) fanned by the curve table's entry
#                                           with no correction at ((i-1)*2pi)/13; one along the flat
#                                           heading from 20 behind at y+6.5 (40). ground_query, then
#                                           put_back_solid; probe 0 only lifts the spot.
put_back_solid              boot 0x0027df50 (bike, y in/out, normal out, ...) the bundle against the
#                                           meshes (type 2, +0x31 & 1), type-3 groups inside 20 ft,
#                                           and in mode 1 the checkpoints; broad phase cached on
#                                           +0x1390 / +0x13a4 (refreshed by 0x001ab310 when +0x13a4 <
#                                           +0x13a8).
put_back_gate               boot 0x0027e410 (bike, point) a bounds table at 0x00420c00 used only when
#                                           two config words are zero; passed on free06 and free09.
# S75 — put_back_solid read whole, and driven from the record test's world (crates/game-sim/tests/
#   ride.rs, TheOriginalsTerrain::solid). Args: (bike, bike +0x148c, filter, bundle = bike +0x1484,
#   normal out or 0, y in/out). The bundle as the put-back fills it: +0x120 count, *(+0x134) lengths,
#   *(+0x138) origins, *(+0x13c) directions, +0x150 centre, +0x160 radius; the record *(+0x6c) is
#   cleared over the count (+0xa4 flags, +0x98 points, +0x94 normals, +0xa8 fractions, all zero).
#   Broad phase: |+0x150 - bike +0x1390|^2 -> bike +0x13a8; past bike +0x13a4 the list at bike +0x1488
#   is regathered (collision_search_gather, mode 1 when the race record's +0x40 is 1, else 3) and
#   +0x1390 moves to the centre. Each entry (object, distance) under the bundle's radius: type 2 with
#   +0x31 & 1 and not (+0x84 & the word at 0x004e2130) -> 0x0011c210 (bundle, object, record), and
#   on a hit y = max(y, record point 0's y) (normal copied with it), and any flag 1..count-1 refuses;
#   type 3 -> each part (group_part_count/group_part) whose u16 cell x/z at +8/+0xc times the world's
#   cell size is within 20 of the centre refuses; then mode 1 with race flag 1: any checkpoint within
#   sqrt(r^2/4 + 100) refuses. Returns the refusal. The overhead probe ignores it and reads only y.
#   The filter argument is not read in it; put_back_clear passes it to put_back_refile.
put_back_refile             boot 0x001ab310 (bundle, centre, filter; radius in f12) -> 0x001adc70 on
#                                           the bundle's +4: re-files the bundle in the world's tree;
#                                           put_back_clear calls it before the ring when bike +0x13a4 <
#                                           +0x13a8, i.e. after a query that regathered
put_back_ring_filter        boot 0x00420d80 the filter quad put_back_clear hands put_back_solid/refile
put_back_over_filter        boot 0x00420db0 the filter quad put_back_keep's overhead probe hands it
bundle_sweep_object         boot 0x0011c210 (bundle, object, record) -> met: nothing unless the object
#                                           is +0x31 & 1 and its sphere (+0x40, +0x60) meets the bundle's
#                                           (+0x150, +0x160); clears the record's flags (+0xa4) and
#                                           fractions (to -1) and its tally first, never its points or
#                                           normals; then by the object's class at +0x68 (2, 3: nothing).
#                                           put_back_solid reads its answer object by object.
put_back_mask               boot 0x004e2130 the word put_back_solid masks an object's +0x84 with: a hit
#                                           there skips it (the bike's own, 0x10000, in both states)
hand_reset_thunk            boot 0x00281930 this -= 80, into player_hand_reset: the input interface's
#                                           slot (vtable 0x00454fb0), so the chord runs before the
#                                           update (0x00281950 -> 0x0027c890, slot at 0x00454f84).
#   Measured: the put-back applies +0x3b0 as it stood the tick before (5 chord, 4 clock resets, 2
#   tracks); ghost_start(this, 0) places the node at +0x3b0 exactly and orients it by
#   basis(+0x3c0, +0x3d0, orthogonalise, keep_forward=0), +0x5e0 = +0x3b0. player_hand_reset in

# S71 — the cannonball's drag, ported: ride::Ride::drag_halved (set by the throw, cleared by both
#   put-backs) and off_map::drag (the .phy Drag, times 0.5 while thrown), read by the ground step's
#   air drag. +0x6a8's only readers on the bike are the .phy loader (writes it), the ground step and
#   the clause/unwreck pair; the ground step is skipped while +0x748 != 0, so the halved value is
#   never read in a ride. Proved in tests/off_the_map.rs: bike_unwreck run over 12,000 bikes gives
#   the .phy drag back bit for bit after a cannonball and leaves other reasons' alone, except a halving
#   flushed to zero (|drag| < 2^-125: stays 0) and the top exponent (doubling clamps to 0x7f7fffff);
#   the five s59 captures' drag column is riding, halved from the throw's row to the put-back's, then
#   riding again, bit for bit.
# S69 — the wreck bike's collision (crates/game-sim/src/wreck_bike.rs), proved in
# tests/wreck_bike.rs against the s62 states and captures: 362 ticks over five wrecks bit-exact.
# The wreck bike hangs off a world node of its own (0xe17ef0 in every s62 state; the request's
# +0x1ac is the part wrapper B, body B-0x780, collision object B+0x8c0); the node's update is the
# bike's whole tick.
collision_node_update boot 0x0026f800 (node, dt in f12): every collision object vt+0x74 (clear), then
#                                     vt+0x7c (collide), then dynamics_world_step on node+0x78
#                                     (iterations 1, rate 115); run alone on the s62 RAM it holds the
#                                     captured body and collision object to each wreck's end
# Wreckbike.phy goes through physics_part_read 0x0015a500 and the setters above (0x0026fd80,
# 0x0026ff10, 0x00270220, 0x002700d0, 0x002701d0, 0x002701b0); its listeners are
# body_wrapper_force 0x0015af40, body_wrapper_preforce 0x0015b050, body_wrapper_post 0x0015b070
# (pose -> the transform's rows by vu_quat_to_mat3 0x0013ebd0). Off the running game, not keys:
# the collidable's flags (C+0x1f8, +0x84) 0x9a058, actors (+0x110) 8, collides-with (+0x114)
# 0x40000. The ray probe's eleven rays are bike\col\wreckbikem.col's segments, bit for bit.

# S72 — the wreck page's put-back delay. Port: crates/game-sim/src/wreck_page.rs, wired into rider.rs;
#   proved in tests/wreck_page.rs and tests/off_the_map.rs. With page != 0 and a screen (player +0x20 ->
#   +0x50), ride_reset_request 0x0027b9f0 registers bike+0x1340 on viewport fader [which] (fader_listener)
#   and calls viewport_fade; which = (0x0041f770 -> 0x3cc -> +0x64 != the player's screen), 0 for one
#   player. Without a page or a screen it calls the screen's vt +0xa0 and respawns at once.
fader_start boot 0x001c8e80 (fader, out ms, in ms) via 0x001c8400: +0x50 = 1, +0x58 = in ms, +0x34 = 0,
#                           +0x38 = ms/1000, +0x3c = 0, +0x48 = 1, +0x4c = 0
fader_update boot 0x001c8a80 the fader's vt +0x38 (vtable 0x00450ec0): t < 1 and duration != 0 ->
#                            elapsed += dt, t = min(elapsed/duration, 1)
fader_draw boot 0x001c8f50 the fader's vt +0x48: fading out, alpha = (int)(255 t + 0.5); at 255 +0x54
#                          (held) = 1, +0x48 = 0, every listener's vt +8; then, with +0x50, the fade in
#                          from elapsed 0. Fading in at alpha 0: held and in cleared, listeners' vt
#                          +0xc, the list emptied
bike_page_listener boot 0x00281c10 the bike's listener vt +8 (vtable 0x00455128): this - 0x1340 to
#                                  player_respawn 0x0027bb20
screen_page_off boot 0x00200ab0 the screen's vt +0xa0, to 0x00200ac0: only the HUD flags +0x144/+0x148;
#                               the fader is not touched
#   The machine's "page up" query is the fader's +0x54 || +0x48.
#   Live (research/s72_page.py, watch/s72-page.toml, copy 5 slot 9 = free09's state 7; captures
#   context/capture/s72/{north,east,west}.csv, seven pages: reasons 6, 2 and 13): the fader updates after
#   the bike in the tick; its dt is whole ms of frame time, 34/33/33 or 34/33/34, so three updates make
#   t >= 0.99999994 (alpha 255) and two never do. The respawn is in the draw after the request tick's
#   third update (end of tick R+2); the bike rides from R+3, and the clock left on it is clock_R + 2 dt.
#   The fade in is three more updates (elapsed 0.1 or 0.101), then held and the listeners clear.
#   Rows may read between the bike's update and the fader's, and once inside the black draw (west).
# S84 — the chord inside the fade out. player_hand_reset's request (page 0) respawns at once and calls
#   the screen's vt +0xa0, which leaves the fader alone, so bike+0x1340 stays a listener and the black
#   draw runs bike_page_listener -> player_respawn on the riding bike: bike_unwreck -> vt +0x138
#   0x0027b650 -> ghost_start, which places the node on +0x3b0 as it stands then (after the tick's
#   put_back_keep) and starts the ghost at 3.0. Live (research/s84_chord.py, watch/s84-chord.toml, copy 2
#   slot 9 = copy 5's slot 9 above; context/capture/s84/d0a, d0b inside the window: chord on R+1 and on
#   R+2; d1a, d1b: chord on R+3, after the page's own put-back): +0x5e0 equals +0x3b0 of the draw's row,
#   bit for bit, on both. Port: rider.rs step 7 calls place_back for a riding bike too.
#   S85: the update's dt ($f12) is whole ms times 0.001 (mul, not div: the live elapsed bits say so,
#   0.066 + 0.034 = 0.099999994); the draw's alpha is cvt.w.s(0.5 + 255 t) out, cvt.w.s(0.5 + (255 - 255 t))
#   in, black. Port: game_sim::wreck_page::Shade, proved on the s72 captures in tests/wreck_page.rs.

# S74 — the put-back's two-tick hold on the rider's body, ported as rider::PutBack
#   (crates/game-sim/src/rider.rs) and held in crates/game-sim/tests/rider_anim.rs
#   (the_put_back_holds_the_body_as_the_original_does: 2695 ticks of five captures, 7 chords, 2 clocks)
#   and crates/game-sim/tests/motion.rs (the_put_back_holds_the_original_pose_joint_by_joint: 18 held
#   ticks, 22 joints, within 1e-4, worst 6.6e-7).
#   The player update 0x0027c890: after the base update 0x002520d0, a delayed counter +0x1480 that
#   raises +0x3a0 when it runs down to 0 (not seen in any capture, not ported); then 0x0027c2d0 fills
#   the request at +0xb80 (+0xb8c = +0x3a0, the body's held; +0xc84 the reason); at the end
#   `if (+0x3a0 && +0x3a4) +0x3a0 = 0; +0x3a4 = +0x3a0;`. +0x3a0 = 1 is set by the ghost chain
#   0x0027b650 (both put-backs) and by 0x0027bbe0. Measured: the request's reason is the bike's
#   +0x748 from before the tick's verdict on every captured tick — the wreck's entry tick has it 0 and
#   a clock put-back's tick still 11 — so the chord (input phase, before the update) is held on T and
#   T+1, the clock's put-back on T+1 and T+2, with the body (mode +0x47c) still 2 on the clock's T.
#   Body actor = the type-4 record less 0x400 whose +0x488 is bike + 0xb80; +0x230 joint count (22),
#   +0x234 joints (0x60 each: +0x34 node, +0x40 translation, +0x50 quaternion); nodes' local rows
#   +0x40..+0x7f, world rows +0x80..+0xbf. Held ticks: state +0x8f0 = 30, ramp +0x68c = 0, both lags
#   (+0x630/+0x634 -> value) snapped to the request's 0.5/0.5, and node 0's local translation is
#   joint 0's. From T+2 (chord) the nodes part from the joints: the riding body is the ragdoll's.
#   The settle (+0x6b4) is kept through the put-back: 2 -> 0 skips the wreck's clean-up arm.

# S70 — the wreck entry's hand-over from the riding skeleton (ragdoll_ride::Down::enter_from).
#   rider_body_wreck_enter calls the actor's vt+0xa4 (rider_ragdoll_pose) with a1 = 0 (moving) and
#   f12 = the tick's frame time (0.03333 on side-2604 -> 2605, hooked), then vt+0xa0 (the reset). No
#   bone is given the bike's velocity there; only the throws of some reasons set velocities after.
#   While riding, the skeleton IS the riding ragdoll: after each world step 0x0030f4c0 (not wreck
#   mode) / vt+0xac 0x0024df80 (wreck mode) normalise every body's quaternion and set its node by
#   transform_set_pose to body.pos less turn(q, cofg) (ragdoll_ride::node_of). So the nodes the pose
#   reads on tick T are the bodies at the end of T-1 (to 0-4 ulps through node_of), and the drawn
#   matrix (part +0x60) is the node at the end of the step before (rows tens of ulps off a quat
#   matrix). On a tick whose row repeats (tip 2473) the drawn matrix is the last step's before it.
#   The writer of part +0x60 per riding tick was not found. The starts the pose leaves on each body
#   (+0x30 pos, +0x40 vel, +0x160 quat, +0x140 rates) survive the entry tick in RAM.
#   tests/ragdoll_entry.rs: the port's pose from what the original's read == its starts bit for bit
#   (side); from the captured bodies within NEAR on side/back/tip (front's 2599 row is torn).
# S110 — the wreck entry from the riding bodies (ragdoll_riding::Riding::hand_over, rider.rs).
#   rider_ragdoll_pose's a1: nonzero = still (velocity and spin zero, as for dt <= 0); the repose
#   passes 1, the wreck entry 0. In rider_body_wreck_enter the five throw draws are rand_raw from
#   0x0024bc5c, 0x0024bca0, 0x0024bce4, 0x0024bd74, 0x0024bda0; the sixth is wreck_sound's pick
#   (sound_pick, return 0x001b5858). run2 (S109): 3716 the verdict (reason 11) and no step at all,
#   3717 a riding tick, 3718 a hidden riding tick then the entry and its first wreck step.
# V41 — the path cars and the scene's props, as the original draws them.
#   car class (the .toy's [CarN] on a .mot clip): init 0x001147c0, mot load 0x00114fc0, arc length
#   0x00115330, update 0x00115540 (the 1/30 s scene step), lag walk 0x00116150, curve 0x00116410;
#   .toy reader 0x00289710; the ground snap is ground_query 0x001dc960; the heading basis 0x0013e330.
#   ini line reader 0x0014a040: drops ' ', '\t', '\r', '\n' outside [...] and quotes before the
#   split at '=' — so SLT=Free06_Power Line_A loads free06_powerline_a.psg (scene.rs slt_name).

#   4235.519,260.544,4304.064 identity, box centre (6.852,3.352,-14.884) half (172.997,10.131,100.064).
# V46 — why the root turn is never drawn, for props and path cars alike. The model object (class
#   ctor 0x00176430, 0x1c0 bytes, vtable 0x0044f870; +0xa0 load 0x00176a90 -> model load 0x001a0360)
#   reads the .psg node tree in 0x001b3890, whose node 0 IS the object: the root's file matrix goes
#   into object+0x40, the object's own local, and the placement then overwrites +0x40..+0x7f

#   space; world box 2313.517,144.045,2810.838 = pos + rows*box, no half turn. Also _04 0xd6cf20,

# S79 — the points: crates/game-sim/src/score.rs, proved in crates/game-sim/tests/score.rs. How each was
# found is in research/journal/2026-10-21-s79-trick-points.
# The rider's score card is *(rider+0xf9c), the score manager *(rider+0xfa0) (one for the session;
# its cards are a list from mgr+0x34 through card+0x1c8). Card words, by offset:
#   +0x8 flags (0x2 takes part, 0x1 a networked card, 0x20 combo open, 0x40 newest node scoring,
#   0x80 newest node growing), +0xc8 the node list (newest first), +0xcc its count, +0xd8 the combo's
#   seconds, +0xdc the banked total, +0xe0 the combo's points.
# Node (0x28 bytes): +0x0 flags (1 counts, 2 round to nearest, 4 round down), +0x4 id (0-23 a trick,
#   24 backflip, 25 wheelie, 26 stoppie), +0x8 points, +0xc base, +0x10 rate a second, +0x14 mult,
#   +0x18 extra, +0x1c seconds held, +0x20 granularity, +0x24 next.
# Manager: +0x5c flags (0x1 running, 0x8 stopped), +0x64 event mode (3 in freeride), +0xac the
#   total's granularity (1.0), +0x48 a free list of 100 nodes shared by every card, +0xf8 its clock.
# Rider words beside the card: +0x13f0 / +0x13f4 seconds on the rear / front wheel alone, +0x13f8 the
#   slow speed (5.0) +0x438 is compared with at the top of the rider's tick, +0x1400 the backflip
#   watch (0..3), +0x140c a per-trick rate bonus (zero for all 26 in every state looked at).
score_combo_start           boot 0x00266e00 (mgr, card) opens a combo on a card that takes part and
#                                           is not networked, the manager running: +0xd8 and +0xcc
#                                           zeroed, the old nodes back to the pool
score_node_push             boot 0x00267620 (base f12, extra f13, rate f14, mult f15, granularity f16,
#                                           mgr, card, id, flags) a node at the head of an open combo,
#                                           points = base * mult; card |= 0x40
score_finish                boot 0x00267820 (mult f12, mgr, card, ok) the head node: not ok it stops
#                                           counting, ok its points * mult and rounded; card &= ~0xc0
score_hold                  boot 0x00267a30 (mgr, card, on) card |= 0x80 if scoring, or &= ~0x80
score_combo_end             boot 0x00266fb0 (scale f12, mgr, card, ok) closes the combo: ok adds every
#                                           node's points * scale to the total; the total rounded
#                                           down to mgr+0xac either way, +0xe0 zeroed, the nodes freed
#                                           (but in event mode 5)
score_grow                  boot 0x00267a90 (dt f12, mgr) the manager's tick, after every rider's: an
#                                           open combo's clock, the growing head's (base + held *
#                                           rate) * mult rounded, +0xe0 the counting nodes' sum
rider_score_trick_start     boot 0x00280b30 (rider, id) rider vt+0x10c: a trick's clock started
rider_score_trick_stop      boot 0x00280d00 (rider) rider vt+0x110: the head * the combo's count
rider_score_trick_abort     boot 0x00280d70 (rider) rider vt+0x114: the trick and the combo lost
# jump_record_end (above, 0x0027b6e0) is the score card's wreck: the head lost, the combo closed
#   unbanked, +0x13f0/+0x13f4/+0x1400 zeroed. Called by player_wreck_entry and ride_reset_request.
# The scoring section of the player update 0x0027c890 is ported as Score::frame (one-wheel nodes,
# the bank, the backflip watch); the manager's mode handler for mode 3 (0x00268d00) scores nothing.
# S92 — the freestyle event: crates/game-sim/src/event.rs, proved in crates/game-sim/tests/event.rs
# and the gate in crates/game-sim/tests/score.rs. How each was found is in
# research/journal/2026-10-26-s92-freestyle-event.
# Correction to S79's card flags: 0x1 is "out" (the card's event time ran out), not networked; the
#   join's flags are 0x80804; 0x40000 holds the card's clock; card +0xb4 seconds taking part, +0xc4
#   seconds left, +0x1c4 the card's listener chain, +0x4 its owner (vt at +0x20).
# Manager, beyond S79: +0x30 its listener (vt at +0x1c, next at +0x4 through +0x18), +0x5c flags
#   (0x80 counting down, 0x2 all done, 0x10 a card timed out, 0x2000 the end called, 0x4000 rank
#   by total, 0x200 zones), +0xa0 the time limit (0 = none), +0xa4 the leading card,
#   +0xbc the countdown's length, +0xc0 its listener interval, +0xc4 its elapsed, +0xc8 the interval's
#   accumulator. The modes written to +0x64 are 1, 2 and 3 only (no store of 4, 5 or 6 anywhere).
score_manager_tick          boot 0x0025fcd0 (dt f12, mgr) countdown while not running (flag 0x80), or
#                                           the mode's handler, the leading card, score_grow, the clock
score_countdown_start       boot 0x002603d0 (mgr) length > 0: flag 0x80, elapsed and accumulator 0;
#                                           else running at once, every card takes part
score_mode3                 boot 0x00268d00 (dt f12, mgr) all done judged first; each card taking part
#                                           and not out: distance, jump records (0x80000), +0xb4, its
#                                           time down by dt unless held, out at 0 or under
# The stunt competition activity (the freestyle event): constructor 0x003dd340, its manager at +0x50,
#   its listener at +0x160. Activity words: +0x19c riders, +0x1a0 the target (hit.dat [HitData]
#   StuntCompetitionScore, read 0x003ddea0), +0x1a4 the leading rider, +0x1b4 tie; player +0xc0 the
#   target-reached latch.
stunt_competition_start     boot 0x003de450 (activity) cards' time 0, countdown 3.33 s at 0.01 s
stunt_competition_join      boot 0x003dd520 (activity, player) the card re-registered, total set aside
stunt_competition_frame     boot 0x003ddf70 (dt, activity) leader, tie, warning, target; then the tick
stunt_competition_end       boot 0x003dd870 (activity, player) the verdict; the total put back
stunt_countdown_listener    boot 0x003dec80 (listener) thunk to 0x003de8c0: past 3.33 s the limit 120

# S78 — rider against rider: the riding bike's collision record, read for the port; the own-chassis
# half of the resolution ported (contact::resolve_ridden) and proved in tests/contact.rs
# (the_contact_resolution_on_a_chassis_matches_the_original: the plane's record pointed at a scratch
# chassis + transform, 10k cases, every write bit for bit, 616 single-contact impulses chassis on chassis).
#   The riding bike's record is bike+0x7d0 (vtable 0x00454a00), wired by 0x0026fbd0(record, chassis,
#   bike+0x7c4, bike+0x7c0, bike+0x7cc, bike+0x7ac): +0xf0 = the chassis, +0xf4 = 0 (no dynamics
#   body), +0xf8 = chassis+0x254 (its transform); +0x1f0 = +0x7c4 (kind 2 rays, "%sm.col", the 11 body
#   rays; its +0x84 = word 0x00468f50), +0x1f8 = +0x7c0 (kind 5, "%sf.col", loaded 0x001ed1e0 ->
#   0x001edda0; its +0x84 = bike+0x394 | bike+0x390 | [0x00468f50] | [0x00468f68]; its +0x80 = the
#   record: the collidable other riders' rays strike), +0x1f4 = +0x7cc (the sweep probe, kind 3).
#   +0x7c8 is a second kind-2 rays object with no file; +0x7a8 the "Wreck%s" physics object.
#   Record +0x64 = chassis+0x210, +0x68 = chassis+0x214, +0x80.. = the chassis' inverse-inertia diagonal;
#   +0x110 (actors) = bike+0x390 | [0x00468f50]; +0x114 (collides-with) left 0xffffffff. The two mask
#   words are 0 in the image (set at run time; not read from a riding state yet). The constructor's
#   ignore lists (0x0011b070 / 0x0011add0 / 0x0011ade0 / 0x0011b080) keep a bike's own collidables off
#   each other; the ghost adds the vehicle class word 0x00468f68 to +0x7c4's and +0x7cc's (S63).
#   collision_record_collide: terrain rays, then the actor query; each actor hit leaves an own entry
#   (kind 0) and a mirrored one (kind 1) in the struck record. collision_resolve runs every substep
#   (vt +0x80, the flag = first substep), so impulses only on a substep's first pass. Both bikes' +0x1f8
#   flags meet the other's collides-with, so a bike pair is mutual and shares the impulse by mass.
#   Damping needs +0xf4: never on a chassis. Own-chassis branches (+0xf4 == 0):
#     relative_velocity: cofg_world = node.rotate(+0x90); arm = node.unplace(point) - (chassis+0x190 +
#       cofg_world); mine = node.rotate(rates+0x290 x arm) + vel+0x260.
#     spring: the kind-0 anchor = node.place(on_body); an unshared share uses chassis+0x214.
#     impulse: m1 = chassis+0x214, i1 = (+0xf0, +0x104, +0x118); the normal into the frame by an inline
#       unrotate; dv to shock +0x170 and vel +0x260, with angular dw = (arm x node.unrotate(j)) * i1 to
#       twist +0x180 and rates +0x290.
#     friction: mass = chassis+0x210.
#     apply: r = at - (chassis+0x60 + cofg_world); torque +0x140 += node.unrotate(r x f) * mask,
#       force +0x130 += f (xyz only, from the disassembly).
#   The wreck it causes is reason 7 (joust), already ported by S54 (air::hit_wreck over ride::hits_among).
