# V48 — the ecosystem: a scene's .esb trees and bushes. research/journal/*-v48-map-ecosystem has how.
ecosystem_load_esb          boot 0x0012acb0 (name, EcoDistance2D, EcoDistance3D, ShrubberyDistLimit, light) from the scene loader
ecosystem_read_header       boot 0x0012aa30 version, 256 species slots, shrubbery, probability map, then the plants
ecosystem_read_species      boot 0x0012a3d0 one slot: names, size triples, atlas rect, three words, v2 tail, solids
ecosystem_read_plants       boot 0x0012b470 9-byte plants: slot (bit 7 snap), u16 x y z times the scale, i8 height, i8 width
#   V49: bit 7 alone snaps (the test is param_3 || bit, with +0x90 the world non-null): ground_query on
#   the scaled point, then y = (u16)(int)(y * +0xd8), +0xd8 = 65536/extent. Every free06 plant has it.
ecosystem_species_models    boot 0x00129180 a species' model and billboard, scales 0.5/half.y and 1/half.x
ecosystem_frame             boot 0x0012bbd0 per frame: fov-stretched distance, fade byte, turn on entry, depth buckets
ecosystem_model_item        boot 0x001e04b0 a near plant's matrix: turned by its two shorts, scaled to width and height
scene_read_environment      boot 0x001a7b30 [Environment]: EcosystemFile, EcoDistance2D/3D (180/120), ShrubberyDistLimit (400)
#   The ecosystem object's pointer is the word at 0x0046a5f8; the camera is *(*0x0048a560).
#   Object: +0xb0 EcoDistance2D, +0xb4 EcoDistance3D, +0xb8 ShrubberyDistLimit, +0xd4 feet per position
#   unit (extent/65536, 0.10546875 on free09), +0x68 the plants (0x1c each), +0xa0 their count, +0x94 the
#   256 species pointers, +0x104 the frame counter.
#   Plant: +8/+0xa/+0xc position u16s, +0xe species slot, +0xf fade byte (0 near, 0x80 far), +0x10/+0x11 the
#   size bytes, +0x12 bit 0 "drawn flat", bit 1 a bend state at +0x80 + 0x50*[+0x13]; +0x14/+0x16 the turn
#   (cos, sin)*32767 taken from the camera's forward (+0x158, -+0x150) when the plant changes side.
#   Species: +0x100/104/108 height mean/min/max, +0x10c/110/114 width mean/min/max, +0x138/13c u0/u1,
#   +0x140/144 v top/bottom, +0x148 trunk u, +0x14c 0.5/half.y, +0x150 1/half.x, +0x184 a colour word.
#   The camera: +0x140/+0x148 eye x/z, +0x150/+0x158 forward x/z, +0x170 the fov in degrees (85 in the
#   biplane state, against the 77 the distances are tuned at).
# S77 — the put-back after a shootoff (reason 13): put_back_keep's cannonball branch, ported as
#   ride::Ride::keep_back_thrown (crates/game-sim/src/ride.rs), wired in rider.rs after the bike's whole
#   update; proved in crates/game-sim/tests/ride.rs (the_record_follows_the_wreck_bike_through_a_shootoff:
#   96 moved + 4 held over three free09 edges; each_captured_post_shootoff_put_back_lands_bit_exact_at_the_landing).
#   Captures: research/s77_edge_back.py (context/s77/free09-{east,north,west}.txt; slots 7, 6, 7).
#   Gate: +0xc84 == 13 (the request's reason, from before the tick's verdict) and *(*(+0x7a8)+0x120)+0x40
#     != 0. *(rag +0x120) is the wreck bike's collision record (part wrapper +0x8c0, vtable 0x00454a00);
#     +0x40 is its touched word (+0x48 the ground's), contact_search::Record::touching in the port.
#   The overhead test (common to both branches) stands on the part *(rag +0x124)+0x40 since +0x748 != 0.
#   Spot = chassis +0x254 (the node at bike +0x18) +0xb0 xyz, w = a stack word the function never writes (0 on every captured
#   tick); heading = that node's +0xa0 as (x, 0, z, stale) through vmul/vadda/vmadd/vrsqrt/vmulq.xyz.
#   put_back_clear(bike, &spot, &heading, bike +0x3d0, 1): the normal goes straight into the record,
#   kept or not (4 refused ticks on the west edge changed +0x3d0 alone). Kept: +0x3b0 = the spot as the
#   clear left it (no +0x13a0 raise), +0x3c0 = the heading flattened once; no normal.y > 0.8 test.
#   The throw's own tick has +0xc84 = 0 and +0x748 = 13: the riding branch on the part (not ported).
#   The touched word goes 0 for good some ticks before the clock's put-back on all three captures (the
#   captured node rose away from the ground after it), so the record freezes on the last touch, and the
#   put-back at +0x794 = 5.0995 lands on it (2137-2743 ft inside the map).
# S97 — the ecosystem's trunks (crates/game-sim/src/eco.rs; tests crates/game-sim/tests/eco.rs).
collision_tree_build boot 0x00111ee0 (nodes out, facets out, matrix out, quadword verts, int[3] tris,
#                              tri count, vert count): the engine's run-time collision-tree builder
#   Loader 0x0012acb0 (from the scene loader 0x001a5210, its 2nd argument = build solids): per species
#   0x00129180(sp, 1), then 0x00129d80(eco, sp) makes species +0x194 solids (+0x198 0x30-byte records:
#   +0 at, +0x20 kind, +0x24 radius, +0x28 height) into collidables at +0x19c. Kind 0: a mesh file
#   (0x001ed1e0); 2: 0x001f0740/0x001f08e0; 3: 0x001ea120/0x001ea230; 1: the cylinder, a 0x250-byte mesh
#   collidable (0x001ecf50) laid out on the stack and handed to 0x001ed430(mesh, verts, tris, 32, 32),
#   which copies the vertices to +0x220 (12 bytes each), counts +0x23c/+0x23e, and calls the builder
#   with nodes +0x234, facets +0x238, matrix +0x160. Then +0x1a0.. identity, +0x1d0 = vf0, +0x84 = the
#   word at 0x00468f48 (4).
#   Builder: plane 0x0013df30 per triangle (work records 0x40 bytes: +0 corners, +0x10 normal, +0x20
#   centroid, +0x34 distance), the identity through vertices and normals in place, centroid × vdiv(1,3);
#   nodes 0x24 each (+0 volume, +4 radius, +8/+0xa children, +0xc centre, +0x18 half) via 0x001e51a0 and
#   0x003eb880; facets 0x14 (+0 corners u16, +8 distance, +0xc packed normal); recursion 0x00111a00,
#   bounds 0x0010fbf0, split chooser 0x0010feb0, partition 0x0010f480.
#   Group parts: 0x00129490 moves the species' mesh to the plant's cell × eco +0xd4 at +0x1d0 (FPU
#   mul), unless plant +0x12 bit 2 (never set on free09). Free09 (recording2 state): 9 tree species
#   with one kind-1 solid each (class 2, +0x116 0x04, category 4), two bush species with none
#   — nine trees; the game keeps the file's species slot n (and a plant's species byte) at n - 1.
#   Bike: the eleven body rays (+0x7c4, flags 0x12/0x32/0x52) search placed groups; the sweep probe
#   (+0x7cc, flags 0x13) does not. put_back_solid's group rule is S75's.
# S102 — trees for the plane and the wreck bike (crates/game-sim/src/{eco,flight_search,wreck_bike}.rs;
#   tests flight_candidates.rs, flight_collision.rs, wreck_bike.rs, eco.rs).
#   Plants into the world tree at 0x0012b470, in the file's order, via the tree's vt+0x20 (key) and
#   vt+0x24 (insert); the box: centre (x, hh + y, z) × eco +0xd4, half (reach, hh, reach), reach =
#   sp +0x10c + width × (sp +0x114 - sp +0x10c) / 128, hh = (sp +0x100 + height × (sp +0x100 - sp +0x104)
#   / 128) × 0.5. Group record 0x1c bytes: +2 class 3, +8/+0xa/+0xc cell, +0xe species (file slot - 1),
#   +0x10 height, +0x11 width, +0x12 flags. free06 (biplane state): 5600 groups, every one in the
#   port's node and slot order among the 353 meshes, at the port's stood height.
#   group_part_count 0x00129460 = species +0x194; group_part 0x00129490 (skips when plant +0x12 bit 2)
#   = species +0x19c[i], scaled by the plant's width/height ratios: sphere (kind 2) +0x178/+0x17c scale
#   and +0x160 position, kind 3 +0x188/+0x18c and +0x150, mesh (kind 0/1) +0x1d0 and vt+0xac.
#   Ray against a sphere: ray_collide_pair 0x001ebe70 case 5 -> 0x00109ee0 (nodes 0x00106b10, leaf
#   0x00105e80) returns a bool and writes no contact. Kinds on the discs: MXU 1 only; MX vs. ATV 1, 2.
#   Camera: its collision object's +0xe8 search is never made; 0x001dc960 queries the terrain only.
#   Wreck bike ray probe: count +0x168 (11), segments +0x170, root +0x160 (0), nodes +0x16c (10 × 0x24,
#   as the builder's, = bike\col\wreckbikem.col's). Its nearby gather's caller not found among

# S101 — the per-plant bend blocks: dead in MX Unleashed (proof: crates/game-sim/tests/eco.rs, the
#   two tests after `mod bend`). Pool: 0x0012b990(eco, world, gatherer, count), from the scene loader
#   0x001a5210 only when env +0x528 != 0; it sets eco +0x1d88 = 1, allocates count × 0x50 at eco +0x80
#   (count at +0xe8; block ctor 0x0012bb70, not a Ghidra function: +0 0, +0x10 quad from 0x0041f740,
#   +0x20 quad from 0x00426da0, +0x30..+0x3c 0, +0x40 byte 0, +0x44 0), keeps the gatherer
#   (0x002188d0 + 0x00218980(25.0, 200.0, g, 128), g +0x38 = *(*0x0048a568 + 0x42c)) at +0x1d90, and
#   makes a radius-10 collision object at +0x1d8c (categories [0x468f88], [0x468f60]; owner eco +8).
#   Environment keys (the reader 0x001a7b30, only when EcosystemFile is not "none"): env +0x520
#   EcosystemWindStrength (default 0), +0x524 EcosystemWindGustRate (0.15, read only if the strength
#   is), +0x528 UseEcoPhysics (int/bool via 0x0014b6f0, default 0), +0x52c EcoMaxPhysicsEco (50.0,
#   read only if Use is). Wind non-zero → 0x0012b8f0 (eco +0x1d60.., a normalised direction at +0x1d70).
#   Readers: the frame pass 0x0012bbd0 (plant +0x12 bit 1 → block [+0x13]: state +0x40 < 2 scales the
#   fade byte by the angle +0x30 over 5° (near) or (angle - [0x0041f640]) over 2° (far); state 4 takes
#   the fade byte from +0x30 & 0xff; +0x10 rotates the culling half-extent) and the near-model draw
#   0x0012d030. Writers: none. Every sb at +0x40, every sb at a plant's +0x12/+0x13 in the ELF
#   (llvm-objdump scan) is the block ctor, the plant ctor 0x001293e0, read_plants (bit 0) or the frame
#   pass (bit 0), or another struct's. No .scn on the disc names any of the four keys; recording2,
#   biplane and s89-along hold eco +0x80/+0xe8/+0x1d88/+0x1d8c/+0x1d90 = 0 and no plant with bit 1.
# S86 — the riding bike's collidables (crates/game-sim/src/bike_col.rs; tests crates/game-sim/tests/bike_col.rs).
#   Loader 0x00252ff0: dir "%sCol" of "Bike\" -> "Bike\Col\"; "%sm.col"/"%sf.col" of bike +0x28c, an
#   inline string, "bike" for both split-screen players (S93's P2 rides another rider and .phy, not
#   another collision file). Both files come out of Tracks\free09_ss_2.res for the split ride.
#   +0x7c0 = f.col through 0x001ed1e0 -> 0x001edda0: an ordinary 0x250-byte mesh collidable (+0x68 0,
#     constructor 0x001ecf50), then 0x00119a40(c, 5) — that 5 is +0x116 bits (4|1 -> ENABLED), not the
#     narrow-phase kind S78's note read it as; +0x84 = bike +0x394 | +0x390 | [0x468f50] | [0x468f68],
#     +0x80 = bike +0x80 (the record's side).
#   +0x7c4 = m.col rays (kind 2), 0x00119a40(c, 2), +0x84 = [0x468f50]; +0x7c8 a second ray probe with
#     no file (+0x84 0 before the start words), +0xe8 list [0x100, bike +0x390]; +0x7cc the sweep (kind 3),
#     +0x84 = [0x468f50], +0x116 bit 0. Record +0x7d0: +0x110 = +0x390 | [0x468f50], +0x114 = ~0.
#   Lists: 0x0011add0 sets +0xd0 mode, 0x0011ade0 appends an object; 0x0011b070 sets +0xe8 mode;
#     0x0011b080 adds a category (returns if listed, else first 0 slot, else grows); 0x0011b160 zeroes
#     the first equal slot. The ghost switch 0x00250580(bike, _, on): on -> +0xb7c 2, +0x7dc = +0x7e0, and
#     0x0011b080 of [0x468f68] on +0x7c4's and +0x7cc's +0xe8 lists; off -> +0xb7c 1, +0x7dc 0, and
#     0x0011b160 of it on both (0x00250950 removes it too). So a bike that was a ghost shows [0] there
#     (s93-short/long P1 and P2) and one that never was shows an empty list (splitscreen).
#   0x0027aa70 (the bike builder, from 0x0020e0b0) ORs [0x4e2128] | [0x4e2130] into +0x7c0, +0x7c4,
#     +0x7c8's +0x84 and the wreck part's (+0x7a8)+0x128 collidable.
#   S93's three images (P1 vehicle 0x00c1f810, P2 0x012e3ad0, the same in each): [0x468f50] 8,
#     [0x468f68] 0x40, [0x4e2128] 0x8000, [0x4e2130] 0x10000; +0x390 0x2000, +0x394 0x80000;
#     +0x84: f 0x9a048, rays 0x18008, second 0x18000, sweep 8; +0xcc all ~0; +0x116 (search bit 0x10
#     dropped) 0x04/0x02/0x02/0x03; +0x60 f 4.3035 (root half diagonal, FPU), rays 4.7327 (reach).
#     Wreck part: collidable +0x84 0x9a058, object +0x110 8, +0x114 0x40000 (S69's, unchanged).
#   0x00109ee0 (the narrow-phase table's case 5, from 0x001ebe70): a ray tree against a sphere at
#     other +0x40 (r +0x170, r² +0x174): node test 0x00106b10, leaf 0x00105e80 (end within r, else the
#     closest approach along the unit segment within r and inside its length); writes only +0x80 = 1.0.
#     Not on any bike's path (no bike collidable is kind 5), so not ported.
# MD5 — collision class 1, the hierarchy (crates/game-sim/src/track.rs Hierarchy; proof in
# crates/game-sim/tests/collision_hierarchy.rs). collision_file_load reads no file-level surface word for
# it; the body is a part count (object +0x200), a word it drops, then per part a bone index (clamped to
# the owner skeleton's bone count - 1; bones flattened from the root by 0x001b3420 count / 0x001b35b0),
# the surface word from version 2 (its low byte to part +0x89) and collision_mesh_load's mesh body. A part
# whose vertex count (u16 at part +0x23c) is 0 is dropped and the next takes its slot. Parts are 0x250-byte
# mesh collidables (+0x78 the hierarchy, +0x230 the bone or 0). Callers: scene_collision_read (owner 0),
# the animated toy (owner its model's skeleton), the machine race gate init, and two others not read.
collision_hierarchy_new boot 0x001ee690 (0x220 bytes, vtable 0x00451c10) shape kind 1
collision_hierarchy_load boot 0x001efca0 (this, owner, file, version) reads the parts, then a 0x34-byte
#                        summary at +0x218: part triangles/nodes/counts/points/transforms/surface bytes
collision_hierarchy_read boot 0x001effe0 (this, file, version) the per-part loop above
collision_hierarchy_split boot 0x001eea50 (hierarchy, count*) -> an array of fresh mesh collidables, one
#                         per part, copying each part's arrays, transform and +0x230 bone
animated_toy_collision boot 0x00119070 a toy's model (0x00143970) and, with HasCollision, its .col by
#                      collision_file_load(model skeleton, name, 5); kind 1 is split, and each part gets an
#                      800-byte rigid body (0x001dd580) bound to its bone (its vtable +0x74) and added as a
#                      child node of the toy (node_add_child); the part's +0x7c is that body
animated_toy_read boot 0x002144d0 an [AnimatedToyN] section: MCFName, InitialMotion, InitialFrame,
#                 InitialTime, HasCollision, CameraCollision, AnimateWhenNotVisible, UpdateSearch, ...
# --- V51: the activities' start zones in the world, read for crates/openmx/src/portal.rs. Checked by
#     eye against PCSX2 at free09 (recording2's state: the RACE and STUNT columns from afar, the hit
#     zone's green disc and column from inside it) ---
asz_load                    boot 0x00326330 reads hit.dat's [Activity Start Zones] ASZCount and each
#                                           [ASZ n] into a 0xa0-byte zone (vtable 0x4598e0)
asz_read                    boot 0x003259b0 one [ASZ n]: Position +0x20, LookVector +0x30, Scale +0x40
#                                           (x) / +0x48 (z), Type -> +0x78 (Hit 1, Run 2, Race 3,
#                                           Stunt 4), HitSetID's place in [Progression HitSets] (Hit)
#                                           or [Run HitSets] (Run) -> +0x70 (0 Race/Stunt, -1 none),
#                                           StringID, AIDifficulty. Colours (float bits): Hit 0x6ea637,
#                                           Run 0xff9933, Race 0x3373cc, Stunt 0x813bb5 (the radar's)
asz_build_hit               boot 0x003256f0 the zone's hit object (+0x4c, vtable 0x459af0): +0x38
#                                           hitzone_start_circle with its material swapped to
#                                           hitzone_start_activity_circle (0x001a16d0), +0x3c
#                                           hitzone_landing_cylinder, +0x30 the trigger box. Vtable
#                                           +0x88 colours the circle at alpha 0.6, +0x94 the cylinder
#                                           at 0.5
asz_icon_part               boot 0x00324ff0 one icon part via 0x00325070 (model, scene add, colour
#                                           0x001a1af0, alpha 0x001a1b20): slot 0 startzone_%d (index+1,
#                                           Hit/Run), 1 startzone_hit/run/race/stunt, 2
#                                           startzone_checkmark (white), 3 startzone_disk; tint, 0.6
asz_place                   boot 0x00323220 circle at (x, 0, z) along the look vector, up (0,1,0),
#                                           scale (sx, 1, sz), model +0xc4/+0xd4 = 50.0 (draped?);
#                                           cylinder at y + 112.5 - 25, scale (sx-1, 225, sz-1)
asz_icons_frame             boot 0x00325280 per frame (from 0x003266c0, the zones walked with the
#                                           camera's +0x140 position and +0x150 facing): d the flat
#                                           distance, h = min(d/500, 1)*75 + 15; number at y+h scale
#                                           20, checkmark/disk y+h+30 scale 20, type word y+h+15 scale
#                                           40, all turned to the camera's facing
asz_show                    boot 0x00325580 flag byte: bit 1 shown, bit 4 circle/cylinder/number/word,
#                                           bits 4|2 (done) checkmark and disk

# S88 — two riders colliding in the split screen, replayed (crates/game-sim/tests/rider_rider_capture.rs;
# journal research/journal/2026-10-22-s88-rider-rider-capture). Comments only: every name is above.

#   [biplane 0x0060b1f0, 0x00c97be0, P1's record, P2's record]. collision_node_update (0x0026f800, node,
#   dt = @frame_dt) from the image of the tick before a contact lands on the contact's capture row, both
#   chassis bit for bit (centre +0x60, force +0x130, moment +0x140, shock +0x170, twist +0x180,
#   vel +0x260, rates +0x290 off the vehicle's +0x7a4), at 3403, 3414 and 5998.
#   ORDER: in all seven S93 images, and S88's 3413, the riders' resolves (0x00270dc0, a0 = record,
#   a1 = first substep) run P2 (1), P2 (0), P1 (1), P1 (0): P2's vehicle updates whole before P1's, so
#   P1 meets P2's chassis already pushed. This overturns S54's static "player order" for the vehicles;
#   the port's Sim::step_all steps the last player first. More than two players is not seen.
#   A DOWNED rider's riding record leaves the node's list (the wreck bike's body object, +0xf0 0 and
#   +0xf4 set, takes its place: 0x01333240 at 3413) and is not resolved, but its collidable stays in the
#   broad phase: at 3414 P2's own rays strike P1's frozen frame (other = P1's riding record) and the
#   mirror goes into that record. Not ported (follow-up).
#   Capture rows tear: 5998's P1 is P1 as P2's update left it (before P1's own); 3403's P1 wreck reason
#   (1, set inside the tick) shows at 3404.

# P121 — how the original's stunt pop-up ends a combo (read for the ride HUD's land/bail colours).
#   The stunt overlay's HUD listener is booted at boot 0x002a6940 and ticked by boot 0x002a5ba0; the
#   trick line's update is boot 0x002a54f0 and its text setter boot 0x002a5660 (formats at 0x436b20,
#   0x436b88, 0x436ba8). A combo's end reaches it as event 2 (landed) or 3 (bailed), and both do the
#   same thing: set the Stunt movie's "Mode" variable (string at 0x435cc0) to 1 and play it, which
#   pops the orange text out and away. Neither path changes the text's colour, and no word (LANDED /
#   BAILED) is drawn. A landed combo of two or more first shows "Stunt Combo Total" (0x436bb8 /
#   0x436bc0) with the banked total. ELF vaddr = file offset - 0x80 + 0x100000 for these.
#   Filmed since (P121 journal entry 2, recording2 under Video Capture): a land, a two-trick land and a
#   bail all stay orange; the end shrinks and fades in ~0.25 s, and the combo total stands ~1 s.

# S111 — the riding request's writer, every field (proved in crates/game-sim/tests/ragdoll_riding.rs:
# the_riding_request_is_the_originals_from_each_row, 1203 of S109's run1 rows, and

# (rider+0xb84)) copies the state pack's snapshot (0x00258d50), which runs after this tick's physics
# and before the animation header. Request offset = rider offset - 0xb80:
#   +0x10 rows: node *(rider+0x18) world matrix via 0x001b2a50 (4 quads, the 4th its position)
#   +0x50 frame: node *(rider+0x964) world matrix, the bike model's "Bike" root (child of +0x18's)
#   +0x90 velocity = +0x840 = chassis +0x260; +0x150 = +0x560 when the reason is 7, else the same
#   +0xa0 rates = transform_unrotate(node +0x964, +0x850), +0x850 = chassis +0x280 (world rates)
#   +0xf8 = !(+0x93c) = !(+0x574); +0xfc = +0x940 = +0x4f4 (the backflip torque flag: the actor
#     latches Posed on it); +0x100 = +0x944 = +0xa44 (no-hander); +0x104 = +0x868 = +0x748
#   +0x16c = +0x648 (the reverse command: the pad read sets it, gas and brake held below speed 5 — P72)
#   +0x170 pitch = +0x898 = +0x9e8 (pose axis) and +0x174 = +0x89c = +0x9ec (fore-aft), both packed
#     before the header: last tick's, except on a held tick (+0x3a0), where stunt_idle's tail has
#     written 0.5/0.5 first (0x00258cc0)
#   +0x178 speed = +0x8c8 = +0x438, the contact velocity's z (bike_air_state), not |velocity|

# P85 — the shootoff's sound and the music fade. The player bike's builder (0x00280fb0) makes a sound

# [Cannonball] (0x001fe8b0): Cannon = SFX\cannonball_stereo (streamed, stereo, one-shot, 8.0 s at 22050)
# and Rumble = SFX\RumbleMono (looping mono, 1.5 s); +0x34/+0x38 the two sound objects (vtable
# 0x0044ed00: vt +0x1c play, +0x20 state, +0x28 stop, +0x70 volume 0..100 clamped, default 100), +0x40
# the reason it saw last, +0x44 -> the bike's +0x748, +0x48 -> the chassis' cofg (+0x7a4 -> +0x60),
# +0x4c the bike's +0x570 edge gate. Neither sound is placed: no 3D call is made on either.
shootoff_sound_update boot 0x001fea70 (child) runs among the bike's children, after its physics and
#                                     before the ride state machine, so it reads last tick's reason.
#                                     Reason 13 newly seen: Rumble stopped, Cannon played, the music
#                                     stream's pause set (0x001686b0, music manager +0x400 -> +0x30).
#                                     Reason 0: newly back from 13, the music's volumes set to the
#                                     options' own (0x001fdf90); then the edge query (0x001a6e50) at
#                                     350 ft on the cofg: d = the nearest edge's distance. Within it,
#                                     Rumble started if idle, its volume (int)((350-d)/200*75)+25, and
#                                     the songs' volumes (int)(v*(1-(350-d)/200)) of the options' v
#                                     (music for songs 0..12, ripped music past), negative let through.
#                                     Outside it, Rumble stopped and the music's pause cleared; the
#                                     volume is left where the last tick inside put it. Both music
#                                     calls gated on the game's +0x570.
music_set_volumes boot 0x001fdf90 (music manager, music 0..100, ripped 0..100) jukebox +0x44: song k's
#                                 volume float at (+0x64)+k*0x3c+0x30 = v/100
stream_pause boot 0x001686b0 (stream, on, a2) +0x40 = on, IOP command 0x17 when it changes; vt +0x18
#                            sets it, vt +0x20 clears it; the pause page (0x00207770) sets every stream's
# Live (context/capture/p85, watch/p85-edge-sound.toml): the pause flag the throw sets is clear again the
# next tick (writer not found); the song stays at volume 0 through the cannonball and is back at the
# options' volume the tick after the put-back. Video Capture: the boom is heard ~6 ticks after the
# rumble stops (the stream's start).
# S58 — the bike's slip. Four things the port had wrong, each found by replaying a save-state capture
# (journal 2026-10-21-s58-tyre-slip, cases launch/turn/brake):
#   bike_input_limiter 0x00251a50: the gas and the brake rise at their rates and drop to the lever at
#   once (a released lever is 0 the same tick); the clutch is rate-limited both ways; bike +0x654 is
#   written from +0x650. The port walked every lever both ways, so a released brake kept braking.
#   bike_ground_step 0x00257a00: the steer write front+0x8ac = front+0x7c * bike+0x670 * scale(+0x438)
#   happens once, before the substep loop. Inside it, after both tire_forces calls, the ground step
#   sets rear+0xc4 = (front+0xdc && !rear+0xdc) before both wheel_spin_integrate calls.
#   wheel_spin_integrate 0x0025c530, the +0xc4 branch (flag set and the wheel's own +0xdc == 0): the
#   load point +0x140 = the probe end +0x160, the normal load +0xf8 = +0xc8 * (+0xcc)->+0xf8, +0x1f0
#   and +0x1f4 copied from the other wheel, the contact velocity redone (0x0025b710), then the contact
#   frame and the tire's drive force: an airborne rear still drives and brakes on the front's load.
#   In the save state both wheels' +0xc4 = 0, +0xc8 = 1.0, and +0xcc points at the other wheel.
#   bike_air_state 0x00251d20: bike +0x430 = front+0x190 when the front touches, else rear+0x190 when
#   the rear does, else chassis+0x270; +0x438 (its z) is the "speed" the steering fade, the lean and
#   the other mv readers take. The save state's +0x430 equals front+0x190 bit for bit; +0x190 is the
#   chassis' velocity less the ground's, in the chassis' frame (not +0x170, the contact's).
# S106 — the brake held on a steep face (journal 2026-10-24-s106-brake-slope-slide). The original's
#   bike does not slide on a 46-53 degree free09 face with the brake held: from standing (nose down,
#   side on) and after braking onto it at 25 ft/s, both wheels lock, it stops within ~20-50 ticks and
#   then creeps at 0.02-0.04 ft/s for as long as the brake is held; let go, it rolls away. The brake's
#   torque fades to 0 below 3.3 ft/s (surface_drag's clamp(0.3 * +0x190.z)), so what holds the bike
#   is the tyres' grip (MaxDriveForce 2.0/2.2 x the load, more than tan 53), not the brake.
#   The engine's words a save state carries, off rider+0x7b8: gear +0x68, ratio +0x80, inverse ratio
#   +0x84, the clutch's launch scale +0x88, its lock +0x64, rpm +0xa8, the curve cursor +0x90/+0x94.
#   A state saved with the clutch slipping in another gear drove a braked rear into a spin at tick 1
#   until these were seeded. The levers a state carries: rider +0x650 brake (+0x654 its rear copy),
#   +0x658 gas, +0x65c clutch, +0x628/+0x62c the squared stick, +0x670/+0x674 the lean.

# P72 — the original's reverse, seen whole (crates/game-sim/src/ride.rs Controls::reverse_rule;
# tests ride.rs gas_and_brake_held_slow_back_the_bike_up, reverse_clips.rs, replay.rs S68)
#   The tail of pad_to_gesture (boot 0x00250fb0), not wrecked (+0x748 == 0): brake +0x640 <= 0 or
#   gas +0x63c <= 0 -> +0x648 = 0; else speed +0x438 >= 5 -> +0x648 = 0, gas = (+0x57c != 0), brake
#   kept; else +0x648 = 1, gas = brake = 0. The wreck branch zeroes the levers and leaves +0x648.
#   The push (bike_ground_step 0x00257a00): +0x548 x (+0x544 - +0x438), rear wheel down and +0x648
#   != 0; +0x544 is the .phy's ReverseSpeed x 1.46666, and every disc bike says -10.
#   Captured (watch/p72-reverse.toml, free09 cr125 from first_recording, quarter speed, Cross+Square
#   from a standstill): +0x648 rises on the row the levers do and gas/brake read 0 while it is up;
#   the body anim (type-4 actor - 0x400) wants and runs +0x8f0 = +0x8f4 = 34 (REVERSE_R, bars
#   centred) from the next row, the clock +0xa3c winds at about 0.03 x |speed| a second and wraps;
#   the bike settles at -13.85 ft/s; on release +0x648 = 0 and the state is back to 30 a tick later.
# P74 — the bike's particles (journal 2026-10-25-p74-bike-particles). The list is the track's

bike_fx_wire          boot 0x0027adf0 points the bike manager's (+0xf64) tire records at the snapshot:

vehicle_fx_spray_ctor boot 0x003d6710 a Spray entry (vtable 0x0045e7b0): SideForceScale 10, threshold 3,
#                                    MinY 1, MaxY 2, ramp 0
vehicle_fx_spray_init boot 0x003d67d0 reads SideForceScale, SideForceThreshold, Min/MaxYVelocity and the
#                                    speed ramp, the emitter and its Zone1..16
vehicle_fx_spray_zone boot 0x003d6a50 zone 0xff -> vt +0x1c (zone off), else the zone's group (as 0x003d7000)

#                                    clock -= dt, rate = file x clock / 0.25, off at 0; else vx = -scale x

vehicle_fx_generic_zone boot 0x003d7000 the Generic/Exhaust entries' zone change, as the spray's

vehicle_fx_entry_on   boot 0x003d86d0 flag |= 1 (live); the emitter on (vt +0x98) unless zone-off (flag 2)

# S81 — the path cars in the simulation: crates/game-sim/src/traffic.rs, proved in
# crates/game-sim/tests/traffic.rs against s89-along (free06, the train and the earth movers).
#   The car (V41's class, ctor 0x00114560): +0x430 path {n, keys (0x30 each: t, len, pos at +0x10,
#   normal at +0x20), clock +0xc}; +0x450 last point, +0x460 point, +0x470 position, +0x480 heading,
#   +0x4a0 up, +0x4e4 "take the next heading and up unfiltered", +0x4f4 the curve's cursor, +0x534
#   TimeScale, +0x558 LagDistance, +0x55c HeadingFilter, +0x560 NormalFilter, +0x254 the model node
#   (world matrix at node +0x80), +0x428 the mesh collidable (category 0x48, its sphere at +0x40/+0x60,
#   world matrix at +0x120, the previous tick's at +0x1e0).
path_car_update       boot 0x00115540 (a0 car, f12 dt) clock = fmodf(clock + dt TimeScale, last t) (twice
#                                    if still past); point = curve(clock or lag walk); heading = filtered
#                                    flat step; ground_query under it; up blended if cos > 0.707; basis
path_car_measure      boot 0x00115330 each segment's arc: ten chords of the curve, sqrt.s summed
path_car_lag          boot 0x00116150 the clip time LagDistance of arc behind s (walks keys back, wraps)
path_car_curve        boot 0x00116410 Hermite on the cursor's segment, Catmull-Rom tangents (chord at ends)
path_car_vtable       boot 0x0044d370 the path car's vtable, at +8 of the car (scan a RAM image for it to find the cars)
# S127 — the HasPhysics cars (journal 2026-10-27-s127-physics-cars; tests crates/game-sim/tests/physics_cars.rs).
#   The .toy's car reader reads [Cars] NumberOfCars and Car1..CarN only (free06's [Car24] is never read,
#   so free06 has two: Car22 ctruck_03, Car23 ctruck_02; free09 none). Keys and defaults: TimeScale 1,
#   HeadingFilter 0.5, NormalFilter 0.2, LagDistance 0, PathIsLevel 0, SteerFrontTires 1,
#   DriveOnSoultree 1, CollidesWithOthers 1, PresampleTerrain 0, HasPhysics 0, UseBackfaceCull 0,
#   LightingType. StopAnimationWithPhysics is not a key the reader asks for: the init's last stack
#   argument (car +0x590, "stop the clock while physical") is a literal 0.
#   HasPhysics 0: a 0x580-byte path car into the scene's update list. 1: a 0x6a0-byte subclass into
#   the toy world's list (reader +0xc -> +0x3c). Subclass fields: +0x580/+0x584 two interfaces
#   (struck listener, world-node listener), +0x588 physical, +0x58c physical last update, +0x590 the
#   stop flag, +0x594 the .pco path ("%s%s.pco", "", model name without extension, so
#   "Tracks\Free06\ctruck.pco" in s89-along), +0x698 a physics object (physics_object_ctor, 0x1e0).
#   The physics object (vt +0x38 0x0044e760): +0x110 its body (+0 enabled), +0x124 the m.col ray probe,
#   +0x128 the f.col mesh, +0x130 = +0x124, +0x134 the world node (0x0060b200 in s89-along), +0x13c the
#   car's model node, +0x1bc the reset countdown (-1), +0x1c0 ResetTime (10 from the .pco).
physics_car_ctor      boot 0x003e3570 the path car ctor, then the subclass's vtables and fields
physics_car_vtable    boot 0x0045f070 the subclass's vtable: the path car's but for +0x8, +0x38 update, +0x6c,
#                                    +0xf8 init; shares vt+0xb4..+0xc0 (velocity, spin) with the path car
physics_car_init      boot 0x003e3680 vt+0xf8: the path car's init (0x001147c0) with the same arguments, then
#                                    the physics object, the .pco read (physics_car_read_pco), body off,
#                                    the struck listener (+0x580) on the car's mesh (+0x428) and on the
#                                    object's ray probe, and the ignore pairs between the car's
#                                    collidables (+0x424, +0x428, +0x42c) and the object's (0x0011ade0)
physics_car_read_pco  boot 0x003e3920 opens +0x594 and hands it to the physics object's vt+0xe8 (the .phy reader)
physics_car_update    boot 0x003e3990 vt+0x38 (f12 dt, a0 car): with +0x588 0, path_car_update and, when
#                                    +0x58c, +0x2e0 = +0x2a0. With +0x588 1: the path clock goes on
#                                    (fmodf, twice, unless +0x590 or no path), the probe's search
#                                    gathered when the body is off and +0x130 set, then the kinematic
#                                    update (0x001dd9b0). +0x58c = +0x588
physics_car_wake      boot 0x003e3b00 (car) struck: unless +0x588, body on (vt+0x11c), +0x588 = 1, the car's
#                                    meshes +0x428/+0x42c stood down (their +0x10 interface vt+0x18),
#                                    body placed off the model node, velocity +0x70 = vt+0xb8, rates
#                                    +0x1b0 = vt+0xbc, +0x584 onto the world node's listeners (0x0026f7d0)
physics_car_settle    boot 0x003e3c40 (car, f12 dt) the world node's listener: body on -> when vt+0x118
#                                    (at rest) holds, body off, countdown = ResetTime; body off and the
#                                    countdown > 0 -> it runs down; at <= 0 the body is put away
#                                    (vt+0x120), +0x588 = 0, the meshes stood up (vt+0x20), answers 1
#   Interface thunks: +0x580 vt+8 0x003e3dd0 (-> vt+0xfc -> wake), vt+0xc 0x003e3dc0 (wake): the
#   listener calls a search makes on what it struck (collide_against_search). +0x584 vt+8 0x003e3de0.

# S169 — the .toy's HasPhysics things, and what their collidables name (journal 2026-10-28-s169-physics-props;
#   tests crates/game-sim/tests/prop_under.rs). On the disc only [CarN] sections carry HasPhysics (free06,
#   free07, free09 Car7, free10, free11 and their _ss twins); the other rigid-body props are the
#   [PhysicsCollections] (S80-S198: bales, banners, crates, barrels, cans, signs, BeachBall, EyeBall).
#   A HasPhysics car's mesh (+0x428): +0x7c = the car (a vehicle, kind 2), +0x80 = 0 (S127) -> a Moving.
#   A collection prop's f.col (collision object +0x1f8): +0x7c = 0, +0x80 = the collision object
#   (physics object +0x120), whose +0xf4 = the object's body (+0x110) and +0xf0 (chassis) = 0, on all
#   62 free09 props in first_recording. So no mover: a wheel on a prop takes wheel_contact_velocity's
#   owner branch (+0x1f0 = the object) on a dynamics body. Run in the interpreter on each free09 prop
#   with a generated body pose, velocity and spin: +0xa0, +0x1b0, +0x1c0, +0x170, +0x180, +0x190 bit for
#   bit with prop_world Collection::under.
# S128 — the path cars' ground switches: crates/game-sim/src/traffic.rs (Spec::presample, Level,
# Spec::curve_kept, Spec::lagged), proved in crates/game-sim/tests/presample.rs on recording2's state
# of free09 (six cars: free_trailercab x3, free_trailerC/A/D, at 0x01241f40 0x01252a20 0x01263570
# 0x01268160 0x01274cc0 0x012798e0) and tests/two_p_truck.rs on the s159 images of free09_ss.
#   The init (vt+0xc4, 0x001147c0) copies the .toy reader's (0x00289710) switches: +0x4d0 PathIsLevel
#   (key 0x00434258, default FALSE), +0x4e8 DriveOnSoultree (0x00434280, default TRUE: every car on the
#   disc sets FALSE), +0x4ec PresampleTerrain (0x004342b0), +0x4d8 SteerFrontTires. Every free09 car:
#   +0x4ec 1, +0x4d0 0, +0x4e8 0, +0x420 (the clip loops) 1, ground query object +0x56c.
#   The ctor leaves +0x4dc (first frame done) 0, +0x4fc (tangents dirty) 1, the cursor 0, +0x568 (the
#   level's last height) 0xff7fffff, +0x4d4 (level held) 0.
#   path_car_update, after the clock, on the first frame (+0x4dc == 0): the soultree segment set up
#   (0x001eab40, half length +0x54c = 5.0); with +0x4ec, ground_query (0x001dc960) on every key's
#   position (+0x10) and normal (+0x20) in place. The lengths stay the measure's, made on the clip's
#   keys at load (vt+0xd0 0x00115330, which leaves the cursor on segment n-2 and its tangents cached).
#   Curve (vt+0xd8 0x00116410): the cursor's keys are pointers +0x500/+0x504, moved only with the
#   cursor; the tangents +0x510/+0x520 are made again only when +0x4fc is set (by a cursor move), so a
#   car whose first point is on segment n-2 rides the raw keys' tangents. +0x538 the segment's t.
#   Lag (vt+0xd4 0x00116150): walking back it tests s against the time of the key the cursor started
#   on (the $7 of its entry), not the reached key's, so a step back goes to key 0 and the arc covered
#   is segment 0's extrapolated; then back by whole lengths, wrapping past key 0 onto key n-1.
#   Presample: height = the curve point's y; up = key i's and key j's normals lane by lane on the FPU
#   at +0x538 (t <= 0: i's; t < 1: a + t (b - a), sub.s mul.s add.s; else j's); its w is stack.
#   PathIsLevel (no presample), not held: ground query (skipped when +0x420 is 0 and the path is
#   level), path +0x10 = its y; equal to +0x568: +0x4d4 = 1, else +0x568 = y. Held: y = path +0x10,
#   up (0,1,0,1). A level car's basis takes the raw up with no orthogonalising and keeps no filtered up.
#   DriveOnSoultree: a vertical segment ray through the collidable's move virtual and probe_collide_world
#   (0x0011a150), the hit taken if higher. Not ported.

#   vt+0xb8; local = unpoint(car node, +0x120 the contact point) (0x001b3180); +0x1c0 = rotate(node,

#   classes: the reader 0x00289710 news and inits each car as it reads it (path car vt+0xc4, the
#   subclass vt+0xf8, which runs the path car's init first), and the path car's init 0x001147c0 adds

#   read order whatever the class. The subclass's physics object adds nothing to the list (slot 23 is
#   the plane's right after Car23). The optional second collidable (+0x42c, also added in the init) is
#   absent on free06's cars. free11's .toy interleaves them (PPP....P....): read-order there too.

emitter_paused_by    boot 0x0041f770 the pointer the emit gate reads its pause byte through (+0x325 bit 3)
emitter_on           boot 0x0014e490 plain emitter vt +0x98: if +0x240 bit 2 (on) is clear, set it,
#                                    idle +0x1d8 = 0, set bit 1 (just on). No timer touched.
emitter_off          boot 0x0014e4f0 vt +0x9c: clear bits 2 and 3 (emitting), prev +0x60 = pos +0x40,
#                                    idle = 0.
emitter_reset        boot 0x0014e540 vt +0xa0: +0x274 delay = +0x278 DelayTime; mode 1: +0x27c left =
#                                    +0x284, clear bit 0 (shot made); 2: left = +0x280 + (+0x284 -
#                                    +0x280) * (rand * 2^-31); 3: left = +0x284.
#   emt_read 0x0014e910: EmitMode (int, 0) +0x270, DelayTime (0) +0x278 and +0x274; then by mode only:
#   1 OneShotTime +0x284; 2 RandomMinTimeOn +0x280, RandomMaxTimeOn +0x284, RandomMinTimeOff +0x288,
#   RandomMaxTimeOff +0x28c (left drawn as the reset does); 3 TimedTimeOn +0x284, TimedTimeOff +0x28c;
#   left = +0x284 for 1 and 3 (1 also clears bit 0). All default 0.
#   emit_timing returns 1 = nothing: pause byte (*0x0041f770)+0x325 bit 3 -> 1; a node (+0xa4) moves
#   prev to pos and pos onto it; bit 1 -> clear it, prev = +0x50 = pos, 1; delay > 0 -> on: delay -=
#   dt; 1 (prev kept); view cull (+0x248 && *0x0041f788) -> 1; mode 1: off -> idle += dt, prev = pos,
#   clear bit 3, 1; left > 0 -> left -= dt, 0; bit 0 -> vt +0x9c off, 1; else generic. Modes 2/3,
#   left > 0: left -= dt; on: left > 0 -> 0, else left = off time (2: +0x288..+0x28c drawn; 3: +0x28c),
#   off, 1; off: left > 0 -> idle += dt, prev = pos, clear bit 3, 1, else left = on time (2: +0x280..
#   +0x284; 3: +0x284), vt +0x98 on, idle = 0, 0. Left <= 0 -> generic: on -> set bit 3, 0; off ->
#   idle += dt, prev = pos, clear bit 3, 1. emit 0x00150540 at 0x0015084c: a frame's count > 0 in
#   mode 1 sets bit 0. Disc: only the fireworks_*.emt (delays 0.75..2.8, shots 0.1..3) and nat12's
#   geyser blast/mist/spray (delay 0, shot 15) are not mode 0 / delay 0; no mode 2 or 3 anywhere.
#   Trigger particles action (vtable 0x00459f08): read 0x003322f0 EmitterName, TurnOn (1); fire
#   0x003323a0: TurnOn -> emitter toy's emitter vt +0xa0 reset then +0x98 on, else +0x9c off.
#   Toy trigger contact 0x001c6190: +0x31c bit 5 = contact this frame; OneTimeOnly (bit 3) and fired
#   (bit 4) -> none; percent != 100: bit 6 (contact last frame) -> none, else percent < int(rand *
#   2^-31 * 100) + 1 -> none; fired, count +0x100 ++, actions 0x001c7560 (an actor already in contact
#   is skipped; TriggerActionDelayTimer counts down in update 0x001c7110, which moves bit 5 to 6).
#   Emitter toy: on/off message 0x003321b0 (vt +0x80; 0 off, else on, no reset); reset 0x003320f0
#   (emitter reset, then on/off by StartOn); toy link 0x00332030 (AttachToToy's object by name ->

#   Race: load 0x002faa50 lists "FireworksAnimator%02d" (+0x3f8) and "Fireworks%02d" (+0x3e0) from 1
#   until a name is missing; finish 0x002fd460 (from 0x002fc410: game mode +4 == 2 and the finisher's

# The state's .toy actor sits at 0x011b43e0, its collection list {count 62, head} at 0x011b43f4;
# cells {data, +4, +8 next made}. Node: +0x40 local rows, +0x80 world rows. Body: +0xc mass, +0x10
# 1/m, +0x20 damping, +0x50 position, +0x120 inverse inertia, +0x130 angular damping, +0x150 turn,
# +0x160 start turn. Every prop node in this state carries negative zeros (posed by its world at
# rest), and twelve rotated ones drift an ulp or two off the scene's frame: the bodies were made off
# already-posed nodes (a second load?). SH_CrateC model 529's start turn is an ulp off the port's.

# S130 — the props' tick (crates/game-sim/src/prop_world.rs; journal 2026-10-28-s130-props-tick).
# tests/prop_world.rs runs free09's Sign_SpeedLmt through wake, swing, sleep, countdown and put-back
# against the first_recording state in the interpreter, and node_bounds over every scene node with
# children. Collection vtable 0x0045da90; the wrapper's 0x0044e760.
# update 0x003b28a0: paused -> nothing; dynamics_world_step(coll +0x250) then the node's children.
# activate 0x003b27a0: +0x20 |= 2, files coll +0x34 on the node's listener list (node +0x48 {count,
# cap, +8 items}) via 0x0026f7d0; with PinToBody (+0x3c) every body enabled and +0x338 = -1.

#                                    asleep -> ResetTime (+0x33c) < 0 deactivates, else counts +0x338
#                                    down by dt, at <= 0 resets every body and deactivates

#                                    collection +0x270 / +0x2b8 (pins +0x288 / +0x2d0). Ported by S196.

#                                    2 * zeta * sqrt(I * k)
torsion_spring_force  boot 0x001f3180 the pull back through quat_axis_angle
# Constraint read (0x003b15b0) beyond S80: degrees x 0x3c8efa34; limits only when min <= max in every
# lane (pin +0x8c = 0, lower/upper = curve_sine(angle * 0.5), +0x70 ConstrainAngles); the Pin's world
# point = orientation_transform_point of the node's world rows. The pin's vtable 0x00451df0 +0x18 is
# ragdoll pin_solve (0x001f49b0).
physics_object_edge_check boot 0x0015b1a0 vt +0x10c, from post: with +0x70 set and a bounds pointer
#                                    (+0x100), inside iff min.x (+0x30) < x < max.x (+0x40) and
#                                    min.z (+0x38) < z < max.z (+0x48); outside -> disable + reset;
#                                    inside -> transform_set_pose
physics_object_at_rest boot 0x0015b400 +0x1b0 fresh -> no; else |vel| < +0x1c4, |rates| < +0x1c8,
#                                    |accel| < +0x1cc, |ang accel| < +0x1d0, |shock| < +0x1d4,
#                                    |twist| < +0x1d8
physics_object_disable boot 0x0015b8c0 vt +0x128
physics_object_place  boot 0x0015a1b0 vt +0xe4 (wrapper, place, look, up): side = look x up, up
#                                    re-crossed, both normalised by rsqrt; start pos/turn, body reset,
#                                    node pose; physics_object_reset (0x0015a030) calls it on
#                                    +0x90/+0xa0/+0xb0
#                                    S205: side = opmsub(opmula(look, up), up, look), up =
#                                    opmsub(opmula(side, look), look, side) (.xyz: the w lanes are the
#                                    look's), both rsqrt-normalised and written back through the
#                                    pointers; pos.w = 1.0; axes_to_quat on them -> sp+128. The nose is
#                                    kept: a slope along it is taken off the up, one across it rolls it
physics_object_struck boot 0x0015ad40 like 0x0015acf0: wakes the wrapper through vt +0x124
# The wrapper's listener (0x0015b2a0, filed at +0x50) counts its own ResetTime down the same way.
node_bounds_merge     boot 0x001b42a0 (child, &any, lo, hi, parent rows, 1): rows x parent's rigid
#                                    inverse, centre and |M0 h.x| + |M1 h.y| + |M2 h.z| into lo/hi
#                                    (vmini/vmax xyzw; lo/hi w start from M0.w/M1.w, two zeros order
#                                    -0 under +0), then its children. node_bounds' own corner and
#                                    final sums are .xyz: centre.w = lo.w, half.w = hi.w.

# S196 — the props' BodyTorsionSpring and a collection's own ResetTime (prop_world::Spring;

# only): s196-sx01.ee.bin (S133 boot.py sx01 0 1, copy 2 slot 21) and s196-free11.ee.bin (boot.py
# free11 2 5, slot 22), both 27 collections, every prop asleep.
# Spring layout: +0 on (1), +4 id, +8 vtable 0x00451ea0, +0x10 stiffness, +0x20 damping (ctor
# (0,0,0,1)), +0x30 body (0x001f3050). Vtable +0xc = 0x001f3180, the force listener: the reader files
# the spring itself on the collection world's force list (coll +0x2b8 = world +0x68) at load, so it
# runs ahead of every object's force listener (filed on enable). Asleep, a banner's force list is just
# its spring. coll +0x270 is NOT a spring list: sx01's bales hold 5..0x1e there with no spring.

#                                    turn +0x160, rel(quat +0x170, start)); (angle, axis) =
#                                    quat_axis_angle 0x0013f110; spin = rates(+0x1b0).axis by FPU
#                                    mula/madda/madd; sum.xyz = stiffness*angle + damping*spin
#                                    (w = stiffness.w); torque = -sum.xyz * axis.xyz into body vt
#                                    (+0x100) +0x5c = 0x001f3360: +0x190 += v.xyz, then the body's
#                                    listeners (+0xd0, none on a prop)
# sx01..sx24 all carry banner.pco: one part, Pin with ConstrainAngles +-70 x, BodyTorsionSpring
# Spring 15,0,0 Damping 0,0,0 (stiffness x = 63.884674 off the inertia). banner_09 (0x00f2e550 in the
# sx01 state) swung at 8 rad/s about x reaches 69.9 deg, sleeps at frame 99, put back at 250.
# PinToBody: no .pco on the disc has one. A [PhysicsParts] ResetTime (coll +0x33c) is only on free11's
# (and free11_ss's) bowlingpin (one collection, ten bodies, 0x00d08680 in the state),
# buggysparetirerear and buggysparetirefrnt, all 5.0; every other collection holds -1.

# V75 — a bird trigger's shape and who strikes it (creature.rs Trigger::struck; journal
#   2026-10-28-v75-bird-trigger-shape). The box class: ctor 0x001e79d0, vtable 0x00451850, kind 6;
#   set 0x001e7b10 stores centre +0x160 (the zero vector 0x00426d70) and Box +0x170 as they are read.
#   AABB 0x001e8050: centre M.c, extent |R|.h; bounding radius 0x001e7f70 = |h|; box-vs-box
#   0x00109330 (c, h, M). So Box is half extents about Position. The trigger reader's basis call is
#   0x0013e330(trigger +0x90, Position, LookVector, UpVector, 0, 1): no squaring of the two.
#   The shape's category +0x84 = the word at 0x00468f78 (0x100 in every state) — the category the
#   bike's second ray probe ignores. Contact only through the collision pass 0x0011a150 / 0x0011a9d0:
#   mover vt+0x94 pair test, then the struck one's +0x110 (0x001c6190). Pair tests against kind 6:
#   the ray class 0x001ebe70 case 6 -> 0x0010a9b0 (rays into box space, tree 0x0010a890, node
#   0x00106000, leaf below); mesh class 0x001ed740 -> 0x001ed910 -> 0x0010a6d0; the sphere class
#   0x001e9540, kind-5 class 0x001f1790 and hierarchy 0x0011a140 have no kind 6 (0). So of a riding
#   bike only the body rays (record +0x1f0, bikem.col, queried by 0x002708a0) strike it: the sweep
#   0x00270390 and the camera 0x001fa4c0 are spheres, the second probe (0x00257820) ignores 0x100.
#   Trigger update 0x001c7110 only exit-tests (0x001e7bc0) actors already in its +0xe0 list.
#   No save state holds a trigger box (callback 0x001c6190 scanned in every .p2s RAM image here).

#                                    the four rows (origin first), less centre; mid/half-length apart

# V104 — the toy trigger's contact, actions and update (creature.rs Trigger / Creatures::step_among;
#   journal 2026-11-02-v104-trigger-contact). Proved by crates/game-render/tests/trigger_contact.rs.
#   Trigger fields: +0x29 bit 0 enabled (StartEnabled); +0xd8 IgnoreTrigger bits: strstr "AI" 1,
#   "PLAYER" 2, "ANIMATIONS" 4 (strings 0x0042c6b8/c0/c8; the disc's AIANIMATION sets AI only). Its
#   only reads are the reader's three ORs: nothing consults it. +0xdc percent, +0xe0 action count,
#   +0xfc action list, +0x100 firings, +0x314 shape, +0x318 last actor, +0x324 EnterTest, +0x328
#   ExitTest. +0x31c: bit 0 exit actions on, 1 enter actions on, 2 keep a contact list (ctor sets all
#   three), 3 OneTimeOnly, 4 fired, 5 touched this frame, 6 touched last frame, 7 an UponExit override
#   nothing sets. Action base (reader 0x001c6670): +0 pending actor, +4 TriggerCounterEquals (-1), +8
#   TriggerCounterGreaterThan (-1), +0xc TriggerActionDelayTimer, +0x10 timer, +0x14
#   TriggerActionUponExit, +0x18 ActionTest, +0x1c vtable (run vt+0x14 (action, actor, trigger,
#   enter)). ScareBirds' run 0x003aea00 acts only on enter 1.
#   Contact: shape +0x78 is the trigger. Gate: the global below, enabled, EnterTest null or passing.
#   Then bit 5; one-time and fired stop; under 100 percent bit 6 stops, else the roll; then fired,
#   count + 1, actions (a listed actor runs none; the counter/delay/UponExit rules; then each added
#   to the other's list 0x0011af30, which reuses a zero slot or grows by realloc), zone hook 0x001c6f60.
#   Update (a0 trigger, f12 dt): bit 6 = bit 5, bit 5 cleared; each listed actor exit-tested by the
#   shape's kind (5: 0x001f09c0, else the one below); a miss zeroes both lists' slots (0x0011b020) and,
#   ExitTest passing, runs the UponExit actions (delayed ones take the delay of the action at the
#   leaving actor's slot index, a listing slip); then timers: t > 0 -> t - dt, at <= 0 t = 0 and run
#   (pending actor, enter 1); last node_update_children. Exit test of a kind 2 (ray) actor: its tree's
#   first node (or first segment) box against the trigger box, = ray_probe::group_reaches.
#   Tests: 0x002131f0 parses "&|!()" into combinators (eval 0x001c6450 calls each child, a null one
#   through null); a name is looked up in the manager (*0x004e18c0) +0x54 table, count +0x50, which
#   only the ctor 0x00212900 writes (0): in the free06/free07/kiowa states it is empty, so a name is
#   null and passes. No trigger or action on the disc names a test or a counter/delay/UponExit key.
#   Path cars: +0x424 (ray) runs the collision pass only under DriveOnSoultree, +0x42c ("m.col" ray)
#   exists only under CollidesWithOthers; all 126 [CarN] on the disc set both FALSE, so no retail path
#   car strikes a trigger (+0x42c null on every car in the s89 free06 state).

#   tests/back_search.rs). put_back_solid's float orders, read from its listing: the move is
#   vsub.xyz(bundle +0x40, bike +0x1390) then FPU mula x*x, madda y*y, madd z*z into bike +0x13a8; the
#   reach bike +0x13a4 is 100.0 (ten feet), set by the bike's constructor at 0x0027a6fc and never
#   written again; the list is regathered unless move <= reach (c.ole.s), so a NaN move regathers.
#   Gather kind: 1 when *(bike +0xf9c) is set and its +0x40 is 1, else 3. Every entry with distance <
#   the bundle's radius (c.olt.s) is asked, no early exit on a refusal; y is lifted only when not
#   (point0.y <= y), so the first of two equal heights keeps its normal. A group part's dx, dz are
#   cvt.s.w(u16 +8 / +0xc) mul.s the world's cell size (*(0x0046a5f8) +0xd4) sub.s the centre, then
#   mula dx*dx, madd dz*dz, c.ole.s 400. Checkpoints: the mode object *(bike +0xfa0): +0x64 == 1 and
#   +0x5c & 1, list at +0x70, next at +0x8c; each node's dx = +0x40 - centre.x, dz = +0x48 - centre.z,
#   mula/madd as above, limit = 100 + (0.25 * r) * r with r at +0x70 (add.s after the two mul.s), and
#   the first within (c.ole.s) refuses and stops the walk.
#   bundle_sweep_object's class 0 (mesh) path: the object's matrix +0x120..+0x15c inverted as
#   transpose (each row keeping its own w) with translation 0 - c0*t.x - c1*t.y - c2*t.z (vmsub chain);

# S138 — a wall and a nose landing. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-26-s138-wall-and-nose-wrecks.
# No new function. Two findings, both port-side:
# * The riding bike's collision object (bike +0x7d0; search_ground 0, search 1, sweep back 1, chassis
#   +0xf4 = 0, capacity 11, nearby) searches the world's props with its eleven body rays (bikem.col)
#   as it searches other riders' frames, and collision_record_resolve (impulses on the first substep
#   only, a spring past HARD) adds the impulse into the chassis' shock +0x170: the g peak the wreck
#   test reads. A head-on wall at 92/s: the original's row after the hit holds g (0.97, 18.24,
#   -32.88), vel (3.88, 5.98, 59.04), pitch 51.01 deg and reason 1 (FrontWreckSpeedLimit, 18.33 on
#   recording2's rider: +0x7a0.. reads 0, 18.33, 22.0, 19.07, 58.67, 66.0, 1.2217, 1.2217, 1.396,
#   1.4835, 1.4835, 29.33); the port, its rays on free09's props, the same to print. Its sweep back
#   (collision_record_sweep_back on the chassis) is still not ported.
# * bike_vehicle_update's riding branch calls 0x00133500 (obj+0x50, dt) after bike_air_state and
#   before the stunt clocks: it walks obj+0x50's children (+0x18, next +0x14; enabled: +0x29 bit 0
#   set, bits 2 and 3 clear, +0x20 & 2) and calls each one's vt+0x38 — the chassis' is
#   chassis_measure 0x0015c4c0, so +0x90 (euler) and +0x34 (speed) are taken again after the physics.
#   bike_wreck_test reads +0x90 (pitch) and +0x98 (roll) from there: a nose 70.058 deg down in the air
#   and 69.863 on its touchdown tick rides out (GroundPitchDownWreckAngle 1.2217); 74.95 in the air,
#   75.03 on touchdown wrecks reason 1 on that tick.
#   S180: that walk is the last measure of a frame. The stunt clocks then copy the node's quaternion
#   back over +0x1a0 but nothing takes the angles again, so next frame's bike_airborne_control reads
#   +0x98 (roll) as the walk measured it, not as the copy would: first_recording's flight in S106's
#   roll capture, tick 66, roll 0xbd64caed against the copy's 0xbd64cd5e (Ee, per frame,
#   tests/replay.rs the_airborne_control_reads_the_walks_roll).
# Captures (context/capture/s138, git-ignored; research/s138_hits.py, research/s138/*.sh): recording2
# (slot 3) teleported (research/s76_tele.py) about free09's farmyard. Garage shed (props 205ff): walls
# x 4612..4673, z 3817..3877, open south side with corner posts at (4670.5, 3818) and (4613.5, 3818).
#   wall   (4642.5, 3650), gas: back wall head-on at 92/s, reason 1 row 2856; states 2849..2851
#   slow   z 3858, gas: back wall at 29/s, reason 1 row 2772; states 2764..2766
#   gprobe (4628.5, 3790), scrape.pad: drifts to the back wall's west end at 50/s, reason 1 row 2816
#   rub2   (4674.2, 3780), gas: clips the south-east post 2 ft in at 46/s (x accel 79.6, yaw 0.26),
#          rides on; states 2785..2787
#   nprobe3/4/6/7 (research/s138_nose.py, idle pad): thrown up and pitched forward at (4642.5, 3650):
#          62 deg at 30/s and 66 at 44/s down ride out (g.y 26.6 / 42.7), 70.06 -> 69.86 rides out,
#          74.95 -> 75.03 reason 1 on touchdown. States 2791, 2819, 2790, 2787.
# S140 — the walk's measure writes +0x34 (speed = |+0x260|) too, after the physics. On all nine S67/S115
# RAM images the oracle's bike_vehicle_update leaves +0x34 == the port's measure of its own post-update
# +0x260, bit for bit, and != the measure of the velocity it began with; every capture row's ch_speed is
# its own row's |ch_vel|. No reader inside the frame: stunt_request, stunt_idle, stunt_clocks and
# bike_wreck_test load no +0x34 (their +0x7a4 uses touch +0x1a0, +0x260, +0x264, +0x294..+0x29c and a
# vt+0x80 call). Readers after the update: vehicle_camera_target through rider+0x860 (0x0027c5ec), the
# speedometer and the engine's sound.

# S139 — the riding chassis' sweep back on a capture. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-28-s139-post-sweep-back.
# The port is S125's (Ride::sweep_back, run at the top of the rider's tick); S139 is its proof. No new function.
# collision_record_sweep_back 0x00270390 on the riding bike's record (bike +0x7d0), chassis branch:
#   r = the node's (+0xf8) turn of the offset +0xb0 = (0, 3, 0); +0xe0 to = node position + r; +0xd0
#   from = to - vel (+0x260) * lead (+0xc0 = 2.0) * dt. The sweep probe +0x1f4 (= bike +0x7cc) searches
#   nearby; its +0x116 bit 0x20 = struck; its contact +0x6c: +0x20 push direction, +0x30 point. Facing
#   +0xc8 = 0.7074, push out +0xc4 = 2.0. On a strike: 0x001b2660 (node, 0, &pos) with pos = point +
#   push * out - r (a parentless node: 0x001b2640, the position written and marked dirty), +0xe0 =
#   point + push * out; then, when +0x1f0 (the body rays) is set, 0x00119be0 on them.
# Why only sideways: the riding chassis is clamped to 135 ft/s (4.5 ft a tick). The eleven body rays
#   all start at the hub (0, 4.06, -0.10) and reach z -3.82..3.63 on the centre line but only x +-1.31,
#   so head on a post is always met by a ray first (post, shove200, shove400, fly300: sw_flags never
#   0x20), while a bike moving 4.4 ft a tick sideways passes a wall between two ticks' rays.
# Captures (context/capture/s138, git-ignored; research/s139/sweep.toml = S138's hits.toml + the
#   record's +0xd0 / +0xe0, the probe's +0x116 and contact; research/s139/shove.sh, post.sh,
#   research/s139_shove.py writes the chassis velocity +0x260 / +0x2d0 / +0x2dc while paused). State:
#   recording2 copied into slot 14 (slot 3 of copy 2 is a career menu).
#   side1    teleport (4700, 3840), thrown (-200, 8, 0): through the shed's east wall (x 4672.88)
#            mid-wall; sweep struck rows 2472..2480, the bike put back each tick; wreck 11 row 2484.
#   sidepost teleport (4700, 3818), thrown (-200, 8, 0): the south-east corner post; struck rows
#            2473..2475, 2477..2479; wreck 6 (the head ray on scenery) row 2481.
#   post     post.sh at (4670.5, 3650), gas: head on into the south-east post at 82/s; no strike;
#            wreck 1 row 2571.
#   Phases: the port's tick N is row N + 1 for the chassis; the sweep lanes are read at the tick's top,
#   so some rows (sidepost 2474..2476, post 2567) hold the next tick's values.
#   S202: on a swept tick the body rays search from where the sweep left the bike (the port did
#   already). Sidepost's early head-ray wreck was the port's frame-end head sweep, which S152 retired:
#   the port wrecks on row 2481 since, and S139's test holds every wreck to its exact tick.

# R10 — MX vs. ATV Unleashed, PC (program `pc`, [other.pc] in kit.toml; research/mxvatv-pc.md). A
# reference, not an oracle: nothing here is called, and a proof stays against the PS2. Each row names
# the boot function it matches and how: `str` = rare strings both read, `off` = the struct offsets
# both touch (Jaccard), `const` = the float constants, `seq` = the same place in the same setter run.
# `./kit ghidra name -o pc` writes these names into the PC project; `./kit fn pc_engine_update -o pc`.
#

# R11 — the bike's per-frame virtuals, by a call-graph walk out from the rows above (`walk`: called in
# the same place, in the same order, from a function already paired), with the float constants and the
# branch shape as the check. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-28-r11-pc-per-frame-virtuals.
# The PC splits bike_vehicle_update over the physics world's listeners on MXVehicleMotocross:
# vf0_at_48 (PreProcessListener) = bike_ground_step's head, vf0_at_60 (ForceListener) = its substep
# body (no substep loop: the physics world substeps), vf0_at_52 (PostProcessListener) = bike_air_state
# with the no-hander and the ground step's tail. The bike's fields sit +0xacc..+0xd20 above the PS2's
# (+0x670 -> +0x1308, +0x6c0 -> +0x1368, +0xa4c -> +0x1518, +0x550 -> +0x1270), not one constant.

# R11 — the same functions in SLUS-21104 (MX vs. ATV Unleashed, PS2), program `atv`: the boot ELF with
# GAME_REL.BIN folded in at its load address (tools/mxvatv_overlay.py; the game's code is all in the
# overlay). It is the PC's code built for the EE: the same five listener vptrs at +0x30..+0x40 (each
# a thunk to a slot of the primary table at the +4 vptr), the same call orders, the PC's field layout
# 0x20 lower in the high fields (PC +0x1308 -> +0x12e8, +0x17d0 -> +0x17b0). Slots were placed by
# aligning the PC's MXVehicleMotocross table against the primary one by content, then checked at the
# call sites (`call`: bike_vehicle_update's +0x3a4/+0x49c/+0x4f0/+0x4f8/+0x4e8/+0x4a0 land on these).

# MD37 — the ATV, MXVehicleAtv (a MXVehicleMotoShared beside MXVehicleMotocross), on the PC and in
# SLUS-21104. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-29-md37-atvs. No MX Unleashed twin: SLUS-20767 has no ATV.
# SLUS-21104's ATV table runs parallel to the bike's, 0x2200 lower (entry 0x003ce364 = Force, 0x003ce368 =
# PreProcess, 0x003ce36c = PostProcess, against 0x003d0564/68/6c for the bike); entries equal in both are
# MotoShared's, the rest are the ATV's own, paired to the PC by the bike's twin at the same table place
# (`table`), by strings (`str`) or by call order (`seq`). Four wheels at +0x17b0/+0x17b4/+0x17b8/+0x17bc
# (PC +0x17d0..+0x17dc): left front, right front (steered), left rear, right rear (driven).

# context/capture/md37a/{race,s}-*.csv. Desert Oasis Free Ride; the game ticks at 30 Hz (vsync +2 a row).
# The player ATV: vptr 0x003ce250 at +4 (the object is the hit - 4), 0x015e0690 in the atv_ (Race) states,
# 0x015e0b90 in the atv_s_ ones; a second, idle ATV sits at 0x01657090 / 0x016573d0. ATV-relative:
# +0xb8 rigid body (pos +0x60, velocity +0x80, quat +0x170, angular velocity +0x1c0), +0x142c engine
# (+0x34 final drive: 4.558 atv_, 10.538 atv_s_), +0x310 brake (Square; reads 0.25 in a stoppie),
# +0x314 throttle (Cross), +0x12a8/+0x12ac stick, +0x12e8/+0x12ec the limited stick, +0x324 on the
# ground, +0x360 euler, +0xc38 speed, +0x6e4 1 from the first wreck on (stays 1 after the put-back).
# Wheels 0x920 apart; wheel +0xdc touching, +0xb4 the rear-pair flag, tire table +0x778..+0x7b4 (150
# atv_, 325 atv_s_). The +0x14ec trick-buttons word of the bike is not that on the ATV.
# Profile model strings: 0x00913c1c (player 1's slot) and 0x00914134; Start Event rewrites both from the
# menu's index, and the atv_s_ models are not selectable on this memory card. atv_phy_path picks atv_s_
# by strstr(model, 0x0039f4e1 "ATV_S_"); the atv_s_ states were made with that needle poked to "ATV_R_"
# (0x0039f4e5 = 'R') before loading the track, so they are the R quad's look on the atv_s_ files (A or P not checked).

# +0x17b0 LF, +0x17b4 RF, +0x17b8 LR, +0x17bc RR. Quad +0x137c/+0x1380 the front's stopped/moving spring
# rate (the stopped key is stored raw first, then the setter's +0x48 over it), +0x1384/+0x1388 the rear's;
# +0x1654 MudTime%d and +0x1694 MudAmount%d (front only, 4 per surface, no guard: a count past 16 runs
# MudTime into MudAmount). Wheel-relative: +0x34 UseParametricCurve (the flag read: default 0 written),
# +0x3c..+0x50 the suspension setter's six, +0x54/+0x58 spin inertia and 1/I, +0x5c TireInertia, +0x64

# * 0.01745329), +0x74 its negative, +0x90 the mount, +0xa0 BodyTireForcePosition (default (0,0,0,1)),
# +0xb8 OtherTireNormalLoadScale (rear, default 1), +0x100 the rest point (mount, y less the radius),
# +0x1dc the sweep shape (rebuilt as (0,strut,0,1)..(0,-radius,0,1) when set), +0x1e4 the body (+0x18

# Order: LF gets everything; RF copies LF +0x34/+0x70/+0x74/+0xa0 and gets suspension (moving), tire,
# steering, derived. LR gets no steering and no MaxSteerAngle; RR copies LR's +0x34/+0x70/+0x74/+0xa0/
# +0xb8 and gets steering from the front's locals (RAM images: LR +0x64/+0x68/+0x6c/+0x8b4 = 0, RR = the
# front's 20/0.8/1.25/200). [Rear Tire]'s NumberOfSurfaces reads into the front's stack word: missing,
# it is the front's count; the front's own missing count is an unset stack word. Donut scale: rear only
# (the front passes 0). The C library's reent (*0x00393a90): strtok keeps its cursor at +0x5c, strtod's
# Bigint freelist is +0x4c. MD37a's six RAM images hold atv_race250p.phy (atv-r-*) and
# atv_s_race250p.phy (atv-s-*), mass 13.043477 / 13.975154.

# MD65 — the quad's put-back and net unpack (quad::put_back and quad::PutBack, quad::net_unpack and
# quad::Remote; tests/atv_quad.rs the_quads_put_back_is_the_originals and
# the_quads_net_unpack_is_the_originals off MD37a's atv-r-spawn.ee.bin). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-11-02-md65-atv-wreck. The PC twins: put-back pc_quad_put_back (vf55, the same
# clauses), net unpack pc_quad_net_unpack (vf70). pc 0x005d8bb0 (vf104) is not the net unpack: it turns
# each wheel's rest point (+0x80 + +0x90) by the body's +0x180 and adds +0xbf0, then calls
# 0x004cdff0 and copies +0x140 to +0x120 (MD65b: pc_quad_settle_wheels).
# Put-back (a0 quad, a1/a2 handed to atv_bike_put_back as they came): the four wheels reset, the
# attachment +0x1480 detached; quad byte +0x45 bit 0x2 cleared (sb) across atv_bike_put_back and set
# again after (the byte read again, & 0xfd | 2) when it was set; atv_reattach(+0x1480, +0x1284, 1, 1);
# the two frame nodes +0x1104/+0x1108 read (a1 0, a2 a 64-byte frame on the stack); the hung list: count
# +0x1470 (signed), nodes +0x1474, kinds +0x1478 (0: the first frame, 1: the second, else none, through
# atv_node_set_frame: a0 node, a1 frame -> node +0x30..+0x6c). Then +0x324 = 1, +0xde0 = 0, +0x141c =
# 0.5, +0x330 = 0, +0x31c = 1, vt +0x378 (atv_net_pack); with a player (+0x680) and +0x1738 non-zero,
# player +0x94/+0x98/+0x9c = +0x1738/+0x173c/+0x1740 (the last through lwc1/swc1); then
# atv_actor_moved(quad + 0x43c). The bike's put-back (the table's 0x004d8940) is the same but resets
# two wheels. Spawn image: +0x45 = 5, +0x1470 = 7 with kinds 0 0 0 0 1 1 1, +0x1738 = 0.
# Net unpack (a0 quad, f12 dt; no vehicle-state guard): the snapshot +0xbcc -> [0] position,
# [1] quaternion, [2] velocity (pointers). body +0x80 = velocity (lq), body +0x190 = quaternion,
# quad +0xc40 = position; body +0x70 = position + body +0x1a0 turned by body +0x190 (vadd.xyz, w the
# position's); +0xbd0 = +0xc40; body +0x1d0 = 0x00396fc0 (0,0,0,1); +0x32c = |velocity| (mula/madda/
# madd, sqrt); atv_node_orientation(+0xe4, 0, body +0x190); forward = the velocity turned into the body
# (z). Per wheel, in the order LF, RF, LR, RR: +0x1d4 = forward * +0x838, +0x1d0 = adda(0, +0x1d0)
# madd(+0x1d4, dt), wrapped: less cvt.w(0.15915494 * a) whole turns (adda/msub 2pi), then > pi (c.ole
# false) less 2pi, else < -pi plus 2pi. After LF: atv_node_local(out, +0xe4, body +0xb0); the target
# 0.5 * out.x, max LF +0x74, min LF +0x70; the filter *(+0xd1c): [2] = min(dt, [1]) / [1], [0] =
# adda(0, [0]) madd([2], target - [0]); LF +0x8ac = [0], +0x12c8 = LF +0x8ac; after RF: RF +0x8ac =
# +0x12c8; LR/RR not steered. Then wheel_clear x4, atv_quad_wheel_contacts (dt), suspension x4, tire
# forces x4 (the Force body's without the integrate), +0xde4 = +0xde0, +0x324 any wheel +0xdc, +0x1270
# all, +0x1274 LF or RF, +0x1278 LR or RR, +0xde0 = !+0x324, then atv_vehicle_net_post.

# atv_quad_animate (a0 quad, f12 dt): atv_biplane_animate first (through the thunk 0x004e7bb0); body

# +0x14c0 = 3, +0x14b0 = 0.785398, +0x14b4 = +0x14b8 = 0.5, vehicle state 1; the set (+0x14f0) has stunts

# MD65c1 — the biplane's base driver as the quad runs it (quad::base_animate, quad::NetClock; Call::NetInterpolate,
# Call::NetUnpack; tests/atv_quad/animate.rs runs atv_biplane_animate and atv_biplane_net_check unhooked in both
# animate tests, 20,000 generated cases with the net clock laid). Journal: research/journal/2026-11-03-md65c1-base-animate.
# vt +0x3e8 is not "is remote": it is atv_biplane_net_check, the remote's snapshot interpolation, and it answers 1
# on every path, so the net unpack (vt +0x3f0) always follows it. PC: MXVehicleBiplane::vf67 calls vt +0x110 then
# +0x118 (dt handed to both). With +0xd04 (snapshots come, sltiu) < 5 it does nothing else. Otherwise want =
# (*(atv_net_clock) +0x4cc - +0xd10) - 0.1 (the literal 0x3dcccccd at 0x00525258; sub.s twice in that order). The
# snapshot slots are +0xcd4, +0xcdc, +0xce4, +0xcec, +0xcf4 (8 apart), each a pointer to a record whose word 0 is
# its time (newest first); records +0x10 position, +0x20 velocity, +0x30 orientation (read by vt +0x3ec). Walk:
# older = slot 1, newer = slot 0; four times (i = 0..3): stop unless want < older's time (c.olt), else newer =
# older, older = slot i + 1. Slot 1 is read twice (i = 0 reloads it), so the pairs are (1,0), (2,1), (3,2) or
# (4,3) and slot 4's time is never compared. t = (want - older) / (newer - older), max.s -0.9 (0xbf666666), min.s
# 1.9 (0x3ff33333); vt +0x3ec (a0 quad, a1 older, a2 newer, f13 t). The quad's vt +0x3ec is its own
# (atv_quad_net_interpolate, not the biplane's 0x004a87c0): ported in MD65c2 (quad::net_interpolate), see # MD65c2.
atv_biplane_net_check       atv 0x004a86f0 = vt +0x3e8 (a0 vehicle): with five snapshots, the pair round the lagged clock and vt +0x3ec; answers 1
atv_biplane_net_interpolate atv 0x004a87c0 = the biplane's vt +0x3ec (a0 vehicle, a1 older, a2 newer, f13 t; position and velocity lerped, 0x00134b90 on the orientations, clamped to the world's box less 25)
atv_quad_net_interpolate    atv 0x004e7bc0 = the quad's vt +0x3ec (a0 quad, a1 older, a2 newer, f13 t; reads a2 +0x40 first)
atv_net_clock               atv 0x0038f768 = the network clock's pointer (its +0x4cc the time atv_biplane_net_check lags)
# MD65c2 — the quad's net interpolation (quad::net_interpolate, quad::Snapshot, quad::Lag, quad::NetSmooth,
# Call::NetPutBack; tests/atv_quad/interpolate.rs, 20,000 generated cases and 2,000 on the spawned quad's own
# pointers, the biplane's half and the slerp run unhooked). Journal: research/journal/2026-11-03-md65c2-net-interpolate.
# Snapshot record (a1 older, a2 newer): +0 time, +0x10 position, +0x20 velocity, +0x30 orientation, +0x40 crossup
#   blend, +0x44 fore-aft blend, +0x48 wreck word, +0x4c stunt id, +0x50 a word (the spawned quad's +0x940).
# The snapshot the unpack reads, *(+0xbcc), nine pointers: [0] position (spawned: quad +0xc40), [1] orientation,
#   [2] velocity, [3] the velocity's change, [4] quad +0x14b4, [5] +0x14b8, [6] +0xc80, [7] +0x1528, [8] +0x940.
# A lag {+0 value, +4 time, +8 fraction}: fraction = min(dt, time) / time, value = adda(0, value)
#   madd(fraction, target - value). f12 (dt) comes through atv_biplane_net_check untouched.
# The quad's part (atv_quad_net_interpolate): newer +0x40 and older +0x40 read first; v = older's at t <= 0
#   (c.ole), newer's at !(t < 1), else adda(0, older) madd(t, newer - older); lag *(+0x1790) -> [4]; the same
#   with +0x44 and lag *(+0x178c) -> [5]; [7] = newer +0x4c, [8] = newer +0x50, [6] = newer +0x48; then jal the
#   biplane's (a0..a2, f12, f13 as they came).
# The biplane's (atv_biplane_net_interpolate): P = older + t (newer - older) on .xyz (vsub, vmulx, vadd; w the
#   newer's), V the same with the velocities, Q = atv_quat_slerp(older +0x30, newer +0x30, a3 1, f12 t), the slerp
#   math::slerp ports (shortest). World *(*(+0x3b0) +0x3b4): +0x550/+0x554 x min/max, +0x558/+0x55c z: with
#   P.x < min + 25 or !(P.x <= max - 25), P.x = min.s(max.s(P.x, min + 25), max - 25); z the same. d = |[0] -
#   P| (mula/madda/madd, sqrt). !(d <= 8): each of the lags *(+0xd14)[0..3] given P's lane as its value, and
#   *(+0xd18)[0..3] V's, a lane equal to FLT_MAX (c.eq) skipped; then !(d <= 16): +0xd40 = P (lq/sq, w
#   newer's) and vt +0x3a8 (atv_quad_put_back, a1 0, a2 0). Then the three position lags run on P (dt the
#   saved f12) -> [0] = (x, y, z, 1.0); [3] = V - [2] on .xyz (w V's); [2] = (V.xyz, 0); +0x32c = |V|; [1] = Q.
#   The velocity lags are never run. Spawned image: position lags time 0.05, velocity 0.03, the blends' 0.3,
#   area 0..5760 both ways (x min -0).
atv_quat_slerp              atv 0x00134b90 = quat_slerp's twin (a0 out, a1 a, a2 b, a3 shortest, f12 t; the same VU0 code, its table pointer 0x003ea9c0)
# MD37b (wheels) — atv_wheel_suspension and atv_wheel_tire_forces ported (crates/game-sim/src/
# atv_wheel.rs; tests/atv_wheel.rs runs both off MD37a's atv-r-spawn.ee.bin). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-10-29-md37b-atv-step. The wheel against MX Unleashed's: steered +0x2c, balancing
# +0x30, max compression +0x3c, max damper speed +0x40, extension rate +0x44, spring +0x48, damping
# out/in +0x4c/+0x50, probe +0x80, mount +0x90, +0xc0 (kept the longer of it and a zero vector: never
# changes); +0xd0..+0x180, world +0x1e0, ground normal +0x200, probe length +0x214 as MX Unleashed's;
# body +0x1e4. The body (rigid body, vtable 0x003cd200): +0x70 centre of mass (world), +0xa0 force, +0xd0
# the bottom-out's correction, +0x190 orientation, +0x1a0 centre of mass (body frame), +0x1b0 moment.
# Points to the world: turn(p - body +0x1a0) by +0x190, plus +0x70. The ray: tolerance a literal
# 0x3f7f069e (cos 5 deg), at most 5 aims, aim = ground normal into the body with x zeroed; the swept
# contact loses under 0x3f34fdf4 (cos 45 deg) or by the 0.1 margin. The tire forces: the bottom-out is
# live here (body +0x70 += 0.9 overlap along the normal; +0xd0 -= the body's up 0x00397000 turned, times
# min(+0x180 . normal, 0); vt +0x80 with -min(force . normal, 0) along the normal), then vt +0x84 or the
# hinge. Unloaded: +0x170 = 0x00396fc0 (0,0,0,1). Add-force-at's scale is 0x00396fd0 (1,1,1,1).
atv_ground_query            atv 0x004833c0 = the world's ground query under a point (a0 world, a1 point in/out, a2 normal out, a3 0, t0 wheel +0x110)

# Spatial update: quad +8 (its database entry; 0 in atv-r-spawn) -> atv_node_world_box(quad +0xe4), then
# atv_spatial_move(quad, centre, half, reach 0.0). Node bounds: node +8 (0 at the spawn) -> world update; stale

# centre = ((row 3 + 0) + row x * c.x + row y * c.y) + row z * c.z (xyz, w c's +0xdc); c +0xd0, e +0xe0. Its
# w = 1.0 stores are overwritten by the sdc2s. Node: +4 vtable (0x003b4ed0 for the spawned quad's), +0x104,

# entry's last centre (+0x10) to the new one crosses (0x0014d970); same cell: sub-database (+8) vt +0x20 picks a
# slot, == +0xc -> vt +0x28 (move), else vt +0x2c (out) and +0x24 (in); new cell: out of the old and every extra

# MD75c1 — atv_spatial_move ported (game-sim atv_spatial.rs spatial_move; atv_spatial::Answers answers
# quad::Call::SpatialMove). Journal: research/journal/2026-11-02-md75-atv-frame/8-spatial-move.md. Proof:
# tests/atv_spatial.rs, 12,000 generated databases laid at 0x01f80000 in atv-r-spawn.ee.bin (zero RAM up to
# 0x01fec000) and the image's own.
# Record (entry +8): +0 extras (pool links: +0 next, +4 cell, +8 sub-database, +0xc slot), +4 cell, +8
# sub-database, +0xc slot, +0x10 last centre (lq/sq, all four lanes). Cell (vtable 0x003b55b0): +0x24 links,
# +0x28 first sub-database (picked by kind bit 0), +0x2c second (bit 1, else none). Portal: +0x30..+0x60
# corners, +0x70 normal, +0x80..+0xb0 edge normals, +0xc4 plane. The walk skips only the link straight back.
# Segment test: na = n.a, nb = n.b (mula/madda/madd, n first); fails unless sign(na-d) != sign(nb-d) (xor < 0);
# t = (d-na)/(nb-na), fails if t < 0 or !(t <= 1); p = a*(1-t) + b*t (xyz, w a's); each edge i: e = c[i+1]-c[i],
# v = p-c[i], x = opmula(e,v)/opmsub(v,e); fails if n.x's sign bit is set (bltz: -0 fails).
# Sphere test: s = (c-c0).n (c-c0 first); fails if !(s <= r) or s < -r; then each edge !((c-c[i]).e[i] <= r).
# Reach: from the record's cell, each link's cell not the home and not gathered already that the sphere
# touches, then recursively from it; 35 slots on the stack, unchecked. Overlaps: each extra in list order,
# gathered -> sub-database vt +0x20 with its slot (then +0x28 or +0x2c/+0x24), the gathered entry zeroed, and the
# kind for new cells is that sub-database's; else vt +0x2c, unlinked, freed. Each remaining gathered cell:
# sub-database of the carried kind (that sub-database becomes the carried one), vt +0x20 with 0, +0x24 if nonzero,
# a pool link pushed at the front.
# List sub-database (vtable 0x003b5510): +8 kind, +0x28 block size, +0x2c block count, +0x30 used in the last,
# +0x34 blocks, +0x38 free (slot words: entry, or next free | 2). Pick: the slot given, else free, else (last ==
# size: blocks reallocated to count+1, a block allocated, last 0) blocks[count-1] + last; in: slot == free ->
# free = *slot & ~3, else last++; then *slot = entry; out: *slot = free | 2, free = slot; move: nothing.
# Pool (0x0040c460 holds it): +8 links a chunk, +0xc/+0x10 chunk chain first/last (+0 next), +0x14 chunks set
# aside, +0x18 used in the last, +0x1c stride, +0x20 chunk bytes, +0x24 free chain, +0x28 next link, +0x2c
# per-link constructor, +0x30 heap (0: atv_heap_alloc), +0x38 chunk header (4). Taking: +0x18 < +8 -> +0x28 (and
# +0x28 += stride, +0x18++); else the free chain's head; else atv_pool_chunk. Free: pushed only if stride > 4.

# No new function. bike_wreck_test (0x002522c0)'s landing crossup clause decides a crooked landing, and

# The .psg node lists (a trailing section after the last stream): u32 count | flag<<24 (0x40 one list for

# S133 — the hierarchy toys' parts (crates/game-render/tests/toy_parts.rs, states s133-free10/-nat11
#   from R7's scripted boot). The parts list is the motion object's +0x300 (count +0x304); a part's
#   +0x230 is by then a node of the toy's skeleton (one of its joints' +0x34; a mesh .col's single part
#   is on the root node +0x30), and its body's +0x254 is that same node (vt+0x74, a one-store setter).
#   The bodies of hierarchy parts are updated every frame after their toy's nodes (+0x2a0 = the
#   node's world in both states); the windsock's mesh part's body, like free09's, never is.
