# S167 — the windmill's body, live. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-28-s167-windmill-body-fling.
# Who updates the body: it is a scene node (+0x14 next, +0x18 first child, +0x1c parent, flags +0x20/+0x29),
#   the last child of group node 0x01290290 (vtable 0x0044d550, vt+0x38 0x00149270, which ends in
#   node_update_children), after the toy's root 0x012905c8 and the mesh collidable 0x01290858; the group is
#   the first child of the rustic windmill toy 0x0128fa80. animated_toy_update's last call is
#   node_update_children(dt, toy), past the visibility gate, so the body updates on each tick the toy steps
#   and on no other. Its two matrices leave the identity only after two drawn steps.
# Velocity vt+0xb8 0x001dd7b0 (a0 out, a1 body) and spin vt+0xbc 0x001dd850 (vt+0xc0 turn: cross(+0x300,
#   node+0xa0) + cross(+0x2f0, node+0x90), x app+0x348, into +0x280; turned by the node into +0x290 and out)
#   on first_recording's body before the second step: (73177.47, 4010.912, 121798.58, 1) and (0, 1.6167299,
#   0, 0); after it, zero. The port's Toys::frame tells the mesh the same (tests/toys.rs S167 test).

# S157 — the 2p recording's world contacts. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-27-s157-2p-world-contacts.
# Tests crates/game-sim/tests/two_p_world.rs (C10's images, c10-<frame>.ram, track Tracks\Free09_SS).
# The level's collision node in the 2p state is 0x0061e630 (S88's split state: 0x0061e720): objects
# 0x1060110, 0x1e54e80, then the two riders' records in either order (P2 0xdc24b0 / P1 0x1033d70 at
# 130412-131482, P1 first at 130895). A wrecked rider's record leaves the list; its wreck bike's object
# (0xdc4550 for P2) takes the place. At 60 Hz (dt 1/60) each riding record resolves once a tick, first=1.
# Both bikes are Race500P: the constructor's strstr("Race50") matches "Bike\Race500P.phy", so +0x588 = 1
# on both, and the hit clause's reach is 4: rays 0 and 1 (end y 4.580028) wreck on the scenery. All
# three of P2's world contacts are tree trunks (none of the scene's props is there): 130413 ray end y
# 1.8583021 no wreck, 130417 4.580028 -> 6, 130896 4.580028 -> 6. P1's at 131483 is the ground search
# (bike on its side), 1.8583021, ridden out. The node tick from 130416 already holds P2's reason 6

# S160 — the 2p recording's ragdolls. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-27-s160-2p-ragdoll.
# Test every_2p_wrecks_bones_are_the_originals in crates/game-sim/tests/ragdoll_wrecks.rs.
# Each rider's body actor in a 2p image: game manager 0x34,0x34,0x8,0x8,0x8,0x8 (P1) or one more 0x8
# (P2), then 0x0: the body's record; actor = record - 0x400 (+0x2e0 == 18). The list is P2 bike, P1 bike,
# P1 body, P2 body. A tick is the player update 0x0027c890 (bike) then rider_body_update (actor), both
# on the dt of the row before; bike_vehicle_update alone leaves the body a tick behind. Updating both
# riders in list order changes nothing. The body's update runs the vehicle animation through the
# actor's vtable: +0x680 (from the clip table) and +0x8f4 (the state asked for) change inside it, and
# the body mode reads +0x680 (Posed) on the next tick; e.g. P1 133954 (the NoHander entry): 1 -> 0
# and 5 -> 40. Nothing but the body's update writes the actor between two updates: not the player
# update, not ride_reset_request (0x0027b9f0), not bike_page_listener (0x00281c10, a0 = bike+0x1340,
# the respawn). The ride machine asks for the put-back (kind 1, page 1) 5-10 rows before the capture's
# body mode goes 0; the fader takes the rest. The human ended P1's 128995, 129945 and 130966 early with
# L1+R1 (pad byte 3 0xb3/0xf3 on the put-back row): ride_reset_request(bike, 1, 0) with bike+0x14d0 = 1
# respawns at once.
# S144 — tail-first landings. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-26-s144-tail-landing-wrecks.
# No new function. bike_wreck_test (0x002522c0) has no clause of its own for a tail landing: on
# recording2's rider (+0x750..+0x790: 0, front g 18.33, back g 22.0, side g 19.07, racing down g 58.67,
# freestyle down g 66.0, ground pitch down/up 1.2217/1.2217, airborne pitch down/up 1.396/1.4835, roll
# 1.4835, no-hander g 29.33, cos(no-hander roll) 0.5003, joust 2.5, landing crossup now 0.4999,
# CrossupWreck 0.99, CrossupWreckTime 0.75) a tail landing wrecks by the down g peak (reason 5, the
# rear shock's bottoming impulse lands in the same +0x554 load), by the post-physics pitch past
# GroundPitchUpWreckAngle once a wheel is down (reason 11, +0x574 set), or by the side g / airborne
# limits. A rear-first touchdown left g.x and g.z at 0 in every capture: the load is all vertical.
# Captures (context/capture/s144, git-ignored; research/s144/tail.sh, research/s144_tail.py, pads in
# research/s144/*.pad): recording2 (slot 3) teleported to (4642.5, 135.6, 3650), thrown up with a
# tail-down pitch rate (airborne auto-levelling damps it: -8 gives ~15 deg, -30 ~70 deg), states saved
# on the record's first row. Ridden out: 8 deg at 26/s down (g.y 20.2), 15 at 32, flat at 47 (46.5),
# 52 at 49 (48.7, rear comp 0.93), 55 at 53 on the gas, 34 at 41 crossed 13, 25 at 40 crossed 35 rolled
# 43, 35 at 64 crossed 20 (62.1, rear comp 1.00). Wrecked: 70 at 52 (48.8, comp 1.00; pitch 69.5 ->
# 71.37) reason 11 three ticks after touchdown; 52 at 73 (g.y 72.46) reason 5 on touchdown; crossed 49
# and leaned 50 front-first reason 2; stick held back loops it over in the air, reason 11.
# A yaw rate alone does not cross the bike up: the unyaw torque (stick centred) takes it out in ~7 ticks;
# a held sideways stick (research/s144/cross*.pad) keeps it. The port matches all twelve (S144 test).
# --- P154: the clutch's launch ramp at construction -----------------------------------------------
# bike+0x6c0 is bike_clutch's ramp timer (+0x6c4 the multiplier it started at, +0x6c8 ClutchTorqueScaleTime,
# +0x6cc the cofg-travel weight). Its only writers on a bike, from an objdump store scan: the bike
# constructor (boot 0x0024f420, sw 0 at 0x0024f6d8) and bike_clutch itself (0x002573f8 adds dt,
# 0x002574cc rearms to 0 with lever +0x644 = 1 and gas +0x63c = 1). The other 0x6c0 stores are other
# objects' constructors (0x002472c0 vehicle_anim_ctor, 0x0024ad90/0x0024c7e0 the rider body, 0x00369b00,
# 0x00390340, 0x00391f10). No put-back resets it. The ramp runs on every frame with the lever out until
# the timer passes the time, gas or no gas, so a fresh bike spends it standing. P21's and S100's captured
# launches drive at the engine's own torque (24.028 at 4500 rpm full gas, x1).
# S129 — the bike meeting free06's train. Journal: research/journal/2026-10-27-s129-bike-meets-the-train.
# Proofs: crates/game-sim/tests/replay.rs the_bike_meets_the_dallas_train_as_the_original_does and
# the_parked_bike_never_gathers_the_train (states and quarter-speed captures in s2's context/states and
# context/capture/s129, made with research/s129/*.py and watch/s129-train.toml). No new function.
# Order: a capture row's train clock (engine path +0xc) is the port's car clock one bike tick on, on
# every row of both rides: the frame runs the path cars before the bike, the port's order.
# The bike's wheel probe (+0x7c8) collides only against the bike's nearby search: 0x00257a00 clears
# +0x7d4, marks the probe moved (vt +0xa8) and calls collide_against_search(+0x7c8, *(bike+0x7bc)) at
# 0x00257868, before the substeps. A candidate is tried only when its distance (from the gather) is
# <= the wheel probe's reach +0x60 (2.79). The search (list +0, count +4, 8 bytes a candidate: object,
# distance; centre +0x10, bound +0x20, least +0x24 30, extra +0x28 50, built +0x30, capacity +0x34
# 0x100) is gathered once a tick after the substeps around the body rays +0x7c4 (S114's 0x00258374),
# and collision_search_gather does nothing unless the probe's reach left the bound (+0x20, 44.07 in the
# braked image) of where it was last gathered. The gather keeps a candidate through
# collision_pair_filter(body rays, candidate, 0): the body rays' +0xe8 category list (mode 1, exclude)
# holds the ghost's [0x468f68] = 0x40 while the bike is a ghost, which drops every 0x40 category: the
# path cars' meshes (category 0x48), the toys (0x48) and the bikes (0x9a048).
# So a bike that last gathered as a ghost and has not moved its bound since meets no car: S129's braked
# state (the bike put on the rail by tele.py from a pause-menu switch) holds 11 candidates — two
# scenery meshes (0x220), a placed group and eight race gates (0x200020) — and the whole train runs
# through it, the bike creeping 0.18 ft down the bed, braked, no wreck (the port, with no car in its
# scene, agrees on the place and the wheel contacts on all 360 ticks). In frame 16344 of that ride the
# front ray is inside the second wagon's side and the record (+0x6c) holds the rail under both rays.
# The across state (the bike 70 ft off, having moved) holds 36: the 21 path cars, two more 0x48 toys,
# three bikes and the gates, plus the scenery.
# The car's mesh collidable (car +0x428): +0x84 0x48, +0xcc ~0, +0x116 0x15, +0x31 bit 0 set, object
# list (mode 1) its own car body, empty category list (mode 1). The wheel probe: +0x84 0x18000, +0xcc
# ~0, +0xe8 mode 1 [0x100, 0x2000], object list the body rays, f.col and the sweep. The pair filter
# admits the car for both the wheel probe and the body rays.
# +0x1e0, the collidable's previous matrix: written by 0x001edb60/0x001ed9f0 with the node's last pose
# (the copy at car +0x2e0); in the braked images +0x120 and +0x1a0 are the node's current pose and
# +0x1e0 the previous one. A scan of boot.elf for loads at +0x1e0..+0x21c off a collidable in the
# contact code (the ray/sweep pairs, collide_against_search's callees) found no reader: the contact
# uses the current +0x120. Not traced live.
# --- V72: the .toy's sun flares ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Reader: `./kit fn 0x002887b0` (the [SunFlares] list: NumberOfSunFlares, then SunFlare1..n). Keys and
# defaults: TextureMap, RetrieveMaterial ("flarezretrieve"), Saturation, MinScale 4, MaxScale 4, Width/
# Height 128, VertexColor 128,128,128, LightDirection (else TargetPosition - Position, normalised),
# useToyFlareSettings 1, then occlusionSampleSize 16, flareColor 128x4 (0x00420f40), flareAlpha 40,
# flareScreenSize 350 into the OcclusionFlare filter +0x178/+0x180..188/+0x18c/+0x194. The disc writes
# FlareRGBA, never flareColor. Per-zone SaturationMin/MaxColor: 0x001ccef0.
# The flare object (sunflare.cpp): ctor 0x001cc8c0, vtable 0x004512d0, init 0x001ccaf0 (+0x38 MinScale,
# +0x3c a constant 4, MaxScale unread, dir at +0x50). Update 0x001ccf40: point = camera - dir*20
# (0x004206d0), +0x98 in-frustum, screen point +0x90/+0x94, +0x40 = 1 - r/400 facing it. Draw 0x001cd240
# -> 0x00220470 queues the z-sample occlusion box, 0x00220670 draws a 2*flareScreenSize px sprite on the
# sun's point, alpha flareAlpha x in-frustum. PC (MX vs ATV) SunFlare vf16/vf18 at 004c54f0/004c56f0:
# 1 - r*0.0025, saturation 0.25*occlusion + 0.75*centredness (guide only).
# V73 — nat01's rain: the weather box toy and the triggers' Weather action (journal *-v73-nat01-rain).
#   tests/weather.rs reads nat01's [WeatherBoxToy1] and RainBegins/RainEnds off the disc and runs
#   weather.rs's ramp; the draw is matched by eye (VU1 microprogram Vu1RainSimulation, not read).
weather_box_list     boot 0x003d97b0 [WeatherBoxToys] NumberOfWeatherBoxToys, then per [WeatherBoxToyN] a
#                                    0x270-byte toy (ctor 0x003d9a20, vtable 0x0045e920, class name
#                                    ToyWeatherBox) read by its vt +0x74 and registered by name.
weather_box_read     boot 0x003d9cb0 IsRain (1) +0xcc, NumberOfParticles (0), FollowCamera (1) +0xe8,
#                                    CameraIndex (0) +0xec, MinParticleVelocity (-180) +0xf8, Max (-220)
#                                    +0xfc, Min/MaxParticleSize (3/5), Min/MaxParticleLife (0.2/1) +0x244
#                                    +0x248, AdjustLifeAndRangeToSpeed (0) +0x250, MaximumSpeed (1) +0x258,
#                                    MinStartingRangeFromTop (25) +0x254, Max (100), AspectRatio (5) +0x260,
#                                    CameraTilt (4) +0x264, StartDensity (1) +0xd8, StartDensityStep (0.1)
#                                    +0xe0, Material. Copies: +0x24c = life, +0xf0/+0xf4 = velocities,
#                                    +0x25c aspect, +0xd0 target = +0xd4 density = start, +0xdc step,
#                                    +0x23c ramp = 1. Box (+0x90.., Radius > 0 all three) is the base
#                                    toy's. 16 entries: +0x184 (MaxLife-MinLife)*rand, +0x1c4 Box.y*(MaxRange
#                                    -MinRange)*0.01*rand, +0x144 MinSize + i*(Max-Min)/15; +0x254 *= 0.01;
#                                    3n points (+0x230): Box.x*rand - Box.x/2, (Box.y - Box.y*+0x254*rand)
#                                    - Box.y/2, Box.z*rand - Box.z/2. n/3+1 packets (+0xc4) of 3 drops
#                                    (0x350 bytes each). Every draw is the C rand (0x0040fd40): 7232 at
#                                    nat01's load.
weather_speed_table  boot 0x003da550 +0x104[i] = +0xf0 + (15-i)*(+0xf4 - +0xf0)/15, i 0..15.
weather_box_reset    boot 0x003da3d0 vt +0x7c: velocities, aspect, density/target/step back to the read,
#                                    ramp 1, clock +0x240 = 0, +0xbc = 0, packet k's time = k*life/n.
weather_box_update   boot 0x003daa70 vt +0x38 (dt): enabled (+0x3c) and not paused -> clock += dt; ramp
#                                    2 (falling): target <= d -> d -= step, d < target -> d = target,
#                                    ramp 1; else ramp 1, life = base. 1: d > target -> 2, d < target ->
#                                    0. 0 (rising): d <= target -> life = base*0.5, d += step, target < d
#                                    -> d = target, ramp 1, life = base; else ramp 1, life = base. The
#                                    step is a frame's, not dt-scaled.
weather_box_draw     boot 0x003dac10 vt +0x48: following, box centre +0x210 = camera +0x140; dir =
#                                    norm(centre + (0,CameraTilt,0) - last centre +0x220) ((0,1,0)-ish
#                                    default when 0), width = cross(dir, camera +0x150). live = packets
#                                    whose time > clock; each expired packet while live < int(n*d):
#                                    time = clock + life + +0x184[idx], 3 drops (0x003db130); then packets
#                                    from index live on with time > clock are set to clock.
weather_drop_start   boot 0x003db130 per drop: velocity (-dir.x, +0x104[idx], -dir.z), size +0x144[idx],
#                                    start centre + point[+0x234] (- speed share * +0x1c4[idx] in y only
#                                    with +0x250), streak = start - dir*size*aspect, width size; born at
#                                    the clock; idx and the point index advance.
weather_action_read  boot 0x003db500 trigger action: WeatherBoxName +0x20, NewMinParticleVelocity (0) +0x30,
#                                    NewMax (0) +0x34, NewAspectRatio (0) +0x2c, ChangeDensity (-1) +0x24,
#                                    ChangeDensityStep (-1) +0x28.
weather_action_fire  boot 0x003db600 finds the ToyWeatherBox by name: density >= 0 -> target +0xd0; step
#                                    >= 0 -> +0xdc; aspect > 0 -> +0x25c; min > 0 or max > 0 -> both
#                                    velocities and the speed table.

# --- V74: the path cars' toy sounds -----------------------------------------------------------------
# Path-car reader 0x00289710: NumberOfSounds (0x00434308), Sound%d (0x00434318), one 0x40-byte sound
# object each (ctor 0x003e54f0, vtable 0x0045f290, class name "CarProceduralSound"; the vtable is
# StaticSound's 0x0045f410 but for the dtor and +0x78), whose +0x78 reader 0x003e55c0 formats
# "ToySound_%s" (0x00448c78) and reads Looping/OneShot/ResetWhenInaudible/Force2D/Stereo, OneShotDistance
# 20, RandomTriggerPercent 100, CycleTime, MaxDistance/MinDistance 0, Priority 10, IsStream, StartEnabled
# 1, AttachToObject "body", RolloffFactor 1; flags OneShot 1 | Looping 4 | ResetWhenInaudible 8 |
# IsStream 0x10 into the world-sound maker 0x00103ef0; AttenuationCurve "Under" swaps the curve
# (0x001b7050). The car then attaches it to its model (0x001b7290, the car's +0x254), which looks the
# AttachToObject node up (0x001b37b0). Animated toys read NumberOfSounds too (0x002144d0, 0x0042f200,
# AnimatedToySound ctor 0x003e58f0, reader 0x003e59d0 adds MotionName/StartTime/ConfigFile): no
# AnimatedToy on the disc names one. PC: CarProceduralSound::vf32 / AnimatedToySound::vf32 at

# --- S131: free09's props in a ride (crates/game-sim/src/prop_set.rs; journal 2026-10-28-s131-props-vs-bike-and-rider)
# Physics object init 0x001597b0 makes, beside the body: the collision object at +0x120 (ctor 0x0026f980,
# the wreck bike's kind), the <model>m.col ray probe at +0x124, the <model>f.col mesh at +0x128 (category
# flags 0x00468f50 | 0x00468f58; owner through vt +0x80 = the wrapper's +0x48, the struck listener
# 0x0015ad40) and a sweep probe at +0x12c. Sign_SpeedLmt has only an f.col; BeachBall both. None of the
# props' f.col files is in free09's scene collision list (276 entries; only nat13_forsale_sign.col of the

# The edge object (+0x100) every free09 prop names in the first_recording state: min (0, 0, 0, 1), max
# (6912, 268.6465, 6912, 1) — the terrain's extent, 256 x cells x spacing on x and z; the port takes it

# --- V76: the animated toys' visibility gate and UpdateSearch (crates/game-sim/src/toys.rs Toys::seen /
#   Spec::unseen / Spec::search, crates/game-render/src/animated_toy.rs drawn, wired in openmx's ride
#   loop; proved in crates/game-sim/tests/toys.rs the_visibility_gate_is_the_originals) ---
# animated_toy_read (0x002144d0) stores: HasCollision +0xec, UpdateSearch +0xf0 (default 1),
#   AnimateWhenNotVisible +0x10c (1), AnimateWhenZoneNotVisible +0x110 (1), DisableWhenIdle +0x148 ->
#   +0x108 (0), DependentToy%d names at +0x12c, resolved into the list at +0x114. Key strings at
#   0x0042f120..0x0042f1b0.
# animated_toy_update (0x002150f0): returns at once when *(0x0041f770) +0x325 bit 3 is set; then the
#   visibility test 0x00215050 on the toy and each toy of +0x114: visible when +0x10c != 0, or when the
#   root node's (*(motion +0x30)) u16 +0 search stamp != the toy's copy +0x144 and root +0x111 bit 0
#   (the draw's last frustum test) is set. Then the +0x110 zone test against the zone id at gp-0x7f64.
#   Not visible: nothing (no motion_advance, no re-place, no stamp copy). Else, last, +0x144 = the stamp.
#   UpdateSearch on (and motion +0x60 == 0 or +0x3c == 0): 0x001b4ba0(root), and with HasCollision
#   0x00119420, which per part runs 0x00119be0 (vt +0xa8 matrix and reach, then 0x00119d00 ->
#   0x001ab310 re-files the part in the world's tree).
# The frustum bit: 0x001a1100 sets +0x111 bit 0 from vt +0xb8 = 0x00176e90: node world matrix times the
#   camera's view-projection (*0x0048a560 +0xc0), box test 0x001cb5e0 on centre +0xe0, half extent
#   +0xf0. The +0 stamp is the search tree's; the base node ctor 0x001ab330 zeroes it.
# first_recording's free09: all five toys +0x10c 0, +0x110 1, +0xf0 0; every root +0x111 = 0x2 (out of
#   view); toy stamps 0x8e30, 0x775f, 0, 0, 0 against root stamps all 0x1244. Root boxes: seti windmill
#   centre (0.0065, 79.73, -2.13) half (30.0, 79.73, 3.93) (posed, a little different per toy); rustic
#   windmill centre (-1.62, 22.01, 0.0002) half (8.02, 22.01, 6.54).
# The disc's .toy files: AnimateWhenNotVisible False on free09's five, free10 SH_windsock, one free10_ss
#   toy, nat02 church_bell (free11/free11_ss balloons too, under a count of 0, so never loaded); True on
#   free09_ss, nat01, nat08, nat11, nat15, free10 shovel_01. No file writes DependentToy,

# C17 — the rotor's contact search (research/journal/2026-11-03-c17-kiowa-rotor-strike). Ported as
# game_sim::kiowa::{ray_tree, WreckRotor::probe, Collision::rotor_struck}.
ray_probe_build boot 0x001eab40 (probe, count, rays, a3, a4): a ray probe's tree off `count` rays
#   (from, to; 0x20 each). Identity transform at +0x120..+0x150; 0x001115b0 takes each ray's centre
#   ((from + to) * 0.5 on the VU, before the transform) and runs the recursive build 0x00111160:
#   one ray is a leaf (volume -1); more, a node boxed by 0x0010f990 over all 2n ends, split where
#   0x001104c0 says, the stable partition 0x0010f780 (centre[axis] > split first, args on the stack
#   at sp+0/+8: below count, axis), above copied back ahead of below, children above then below.
#   An empty side falls back to n/2, n - n/2 copied out of lists never written (never reached when
#   centres differ). 0x001104c0: box of the ends (start point 0; > max raises, else < min lowers),
#   longest axis (first strictly > 0, then strictly longer), cuts lo + k * (ext / 20); first guess
#   k = 10 on that axis, its cost vol(above) + vol(below), or 100 * vol(all) when a side is empty;
#   then k = 1..18, axis x, y, z within each, taking a strictly smaller cost. Flatten 0x001117f0 is
#   depth first: node index in visit order, leaf index from -1 counting up (so 0 first), child word
#   leaf | 0x8000; the radius it writes is vec3_length(half). Probe +0x160 root, +0x168 count.
# The rotor probe heli+0x458 (C14's image): category +0x84 0x80000, +0xcc 0xffffffff, flags 0x12, no
#   listeners, object list mode 1 [heli's own collidable], category list mode 1 [0x100, 0x100000].
#   kiowa_wreck_check, reason 0: rotor-in-ground over the probe's segments, then probe vt +0xac with
#   heli+0x4d0 (the measure's matrix), collide_against_search (0x0011a9d0) on heli+0x16c (the main
#   object's nearby list, as last gathered); if probe+0x116 & 0x20, struck = objects[0] of the
#   probe's ray record (+0x6c -> +0x9c); wreck if struck and (struck+0x84 & heli+0x464) == 0, the
#   struck mask 0x40000 in C14's image.
kiowa_rotor_altitudes boot 0x003900f0 (rotor, MinAltitude f12, MaxAltitude f13) -> +0x30, +0x34
kiowa_wing_geometry boot 0x0038f960 (aero, chord, span, area, ratio, aoa in f12..f16) -> +0x34..+0x44;
#                                 a zero chord with a positive ratio becomes span / ratio

#   +0x1fc width (and +0x80 anchor += width - old), +0x54 1/inertia, +0x58 gyro; 0x0039aea0 +0x5c steer;

# S247 — the cars' keyed-loop engine voice (carengine.aud), research/journal/*-s247-car-engine-sound.
# Ported as openmx audio::CarEngine / CarGas / CarNote (presentation: matched in its keys and ramps, not
# bit-exact). The car's sound setup (called from the car's loader) builds it with the engine's +0x130
# (its torque table's last rpm, the top revs), pointers to the engine's +0x138 (the sound pitch) and the
# car's +0x3ec (the throttle), and the body's place/velocity; the profile is the machine's directory name.
car_sound_setup             boot 0x003990f0 (car, ..., archive, profile): the voice, the tire params and more
car_voice_ctor              boot 0x003a5230 (voice): vtable 0x0045d420, +0x218 = 44100, +0x220 = 1 (first frame)
car_voice_init              boot 0x003a53d0 (top revs, voice, archive, profile, pitch*, at*, vel*, throttle*)
car_voice_settings          boot 0x003a5930 (voice, ini, profile): the attenuation, throttle and Doppler keys
car_voice_loops             boot 0x003a5ab0 (voice, ini, profile, reload): MinRPM, MaxRPM, the loops and keys
car_voice_frame             boot 0x003a54c0 (dt in f12, voice): the vtable's frame; the ramps, revs and keys
# Voice words: +0x200 MinRPM, +0x204 MaxRPM (ints), +0x208 pitch pointer, +0x20c the revs last keyed,
#   +0x210 loop count, +0x214 loop records {base rpm, key count, base key index, keys*}, keys 12 bytes
#   {rpm, volume, pitch word = (OriginalSampleRatePS2 + rate) * 4096 / 48000} (ints), +0x218
#   OriginalSampleRatePS2, +0x21c ThrottleThreshold (0.9), +0x224 the top revs, +0x228 throttle pointer,
#   +0x22c 1 - OffThrottleVolume (1), +0x230 OnThrottleVolumeRampTime, +0x234 OffThrottleVolumeRampTime
#   (both 0), +0x238 ramp left, +0x23c on the gas last frame, +0x240 the loops' sound objects (vt +0x30
#   volume, +0x40 pitch word), +0x9c DopplerFactor (0.25), +0x98 RolloffFactor (1), +0x88/+0x8c
#   AttenuationMin/MaxDistance. The frame: on = throttle >= threshold; the ramp restarts on a change
#   (on ramp coming on, off ramp going off); gain on = 1 - cut*left/onramp, off = 1 - cut + cut*left/
#   onramp (both over the on ramp); revs = MaxRPM * pitch / top held to [MinRPM, MaxRPM]; each loop
#   silent outside its keys, a key's own on a key, else between the last key under and the first over,
#   volume (int)((lo + (int)(t*dv)) * gain), pitch lo + (int)(t*dp); then the ramp less dt, at least 0.

# car_preforce order: controls, the tires' clear (0x0039af70 x4: +0xb4 solid, +0xb8 sorted, +0x1ac,
#   +0x1b0 = 0; +0xbc terrain-held is kept frame to frame), the sort, the four probes, then +0xc4.
# car_sort_rays_build, called last in car_phy_load: per tire FL, FR, RL, RR a ray from mount (+0x80) +
#   (0, MaxCompression +0x3c) down to mount - (0, radius +0x1f8), through 0x001eab40 (probe, 4, rays, 0,
#   0). Its tree (0x00111160 build, 0x001104c0 split, 0x0010f780 a stable partition, greater first)

# The sort: probe vt +0xac with car +0x5c0 (the machine matrix), collide_against_search on car +0x16c;
#   per hit the ray's from.z > 0 picks a front tire, from.x > 0 the right one (FR +0x54c, FL +0x548,
#   RR +0x554, RL +0x550); 0x0039af90 sets point +0x110, normal +0x120, +0x1ac object, +0x1b0 mover,

#   listeners (hit +0x80 -> +0x1f8 -> +0xf4 list, vt +0xc) are not ported.
# car_tire_probe after its loop: a sorted tire whose normal faces the car's up (+0x5a0) under 0.707
#   (mula/madda/madd) loses it. If the terrain is not below the lower end: an unsorted tire takes the
#   terrain (solid); a sorted one sets +0xbc when its axle (unit upper - lower) faces the sorted normal
#   under 0.707, or when unheld and the terrain stands over 0.1 above the sorted point; a held one is

#   normal, sorted cleared.
# car_tire_suspension: the contact path runs on solid || sorted; its bottom-out push reads solid (+0xb4), not sorted.

# --- S248: a driven car's particles (crates/openmx/src/particles.rs PlaneFrame::car / TireFrame::car /
#   car_list, main.rs load_car_fx; tests/car_particles.rs; journal 2026-11-02-s248-car-particles)
# The car's particle loader (buggy and baja 0x0039e230, the monster truck's 0x003dfd90, called from the car
#   setup 0x0020a640) reads tracks\buggyparticles.dat / monsterparticles.dat / bajaparticles.dat (a

#   (ground speed), wreck +0x754. +0x754 is never set (C18 below): the port feeds no wreck.
# The particle nodes (FrontLeft_TireParticles, buggy_FtireL_particles, baja_exhaust_particles, ...) are all
#   children of the model's root.
# The DirtSpray and Chunks zone descriptors say none to Zone1 (surface 0): a car on surface 0 dusts but
#   throws no dirt or chunks.
# --- C18: the car's particle manager against PINE captures (tests/car_particles.rs
#   the_cars_emitters_follow_the_original; journal 2026-11-03-c18-car-particles-capture)
# The loader's wreck word +0x754 is never set: its only stores are zeros in the car ctors (0x0039d3e8 in
#   0x0039d310, buggy/baja; 0x003ded7c in 0x003deca0, the truck), no sq/sd covers it. Readers: 0x003a0340
#   (rumble only while 0), 0x0039e9c0 (== 0), 0x003a1a00 (== 0xd with +0x530), the truck's 0x003e0554 /
#   0x003e1a3c / 0x003e2f50 / 0x003e3078; its address is also kept in car +0xad0 (block at 0x0039ed00)
#   and the feed's +0xa94. It looks copied from the bike's wreck reason (+0x748). The car's own wreck is
#   +0x2c4: vt +0x1d0 (0x00398bd0) writes the reason there and zeroes +0x2c8 (lifted), vt +0x1d4
#   (0x00398be0) undoes it; the wreck step 0x00398820 (the edge throw) calls vt +0x1d0 with 13, the
#   car's only wreck. Live: +0x754 stays 0 through a reason-13 throw (truck-edge, frames 460..670), so the
#   car's manager always reads NotWrecking: its exhausts (Disable Wrecking only) stay live all wreck.
# The feed block, car +0xa70 (written by the loaders 0x0039e230 / 0x003dfd90): [0] tire count 4, +0xa74
#   the tire records (stride 0x2c; +0x28 flags byte 0x10|NoContact 1|Ground 2|Object 4, +0x29 zone),
#   +0xa7c car +0x170, +0xa88 &throttle (+0x3ec), +0xa8c &clutch (+0x3f0), +0xa90 &speed (+0x55c),
#   +0xa94 &wreck (+0x754). The manager reads it at manager +0x30 (feed +0x24 the wreck pointer); its
#   entries: count manager +0x34, list manager +0x3c. The manager is car +0xf00 (buggy, baja) / +0xff0
#   (truck); the car is 0x00605270 in every S149a free07 state.
# An entry: +4 tire record, +8 flag byte (1 live, 2 zone-off), +0xc Disable, +0x10 vtable, +0x14 emitter
#   (+0x240 switch byte, bit 2 on; +0x3b0 carry; +0x340 rate; +0x40 point; +0x80 velocity x), +0x28 a
#   spray's fade clock. Vtables: 0x45e690 CollisionModel, 0x45e780 Exhaust, 0x45e750 Dust, 0x45e720
#   Spray (dirt and chunks), 0x45e6f0 Generic (the truck's rear dust sprays), 0x45e630 TireTrack (last
#   four). Entry counts: buggy 22, truck 22, baja 21 (the port's 18/18/17, tracks left out, same order).
# Phase: a row's manager state is after it read that same row's words (not the row before's, as P74's
#   bike capture). A record's zone byte equals the tire's +0x100 on every grounded row; in the air it
#   differed on 7 tire-rows of 2600.
# A zone-off (flag 3) tire dust never emits: 0 of 228 captured frames grounded, compressed past 0.35 and
#   over 20 ft/s, against ~90% of live ones (P60's reading that the dust's update switches the emitter
#   back on does not hold).
# Edge-state recipe: shorten the map's depth in a copy of the tape's save state (edge_state.py rewrites
#   area +0x534, area = *(*(car +0x2b4) +0x3b4); the edge test is within 150 ft of (+0x530 x 256 x
#   +0x538) by (+0x534 x 256 x +0x53c); free07 3,3,9,9 = 6912 ft); depth 2400 throws S149a's parked truck
#   at frame 460.
# S269 — the cars' TireTrack entries (crates/openmx/src/tire_tracks.rs Bike::car, main.rs step_car_tire_marks;
#   tests/car_tire_tracks.rs; journal 2026-11-03-s269-car-tire-tracks): the car loaders read their list through
#   vehicle_fx_read, so the four tracks run on the same records (contact +0x140, normal +0x120). Every list is
#   Material ttrack; widths buggy 0.25/0.5, truck 2.0, baja 0.5; MinC/MaxC 0.75/1 (truck 1.5/2.5).
# MD76 — Desert Oasis's ground for the ATV (crates/game-sim/src/atv_ground.rs; tests/atv_ground.rs runs the
# query off MD37a's atv-r-spawn.ee.bin over 29,450 points, nothing hooked). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-11-02-md76-atv-ground. Desert Oasis Free Ride is Env/MC02 (mc02.res, member
# Env\mc02\MC02.ps2.trn; its heights match the image's cells). The terrain object the wheels hold (wheel
# +0x1e0) is 0x0184c7d0 there: +0x30 box min (0,0,0,1), +0x40 box max (5760, 343.9, 5760, 1), +0x50 margin
# 0.01, +0x90 height scale 4.5, +0x94 the cell reader's terrain (0x01840a10), +0x770 split one way (1),
# +0x788 samples per foot 0.2222222, +0x8b8 coarse (0), +0x9e0 ring set up, +0x9e4 ring index. MX Unleashed's
# offsets are these less 0x10 from +0x770 on (+0x760, +0x778, +0x8a8) and +0x80/+0x84 for +0x90/+0x94.
# The query = MX Unleashed's 0x001dc960 with x and z held in the box first (max(p, min + m), min(., max - m))
# and both height sums on the FPU accumulator (adda 0, h; madda u; madd v). The ring: 0x00541550, 0x70 bytes an
# entry (normals +0, heights x height scale +0x40, key ix<<16|iz +0x50, surfaces +0x5c, ix/iz +0x60/+0x64),
# count 10 at 0x005251f0, keys of 32 cleared when +0x9e0 is 0; keyed by the sample alone, shared by terrains.
# The cell reader: heights hs * raw, or hs * (adda 0, base; madd vs, raw - base) when vs != 1; normals
# (n0 vs/127, n1/127, n2 vs/127, 1) with 1/127 a div.s, unless the flag 0x00541518 is set (only read in the
# ELF), when they come from a 256-direction table at 0x0039e320 by the block's byte +4. Surfaces & 0x7f. The
# block in RAM: base +0, table index +4, surfaces +0x125, heights +0x27c (rows of 20), normals +0x550 (rows of
# 17, 3 bytes each).
atv_terrain_cell            atv 0x0047b300 = the terrain's cell at a sample (a0 terrain, a1 ix, a2 iz, a3 heights out, t0 normals out, t1 surfaces out, t2 step); 0 = none
atv_ground_ring             atv 0x00541550 = the ground query's ring of read cells (0x70 bytes each, 10 used)
atv_normal_table_flag       atv 0x00541518 = the cell reader takes normals from the 256-direction table when set

# MD86 — the quad's Moto answers ContactCall::Collide (quad::Steps's sweep, atv_search::Oasis; tests/atv_quad/oasis.rs
# runs atv_quad_wheel_contacts 0x004bd2d0 unhooked off MD37a's atv-r-spawn.ee.bin). The distributor: quad +0x143c = 0,
# the trigger's vt +0xc8 (atv_search_node_stand: the node brought up to date, its world copied from +0x70, the probe
# stood there), atv_collide_against_search(trigger, quad +0xc4, 1), the hit bit (+0x126 bit 5), then the record onto
# the wheels. The gather into +0xc4 is not the distributor's: the PostProcess's collision sync does it round the active
# probe (+0xd8, 0x0159c500), which is why an image's trigger frame lags its node. A plant's sphere can set the hit bit
# with no contact (the port does not test plant spheres; nothing reads the bit then). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-11-02-md76-atv-ground/5-contacts-MD86.md.
# MD76a1 — MC02's static collision and the quad's gather (crates/game-sim/src/atv_world.rs; tests/atv_gather.rs
# runs the original's gather off MD37a's atv-r-spawn.ee.bin, gp 0x003dbaf0). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-11-02-md76-atv-ground/3-collidables-and-gather-MD76a1.md. The gather (a0 list, a1 probe,
# a2 kind 3; called from 0x004942c0) always refills the list. List fields: +0 entries (8 bytes: collidable,
# distance), +4 count, +0x10 centre, +0x20 bound, +0x24 least (10), +0x28 extra (30), +0x2c no groups, +0x30
# built, +0x34 capacity (0x100), +0x38 filter, +0x3c flat; +0x40 prepare is not touched. The bound is
# min(c - lo x, c - lo z, hi x - c, hi z - c), min'd with max(radius, extra), max'd with least, every min/max a
# compare-and-pick. Distances: 3D 0x001169a0, flat 0x001169f0 (dz first into the accumulator). A plant group's
# distance: adda 0, p; msub cell x cell size (z first), reach = near + blend x (far - near)/128, then 0 + reach;
# d <= 0 -> 0. Group record: cell x +0xc, cell z +0x10, model +0x12, blend (i8) +0x15. The eco object
# 0x0052cd88: +0x84 models (+0x10c near, +0x114 far), +0xc4 cell size. Models are the species in loaded order
# with empty slots skipped (MC02's slot 0 is empty; slots 1-11 are models 0-10). The pair filter is
# world_search::accepts at other offsets: +0x126 flags, +0x45 bit 0 nearby, +0x94 category, +0xdc collides
# with, +0xe0/+0xe4/+0xe8 objects (mode, list, count), +0xec more objects, +0xf0/+0xf4 the second loop's
# list, +0xf8/+0xfc/+0x100 categories. Queries 0x0019d260/0x0019d2b0/0x0019d360/0x0019d760. The tree 0x006547c0
# is a quadtree (vtable 0x003b6700: begin 0x00180dd0, collect 0x001810b0, insert vt +0x24 = 0x00180290) built
# by 0x0017fc40 (via 0x001ae990 from 0x0031a750) as broad_build::tree_over with least 96; box (-0, 5760), the
# cell-size global 0x0040c1a0, stamp 0x003d3b88; fields origin +0x28/+0x30, extent +0x40/+0x44, cell +0x50,
# scale +0x54, root +0x8c. Scene meshes: vtable 0x003b6d00, category 0x600, flags 0x0c, collides with all,
# categories mode 1 empty, nearby. Zones: vtables 0x003b6ec0 and 0x003b6a60, category 0x8000, enabled, not
# within bound (not built). Zone_Platform_Machine.col's mesh 0x006933d8 is filed twice in nodes 023102 and
# 023103 (scene order, then after the plants behind a zone 0x013efe88 at the same place). The moving list
# 0x006552b0: the quad's body (0x3012) and probe, rider parts (0x1000, off), category-0 objects. The quad's
# list is quad +0xc4 = 0x01627a40, its probe 0x0159c500.
atv_gather_nearby           atv 0x001d6a60 = the quad's nearby list refilled from the broad phase (a0 list, a1 probe, a2 kind)
atv_distance                atv 0x001169a0 = the distance from a point to a collidable's sphere
atv_flat_distance           atv 0x001169f0 = the same on x and z
atv_pair_accepts            atv 0x001144f0 = the pair filter (world_search::accepts)
atv_quadtree_insert         atv 0x00180290 = the quadtree's vt +0x24 (insert)
atv_quadtree_new            atv 0x0017fc40 = the quadtree constructor (least 96)

# MD76a2 — the quad's wheel-ray search (crates/game-sim/src/atv_search.rs; tests/atv_search.rs runs the
# original's stand, gather and search off MD37a's atv-r-spawn.ee.bin, gp 0x003dbaf0). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-11-02-md76-atv-ground/4-search-MD76a2.md. atv_collide_against_search is
# world_search::search_nearby but: a class-2 collidable whose word 0 is 1 is passed over before its
# searched bit is set; prepare (vt +0xd0) runs only if list +0x40 != 0 and a2. atv_quad_wheel_contacts sets
# quad +0x143c = 0, calls the search's vt +0xc8 (stand at the node's matrix), then the search (a2 1); on
# the hit bit (0x20) it walks the record: rays +0x90 (0x20 a ray, x from [0], z from [2]), normals +0x94,
# points +0x98, objects +0x9c; owner obj +0x90, vehicle obj +0x8c (counted if its +0x1a0 == 2); the wheels
# quad +0x17b0..+0x17bc (LF, RF, LR, RR). The trigger probe quad +0x1430 = 0x0162cd30: ray-probe vtable
# 0x003b6c20, category 0x12, collides with all, objects mode 1 (4, the quad's), more objects mode 1 empty,
# categories mode 1 [0x8000, 0x4] (zones left out), flags 0x12, nearby, no listeners, word 0 = 0, smooth
# +0x184 0, filter +0x188 0; tree root u16 +0x170, ray count +0x178 (4), nodes +0x17c (0x24 each), rays
# +0x180 (0x20 each), in the order RF, LF, RR, LR. Stand: matrix to +0x130, centre +0x50 = row 3 + rows x
# root centre (accumulator), radius = sqrt(mula hx hx, madda hy hy, madd hz hz). The node matrix is the
# body's rotation with position = body pos - row 1 (CofG +0x1a0 = (0,1,0)). Record: from +0, to +0x10,
# normal +0x20, point +0x30, push +0x40, low +0x50, high +0x60, first point +0x70, first +0x80, part +0x84,
# capacity +0x88 (3 x rays), count +0x8c, rays +0x90, normals +0x94, points +0x98, objects +0x9c, placed
# +0xa0, fractions +0xa8, +0xac a word a contact zeroed at every pair and never written, segments u16
# +0xb0, nearest +0xb4, flags +0xb8, one per ray +0xbc = 1. Plants (eco 0x0052cd88, models +0x84): each
# species' parts +0x19c (count +0x194), shared by all its plants and stood at the plant at each ask
# (skipped if group +0x16 bit 3): kind 5 sphere (category 0x80; its ray test writes no contact, only the
# hit bit), kind 0 mesh (category 0x40; MC02's prickly pear B and weeds B, built cylinders of height 0,
# nodes +0x244, facets +0x248, root +0x23c 0) at rows +0x1b0..+0x1dc (identity), position = cell x eco
# +0xc4, then vt +0xcc; the turn (group +0x16 bit 2) reads eco +0x70, NULL here. Scene meshes: kind +0x78 0,
# frame +0x130 = the prop's to_world bit for bit.
atv_ray_probe_stand         atv 0x001bcfe0 = the ray probe stood at a matrix (a0 probe, a1 matrix): its frame, centre and reach (vt +0xcc)
atv_eco_part_count          atv 0x0046d390 = a plant group's part count (the species' +0x194)
atv_eco_part                atv 0x0046d3c0 = a plant group's part i, stood at the plant
atv_ray_sphere              atv 0x001095d0 = the ray probe against a sphere (hit bit only; not ported)
atv_ray_mesh_leaf           atv 0x00107a20 = the ray probe's leaf against a mesh's facet
atv_ray_tree_pair           atv 0x00108820 = the ray probe's tree against a mesh's tree
atv_listener_at             atv 0x00117460 = a collidable's listener j
atv_record_current          atv 0x00116990 = the record's current contact
pc_quad_collide_search      pc 0x00424350 = the PC's collide_against_search (the quad's search)

# P285 -- the ridden bike's non-engine sounds (research/bike-sounds.md, journal bike-ride-sounds).
# No track sets [Crowd] CrowdPresent (all 54 .aud have it commented out), so the crowd never sounds.

crowd_play boot 0x001fe380 (crowd, kind, position) plays only when the kind's last sound stopped: 0 Oh
#                          (+0x74, panned), 1 Boo (+0x5c), 2 Cheer (+0x2c, panned), 3 PlayerCheer (+0x44
#                          stereo stream). Crowd object at game+0xc0, +0x14 Ambient
bike_place_change_crowd boot 0x0027edb0 (bike vtable 0x455164, thunk 0x281ad0, score-card owner interface)
#                          gain: card +0xb4 >= 5.0 -> PlayerCheer if card +8 & 4 else Cheer at +0x60;
#                          loss: Boo when the loser is human, the passer not, and the passer's +0x2c == 1.
#                          Counters bike+0x12ec/+0x12f0/+0x12f4. No caller on a trick landing
race_ranking boot 0x00261b80 sorts the score cards by +0x1c, writes place +0x2c, calls the place change
#                          (player_wreck_entry 0x0027b810 is the third crowd_play caller: Oh at the chassis)
material_sound_load boot 0x003ea810 loads a material's CollisionSoundFile (+8) into +4
material_hit_queue boot 0x003ea8a0 the material table's listener (+0x34, vt+8 via thunk 0x3eb620): queues
#                          owner -> +0x80, struck record -> +0x98, 0 -> +0xb0. vt+0xc 0x3ea8f0 is a no-op
material_listener_attach boot 0x003eada0 (table interface +0x30) adds the listener to obj+0x1f0's list (vt +0x80)
material_listener_detach boot 0x003ead60 removes it
material_hit_play boot 0x003ea900 run by the update 0x3ead10 when queued: speed = |impulse| of the owner (+0x80
#                          -> +0xf4 body +0xb0, else +0xf0 +0x170), repeated pairs keep the faster; material =
#                          struck +0x89 (0x10 via 0x0026f4b0; struck +0x84 & word 0x00468f48 -> +0x65
#                          SolidFoliageID); speed >= MinImpulse (+0x114): level clamp(speed/MaxImpulse(+0x118)
#                          *100, 0, 100), vt+0x30, vt+0x5c position (+0x60), sound_3d 0x00104480 with
#                          +0x10c/+0x110 distances, vt+0x1c play(4)
#                          B27: the speed is the body's shock peak (+0xb0, cleared at preforce) or the
#                          chassis' impact accumulator (+0x170, cleared by the ground step), not |vel|, so
#                          only the strike frame passes MinImpulse; vt+0x1c start 0x166380 never stops the
#                          old voice (play(4) always allocates). f27a1 2903: 16.81 -> level 67, 2904-2906: 0

# --- F27: the material table's collision sounds (crates/game-sim/src/material_hit.rs, openmx audio.rs HitTable;
#   tests/material_hit.rs; journal 2026-11-03-f27-material-hit-sounds)
# Table = game_manager +0x40 (ctor 0x003e9f00); s195-knock-2881 has it at 0x00e17d90. Interface +0x30
#   (vtable 0x0045f684) registered on the collision node by 0x0026f7f0; the node's file 0x0026f680 calls
#   every interface's vt +8 (attach 0x003eada0: listener +0x34, vtable 0x0045f694, onto record +0x1f0's
#   probe list), unfile 0x0026f720 its vt +0xc (detach). So plane_probe_sound_listener is this listener,
#   on every filed object's probe: in knock-2881 the four bikes' 11-ray body probes (record +0x1f0,
#   category 0x18008; human bike 0x00dad810 probe 0x00da2ff0, not the wheel probe +0x7c8), the parked
#   plane's 41-ray probe (wrapper 0x00e1e260), and the two once-woken beach balls (filed asleep through
#   DetectCollisionsWhenDisabled: three listeners, object / collection / material; never-woken balls two).
# Queue: owners +0x80 (count, ?, ptr +0x88, cap +0x10 growth), struck +0x98 (ptr +0xa0), speeds +0xb0
#   (ptr +0xb8). Pair drop: f(x) = x +0x80 record -> +0x1f8 collidable; entry j with struck == f(owner i)
#   and f(owner j) == struck i: i goes if speed i < speed j (swap-remove, i looked at again), else j
#   (search goes on from j). Struck +0x89 == 16 takes f(owner) +0x89; with f(owner) 0 the register $s7
#   keeps the last entry's material (0 at entry assumed). Lookup: first of list +0x4c (count +0x44)
#   with the ID; material 0 plays nothing; speed < +0x114 skips; level cvt.w.s(100 * (speed / +0x118)).
# Loader 0x003ea350 keeps only IDs in table +0x40's in-use list (free09: 1-7, 9-11, 14, 16, 19, 21, 22).
#   Key strings 0x00448e20..0x00448f00: CollisionSoundFile, CollisionMaxDistance (+0x110),
#   CollisionMinDistance (+0x10c), CollisionMinImpulse (+0x114, default 0), CollisionMaxImpulse (+0x118,
#   default 1). [Settings] TerrainMask +0x64, SolidFoliageID +0x65 (22). Every free09 entry 30/125 ft
#   and 5/25 but 16 (Impact\bikehit1.vag) 2/9; 21 Impact\ball.vag, 22 Impact\treehit.vag.
# F27a: the props node 0x00e17ef0's update 0x0026f800 queues the table's hits (no table update in it):
#   knock-2881 queues bike probe 0x00da2ff0 -> ball 2 (struck +0x89 21) and ball 2's probe -> the bike's
#   frame collidable 0x00da3180 (+0x89 16, +0x84 0x9a048); knock-2882 only the bike's; knock-2883 none.

# MD80a — SLUS-21104's biplane and Kiowa flown and captured (journal research/journal/2026-11-03-md80a-mxvatv-
# flier-captures; specs research/md80a/{biplane,kiowa}.toml, captures context/capture/md80a, images
# context/mxvatv-ps2/md80a). Desert Oasis Free Ride places both. Field names marked (value) were checked
# against the captured numbers; (shift) are MX Unleashed's names moved down 0xc as # MD80 says, not checked.
# Vtables (+4): biplane 0x003ce7b0, Kiowa 0x003cfad0. Model word +0x1320 / +0x1340: 1 model one. The body is
# *(+0xb8): +0x60 position, +0x80 velocity, +0x170 quaternion (value), +0x1d0 angular velocity in the body
# frame (value: the quaternion's derivative, 2-3% mean); +0x1c0 is not it (large, undecoded).
# Both: +0x310/+0x314 the throttle-down/up (Square/Cross) values (value), +0x32c |velocity| (value),
#   +0x340..+0x348 the angular velocity in the world frame (value), +0xc80 the wreck word: 1 a hard landing
#   or tip-over, 13 a hit on a hard object; the engine/aero (rotor/aero) skip while it is set.
# Biplane: +0x318 throttle (value: engine +0x50 = 250 x it; Square takes it to -0.0117), +0x10f4/+0x10f8/
#   +0x10fc elevator/aileron/rudder commands (value: the stick, cubed with a dead zone; L1 -1 / R1 +1), +0x1110 nose steer and
#   +0x1114 rudder, ramped 1/s toward the rudder command (value). Parts: engine *(+0x1164) 0x80 bytes, aero *(+0x1168) 0x110,
#   tires *(+0x116c..+0x1174) 0x380 apart. Engine +0x3c prop angle (+-pi), +0x50 power = MaxPower x throttle
#   (value), +0x54 rpm = 500 + 2000 x max(throttle, 0) (value), +0x60 thrust, +0x64 the sound's rpm (shift),
#   +0x68 clock, +1/30 a tick (value). Aero +0xdc..+0xec CL..CM (shift). Tire +0xc0..+0xc8 the ground
#   ray's hit (value: the ground under the gear), +0x8c..+0x94 nonzero only on the ground, +0xe0..+0xe8
#   the last contact, +0xf0/+0x100 the gear point in the world (value: +0x104 - +0xc4 is the height).
# Kiowa: +0x10f0 climb (Cross), +0x10f4 descend (Square), +0x10fc = -stick x, +0x1100 = -stick y, +0x1104
#   pedal (L1 +1, R1 -1) (value); +0x1108 collective (1 climbing, ~0.32-0.34 holding height), +0x110c..
#   +0x1114 cyclic and pedal outputs, +0x1138 the held height (value: the top of the climb). Parts: rotor
#   *(+0x11c0) 0x150 bytes, aero *(+0x11c4). Rotor +0xc0 blade pitch (0.2618 = 15 deg climbing), +0xd0/+0xd8
#   tilt, +0xf4 thrust, +0x100..+0x108 thrust along the tilted axis (value: +0x104 = +0xf4 hovering).
#   Aero +0x128 a 0..1 fade with speed, +0x134/+0x140 on the ground -0.5236/0.0785 (-30 deg / 4.5 deg).
# MD89 — the quadtree sub-database's moving-entry virtuals (game-sim atv_spatial.rs Tree; atv_spatial's Database
# dispatches List/Tree). Journal: research/journal/2026-11-02-md75-atv-frame/8-spatial-move.md "MD89". Proof:
# tests/atv_spatial.rs (generated trees laid at 0x01f80000 in atv-r-spawn.ee.bin; the image's own tree 0x006547c0).
# Tree (vtable 0x003b6700): +8 kind, +0xc flags (bit 0 a query open), +0x28/+0x30 origin x/z, +0x40/+0x44 extent,
# +0x4c deepest level, +0x54 cells per unit, +0x58 + 4 level the node side (0x4000 >> level), +0x8c root, +0x90
# chunk pool, +0x94 query pool, +0x98 bit 0 the node free keeps chunks, +0x9c/+0xa0/+0xa4/+0xac query walk,
# +0x880 nodes made less freed, +0x888 entries put less taken out, +0x8a0..+0x8ac query cells (0x80000000 closed).
# 0x0040c198 the tree being changed (in and out set it; the node new/free count in its +0x880).
# Node (vtable 0x003b66e0 at +0x10): +0 children (0, or node +0x14 once grown to 36 bytes by realloc), +4 chunk
# chain (pool links: +0 next, +4..+0x10 four entries), +8 mid y, +0xc half y (new: 0, -FLT_MAX).
# Pick (vt +0x20, ignores the current key): x0 = (cx-hx)-ox, x1 = (cx+hx)-ox, z the same; 0 if x1 < 0, !(x0 <= ex),
# z1 < 0, !(z0 <= ez), !(x1 <= ex), x0 < 0, !(z1 <= ez), z0 < 0. Cells cvt.w(x0 s), cvt.w(x1 s)+1, z the same; the
# first level whose side exceeds both width and depth (or the deepest), corners masked to the side, one level up
# (twice the side) if they span more than one node and level != 0; corners 0x10, 0x20 z, 0x40 x, 0x80 both; x0/z0
# >= 0x8000 -> 0x7fff. Key = level | corners | z0 << 5 | x0 << 17 (unpacked: key & 0xf, (key & 0xfff00000) >> 17,
# (key & 0xfff00) >> 5). Walk: child 2 (x & bit != 0) + (z & bit != 0), bit 0x4000 down, levels 0..=level.
# Widen (lo = cy-hy, hi = cy+hy; nothing unless lo <= hi): a = min(lo, mid-half), b = hi if !(hi <= mid+half),
# mid = (a+b)/2, half = (b-a)/2.
# In (vt +0x24): key 0 nothing; close an open query; stamp++; the entry's first halfword = 0; per corner the root
# and each node down widened, children grown and nodes made on the way; filed in the first empty slot, else a
# pool link zeroed and chained last. Move (vt +0x28): key 0 or !(lo <= hi) nothing; per corner widen the root and
# each node down, stopping at one without children. Out (vt +0x2c): key 0 nothing; close an open query; stamp--;
# per corner walk down (skip the corner at a missing node), unfile; a node left with no chunks and no children
# is freed and its parent's child cleared. Unfile: each chunk in turn, every slot holding the entry cleared, the
# chunk freed if left empty, stop after the one that held it. Close: pool reset, walk words 0, cells 0x80000000,
# flags &= ~1. Pool reset: chunks prepended to those set aside, chunk chain 0, used = capacity, free chain 0.
# Callers: the overlaps skip a newly reached cell whose pick is 0 (no in, no link), the carried kind still taken;
# a walk to a new cell puts unconditionally (in returns on key 0).
atv_quadtree_pick           atv 0x00180060 = the quadtree's vt +0x20 (the key of a box, 0 if not wholly inside)
atv_quadtree_move           atv 0x00180620 = the quadtree's vt +0x28 (the nodes down a key widened to a box's height)
atv_quadtree_out            atv 0x001807f0 = the quadtree's vt +0x2c (an entry taken out from under a key)
atv_quadtree_file           atv 0x00180b60 = an entry into a node's first empty slot, else a new chunk
atv_quadtree_unfile         atv 0x00180c80 = an entry out of a node's chunks; whether the node still holds anything
atv_quadtree_node_new       atv 0x001809f0 = a quadtree node constructed (the current tree's count +1)
atv_quadtree_node_free      atv 0x00180a30 = a quadtree node's children and chunks freed (the current tree's count -1)
atv_quadtree_node_grow      atv 0x00182670 = a quadtree node reallocated with its four children after it
atv_quadtree_make_node      atv 0x001aea20 = the quadtree's vt +0x4c (a new node)
atv_quadtree_close          atv 0x00181d90 = the quadtree's vt +0x40 (the open query closed)
atv_pool_reset              atv 0x00106130 = every link of a pool handed back (chunks set aside)
atv_heap_new                atv 0x001b7620 = allocate with a file and line (a0 bytes)
atv_heap_free               atv 0x001b76d0 = free

# MD81a — MX vs. ATV Untamed's (SLUS-21701) bike step against SLUS-21104's. Static, over the folded ELF

# Comment rows only (SLUS-21701 addresses are not names here). Twins, SLUS-21104 -> SLUS-21701:

#   0x00542db0 -> 0x00545978; the 16-byte compare at 0x00396fc0 -> 0x003b5f60; the clock's event post

# MD93 — Untamed's (SLUS-21701) reworked preload, wreck entry and put-back, ported as handling::untamed
# (preload, wreck_entry, put_back) under Handling::Untamed and proved by tests/untamed_reworked.rs off
# context/untamed/ram/nat_or.bin. Comment rows only, as # MD81a. Journal: research/journal/2026-11-03-md93-untamed-bike-step.

#   velocity body +0x80 (body +0xe8), cue latch +0x724, event tag +0xd10, receiver bike+0x698. New: +0x12c8 = 1
#   when action 0x53 is bound (0x00117c10 != -1), +0x12b4 its analogue axis (pad word 0xe5). Bound, not firing on
#   entry, on the lip and axis > 0.001 (0x3a83126f): firing 1, force.y = map_clamp(axis, 0, 1, 0, MaxPreload),
#   timer = PreloadTime, armed 0; then the stick's path as SLUS-21104's, only when not firing on entry (so after
#   an axis fire, in the same call). Cue: latch 0 and firing -> latch 1, post (8, 14, +0xd10, 0); else timer <= 0

#   then the listener count, halfword body +0xe8). Body vtable 0x003ef6a0.
#   wreck entry 0x004fa390 (vt +0x368): vt +0x4a0 (0x004fce80, the stunts ended), vt +0x4a8 (0x004fd000, -1:
#   the clip stopped); reason 13: rand 0x00344360 three times x 2^-31 (0x30000000), one quadword (u1, u2, u3, 0)
#   to body +0x1d0 (SLUS-21104 stores each lane and keeps w); vt +0x398 (0x004f98b0, 0), vt +0x484; +0xc44 = 0,
#   +0xc40 = 0, +0xdd8 = 0, +0x1714 = 1; base entry 0x004cbc60 (bike, reason: +0xc70/+0xc74/+0xc7c, rumble and a
#   new call 0x001c3d70 for a player, post (8, 0xb)); +0x17c4 = reason != 0; +0x708 += 1.
#   put back 0x004ca900 (a0 vehicle, a1 snap, a2 keep): +0x80 = +0xd30; driver +0x6a4 set and +0xd60, +0xd64 both
#   non-zero -> driver +0x94/+0x98/+0x9c = +0xd60/+0xd64/+0xd68; snap and ground +0x3e4: ground query 0x004b0910
#   (ground, bike+0x80 in/out, normal out, 0, 0), +0x80.xyz += normal.xyz x 0.3 (0x3e99999a, vmulx/vadd.xyz),
#   +0x90 = +0xd40, +0xa0 = the normal; else +0x90 = +0xd40, +0xa0 = +0xd50. Then 0x004c8880 (settle: 0x004b9130,
#   +0x34c = 1); keep: +0xdd8 = +0xddc, vt +0x3a0; else +0xdd8 = 0, vt +0x39c. +0x10cc set: 0x00512e90 (a0 it).
#   Post (8, 0x15, +0xd10, 0). SLUS-21104's 0x004a6f70 drops the normal and always restores +0x60/+0x70.

#   and one stick (+0x12ac, hold +0x12b0). SLUS-21701 reads a pad record at +0x14f4 (filled by 0x003a25b0 from

#   non-zero -> fires with hold 0.8 (0x3f4ccccd, abs), active 1, grace 0x3f19999a; armed untouched.
#   The torque map, the equal-times delay-slot answer, the grace, the end and the flipping/authority tail as SLUS-21104's.

#   is set: a bound 360 turns the way the last one did (from a direction of 0 it has no yaw, and ends once armed
#   unless the min yaw is 0 or less).

#   middle and 0. Timer: c.ole timer, 0 / bc1t skips (an unordered timer runs; the decomp's 0 < timer does not).

# MD93d — Untamed's (SLUS-21701) bike air state (PostProcess) 0x004f1270 (1524 B; f12 dt, a0 bike) against
# SLUS-21104's 0x004d9290 (1544 B), and the no-hander 0x004f3c70 (1332 B) against 0x004dbd10 (1328 B).
# The air state is ported as handling::untamed::air_state (calls as untamed::AirCall) and proved by
# tests/untamed_reworked.rs off context/untamed/ram/nat_or.bin. Comment rows only, as # MD81a. Journal:
# research/journal/2026-11-03-md93d-untamed-bike-step. Read the same way as # MD93c. Diffed with
# research/md81a/sdiff.py, with the frame's addiu $sp masked, the MEM regex taking any base register, and the
# bike register ($s0 in SLUS-21104, $s1 in SLUS-21701) renamed to one name.
#   No-hander: scheduling only. $f6 <-> $f5 swapped, and one mul/adda/madd len2 block reordered to the same sum.

#   quadword 0x00396fc0 -> 0x003b5f60. Callees 0x001351d0 -> 0x00132f40 (quat axes), 0x00129aa0 -> 0x00127690,
#   0x001340b0 -> 0x00131aa0, 0x00135620 -> 0x00132fd0. No port.
#   Air state frame 144 -> 160 B; $s0 holds body+0x190. Callees (SLUS-21104 -> SLUS-21701):
#   balance cleared 0x004e65b0 -> 0x004fe950 (zeroes +0x14f8/150c/1510/1518 -> +0x1518/152c/1530/1538; same code);
#   no-hander 0x004dbd10 -> 0x004f3c70; quat axes 0x001351d0 -> 0x00132f40 (same; xyz of +0xbd0, +0xbe0 only);
#   quat to euler 0x001358d0 -> 0x00133280 (888 -> 788 B: out-of-line b stubs become bc1fl/bc1tl with mov.s in
#   the slot, same clamps; writes w); in-world 0x00494200 -> 0x004ba650 (same; its +0x45 -> +0x75, +0xe8 ->
#   +0x118; a1 the origin); event post 0x00272300 -> 0x0025e740 (a0 *0x00442eb0 -> *0x00475bd0); vehicle post
#   0x004a4f00 -> 0x004c8b20; node position 0x00194c20 -> 0x00190830 (same, node fields +0x30).
#   Fields: vehicle state +0xbb4 (+0x28 kind, 3/4 return), reason +0xc70, stunt id +0x1544, balance id +0x154c,
#   speed +0x35c, local vel +0x380, fwd/up +0xbd0/+0xbe0, euler +0x390, world ang vel +0x370, on ground +0x354,
#   both +0x1260, airborne +0xdd0 (last +0xdd4), hang time +0x1180, contact vel +0xc20 (wheel +0xdc touching,
#   +0x190 vel), flipping +0x1188, spin ramp +0x11c4, authority +0x1178/time +0x117c, crossup +0x360 (speed
#   c.ole 10.0 skips; pi/2 0x3fc90fdb), record +0xf8's +0x24 = up.y c.ole 0.25, tag +0xd10, node +0x114 ->
#   +0xbc0, kept +0xc30. Body +0x70 pos, +0x80 vel, +0x190 quat, +0x1a0 cofg, +0x1d0 ang vel.
#   THE CHANGE, in-world test 0: SLUS-21104 zeroes +0xc80, +0x868, +0xc1c, posts (8, 13, tag +0xd20, 0; a1
#   bike+0x674) and calls vt+0x3a8 = 0x004d8940 (bike, 1, 1) (put-back, snap 1). SLUS-21701 posts (8, 13, tag
#   +0xd10, 0; a1 bike+0x698) and calls vt+0x36c = 0x004fa4d0 (bike, 1) (unwreck). That unwreck: vt+0x4a0;
#   quadword 0x003b5f60 -> +0x1240 and body+0xc0; vt+0x4a8(-1); +0x1544/+0x1548 = 255, +0x13f4 = 1; zero +0x1690,
#   +0x118c, +0x1190, +0x1184; vt+0x488; vt+0x3a8(bike, 0, a1) (put-back, no snap); 0x004c34d0(+0x143c); driver
#   +0x6a4's +8 |= 0x40000; +0x17c4 = 0; the +0x17b8 record; base unwreck 0x004cbda0(bike, 1).

# MD93f — what Untamed's (SLUS-21701) backflip and 360 read from the pad, traced to the bindings on
# context/untamed/ram/nat_or.bin. Comment rows only. Journal: research/journal/2026-11-03-md93f-untamed-bike-step.
#   Corrections to # MD93a: the pad record's word +0 is not the device. 0x003a25b0 stores 3 there (sw 3, 0(rec))
#   every call, so it is the binding style, always 3 (the classic one). The trick bytes are not debounced: the
#   "== 3" test before each is always true, and each byte is just (entry value > 0.0).
#   Binding lookup 0x00117c10 (controls = *(*0x003ff6b8), action, 2, 10, device): map = controls[device], then
#   0x00127c00 (map, action) = *(map + 0xc + action*4), or -1 when action > 0x5a or there is no map. The result
#   is a control index into the pad record's 0x18-byte entries (value at +4); an analogue action reads entry
#   idx + 0x20. The reader's lookups for 0x51..0x55 pass device 0 (move a4, zero).
#   Control order (device +0x818: flags, +0x81c pressure index, +0x820 mask, 16-byte stride) = MX Unleashed's
#   input::Control: 0 Triangle, 1 Circle, 2 Cross, 3 Square, 4 L1, 5 R1, 6 L2, 7 R2, 8 Select, 9 Start, 10 R3,
#   11 L3, 12 Up, 13 Right, 14 Down, 15 Left.
#   Defaults on the image (device 0 map 0x792490 and device 1 map 0x792dc0, the same): 0x11 Circle, 0x12 Triangle,
#   0x13 Select, 0x14 R1; 0xe Cross (gas), 0xf Square (brake), 0x10 L1 (clutch), 0x4 Start, 0x21 L3; analogue
#   0x51 -> 1, 0x52 -> 0, 0x54 -> 0, 0x55 -> 1 (entries 0x21/0x20/0x20/0x21); 0x53 unbound. So the backflip's arming
#   button (0x14) is R1 = MXU preset 0's TRICK_3, 0x11/0x12 are TRICK_1/2, and 0x13 is Select, which has no trick
#   command in MXU. The flip hold +0x12c0 (0x55, entry 0x21) and the spin hold +0x12b8 (0x54, entry 0x20) are the
#   entries SLUS-21104's reader 0x004e36c0 reads for its +0x12b0 (entries + 0x31c) and +0x12b4 (entries + 0x304).
#   Stunt table *(bike +0x1510) = driver (+0x724) + 0x2a0 (SLUS-21104: +0x14f0 = driver +0x6a4 + 0x2a0), built by
#   0x00251e60 from the stunts file (init 0x00251d30). +0 = 1; +8 DepreciationPct; +0x10 NumStuntsToRestoreOne;
#   +0x14 NumStuntsToMaxDepletion; +0x18/+0x1c/+0x20 the stunt indices named "backflip"/"threesixtyL"/
#   "threesixtyR" (49/52/53 on the image, -1 until found); bytes +0x24/+0x25/+0x26/+0x27 = the backflip's
#   DefaultKeyMappingStyle1/2/3/Classic is non-empty (+0x27 inits to 1). Then 0x36 stunts of 0x58 bytes from +0x28:
#   +0 the name, +8 the key-mapping combo the matcher 0x003a37d0 reads. Ini keys per stunt: Name, DisplayName,
#   HoldTime, SafeTime, Duration, PointValue, HoldPointValue, UsedForBike, HasAnimation, DefaultKeyMapping{Classic,
#   Style1, Style2, Style3}. 0x00252630 (table, style) = byte +0x24 + style, so with style 3 it reads +0x27;
#   0x00252640 is 0 when +0x18 is -1, else the matcher on table + 0x30 + 0x58*index; 0x00252690 the same on +0x1c.
#   On the image every bike's +0x24..+0x27 are 0 (bike 0x1b16440, table 0x8ecd80) and stunt 49's combo is empty:
#   the shipped game never binds the backflip, and the stick path always runs.
#   The lip's stick. The reader 0x004fb7d0 copies +0x1298 <- word 0xdf, +0x129c <- 0xe0, +0x12bc <- 0xe3,
#   +0x12c4 <- 0xe4, +0x12b4 <- 0xe5; the d-pad (actions 0..3) writes ±1 into both pairs alike. So inside the bike
#   the two sticks differ only in the pad words they come from. Who writes words 0xdf..0xe5 was not found: the
#   pad device's poll (vt +0x5c = 0x00150780; axes at +0x714 + 0x20i, deadzone remap 0x00116e90, axis bytes
#   (6, 7, 4, 5, 0), axis 2 inverted) does not lay out a record like this one.

# MD93g — what Untamed's (SLUS-21701) 360 reads from the pad and which stunt it starts, on
# context/untamed/ram/nat_or.bin. Comment rows only. Journal: research/journal/2026-11-03-md93g-untamed-bike-step.
#   Bound (0x00252630, byte table+0x24+style) is the backflip's byte for the 360 too: the stunts loader
#   0x00251e60 sets bytes +0x24..+0x27 (Style1/2/3, Classic non-empty) only on the row whose name is
#   "backflip"; for "threesixtyL"/"threesixtyR" it keeps only the index (+0x1c/+0x20). So the 360 takes the
#   bound path exactly when the backflip does. On the image all four bytes are 0: never.
#   Bound left/right 0x00252690/0x002526e0 (table, record): 0 when the index is -1, else the matcher
#   0x003a37d0 (combo table+0x30+0x58*idx, record). Matcher by record word +0 (the style): style 3 (classic)
#   matches when record word +4 == combo +0, and bytes +0x12/+0x14/+0x18 == combo bytes +0xe/+0xf/+0x11
#   (Circle, Triangle, R1: actions 0x11, 0x12, 0x14; Select +0x16 is not compared); on a match it prints
#   (0x00121d40). Classic combo parse 0x003a3220: 'C' -> +0xe, 'T' -> +0xf, '1' -> +0x11, one of "HUDLR"
#   (0x003d45f8) -> +0 its index. Record word +4 is the stick's direction, set by 0x003a25b0 off +0x1298/
#   +0x129c: |y| < |x| and |x| > 0.5 -> 4 (x > 0) / 3; else |y| > 0.5 -> 2 (y > 0) / 1; else 0. Style 1 only:
#   Circle and Triangle both held become R1 alone (else R1 is cleared).
#   On the image stunts 49/52/53 ("backflip"/"threesixtyL"/"threesixtyR") have all-zero combos: called, both
#   would answer pressed on a centred stick with no button held.
#   Stunt words: bike +0x13c8/+0x13cc/+0x13d0 = 49/52/53. The 360 starts +0x13cc (threesixtyL) for a direction
#   under 1 and +0x13d0 (threesixtyR) for 1, through vt +0x4b0 = 0x004fe710 (bike, stunt, 1). It runs when
#   the bike is airborne (+0x354 == 0) and unwrecked (+0xc70 == 0), or the stunt is one of +0x13c8..+0x13d8.
#   Then +0x1548 = stunt for any other stunt, for +0x13c8/cc/d0 only when +0x1548 > 48, for +0x13d4/d8 never.
#   So while the 360's clip runs, the rider's id is 52/53 unless a trick (<= 48) holds it.

# F31 — SLUS-21104's own bike frame replays C15's ride and flip bit-exact in the interpreter, off state 4's RAM
# image (context/mxvatv-ps2/state04.ee.bin), proved by crates/game-sim/tests/mxvatv_bike_ride.rs. Comment rows
# only. Journal: research/journal/2026-11-03-f31-mxvatv-bike-replay.
#   The frame: sim object 0x006659e0 (vtable 0x003b9320), vt +0x40 = 0x00204040 (a0 sim, f12 dt): the +0x24
#   list's vt +0x84(dt) (or vt +0x7c), the physics world (sim +0x6c = 0x00665a4c; 1 iteration at 115/s, its
#   body list [bike +0xb8]) through 0x00496b70, 0x00128af0, then the +0x3c list's vt +8. dt bits 0x3d08850a, the
#   same 30 Hz tick as MX Unleashed's (1/30 leaves ulp-level misses; found by search over the body block).
#   Inputs: the pad reader 0x004e36c0 (vt thunk 0x004bfa80, this - 0x43c) runs outside this frame and stores
#   +0x14ec (trick byte: R1 4, left 0x10, right 0x20, stick up 0x40, down 0x80), +0x12a0..+0x12b4 (d-pad, stick,
#   +0x12b0 the flip hold the backflip reads), +0x310/+0x314 brake/gas in, +0x12c0/+0x12c4, +0x14c8, +0x14d4
#   (the put-back request) and +0x17ac. While wrecked it holds the gas and the trick byte at 0. Tape tick t is on
#   row t + 1; the wreck state it sees is row t's.
#   Also outside: the put-back spot +0xd38..+0xd6c (row r's goes into frame r + 1; the put-back moves the bike
#   there), +0x44 bit 0x400, and obj words the frame never reads (+0x6f8/+0x6fc, +0x790.., +0x870..+0x898,
#   +0x908/+0x14e4, +0x930..+0x978, +0x940, +0xd90..+0xd98, +0x12b8/+0x12bc).
#   Wreck state: the collision record +0xcc is +0x1434 (wreck part) or +0x1438 (riding). The flip wrecks on row
#   125 and is put back on row 193; the ride never wrecks.
#   Hooks: kernel DIntr 0x00348fa0 / EIntr 0x00348ff0 (mfc0); syscall stubs (addiu v1, zero, n; syscall; jr ra)
#   in 0x00300000..0x00360000 answer a0, GetThreadId (0x2f) 1 (WaitSema 0x0033ef20 from the heap 0x001b68d0);
#   bike vt +0x544 = 0x004e7e70, the network snapshot send (reads the T0 counter 0x10000000, packs, sends through
#   *(bike +0x400) vt +0x28; writes nothing of the bike's).
